  We present QDAG, a production system at LinkedIn that represents an analytics methodology as a declarative directed acyclic graph of typed steps. Nodes may execute Apache Pinot queries, downstream service calls, in-memory SQLite joins, jq transformations, conditionals, differentially-private aggregations, or calls to other QDAGs. The engine evaluates graphs demand-driven, memoized, pruned, and parallelized in the per-request analytics mid-tier. QDAG is deployed across more than 500 hosts and over 100 production use cases, adding roughly 10 ms median orchestration overhead and under 50 ms at the 99th percentile. Our experience shows that making methodologies declarative improves reuse, testability, and cross-product consistency while preserving interactive latency.

</details>


### [115] [Data Flow Control: Data Safety Policies for AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05679)
*Charlie Summers,Eugene Wu*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出了数据流控制（DFC）框架及其查询重写工具Passant，通过将策略定义植入DBMS查询层，在极低开销下实现了对元组级数据流的安全合规强制执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着智能体（Agents）生成SQL及自动化分析的普及，语义正确并不等同于合规安全。当前的查询生成机制缺乏对监管、隐私及业务约束（如数据组合与发布限制）的强制执行手段，亟需将安全合规从应用层下沉至数据库内部。

Method: 提出一种名为“数据流控制”（DFC）的框架，通过在DBMS查询中定义针对元组级数据流的声明式策略；采用一种形式化的方法，即基于溯源单项式（provenance monomials）的聚合谓词来定义数据安全；实现了名为Passant的便携式查询重写层，无需物化溯源信息即可执行策略。

Result: 在五个主流数据库引擎（DuckDB, Umbra, PostgreSQL, DataFusion, SQLServer）中验证，Passant实现了接近0%的性能开销，在大规模数据处理中性能远超现有替代方案。

Conclusion: DFC是一套将数据安全保障从提示词（prompt）和事后检查（post-hoc）转移到底层数据基础设施的框架，能够实现安全策略的声明式定义和高效执行。

Abstract: Agents increasingly generate SQL, orchestrate pipelines, and automate data analysis on behalf of users. While recent work improves query correctness, correctness is not safety. A query may be semantically valid yet violate regulatory, privacy, or business constraints that govern how data may be combined and released. We argue that enforcing such constraints is fundamentally a data infrastructure problem.
  This paper introduces Data Flow Control (DFC), a framework to declaratively specify and guarantee policy enforcement over tuple-level data flows within a DBMS query. A key challenge is defining a policy language that is optimizer-invariant yet efficient to enforce at scale. We formalize data safety as aggregate predicates over provenance monomials and present Passant, a portable query rewriting layer that enforces DFC policies without materializing provenance. Across five DBMS engines -- DuckDB, Umbra, PostgreSQL, DataFusion, and SQLServer -- Passant achieves ~0% overhead and outperforms alternatives by orders of magnitude. As a result, Data Flow Control is the first step towards moving data safety from prompts and post-hoc checks into the data infrastructure. Data Flow Control is available open source at https://github.com/dataflowcontrol/data-flow-control.

</details>


### [116] [Causal Scaffolding for Physical Reasoning: A Benchmark for Causally-Informed Physical World Understanding in VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05966)
*Tianyi Tang,Zhuoyi Lin,Zeyu Feng,Tianyi Ma,Yew-Soon Ong,Ivor Tsang,Haiyan Yin*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 针对VLM在物理因果推理中的缺陷，该文提出了CausalPhys基准和评估指标，并开发了CRFT方法以实现因果驱动的模型对齐，从而显著提升了模型的推理准确性与可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前最先进的视觉语言模型（VLM）尽管能给出合理的回答，但在物理世界的因果推理任务上表现不佳，缺乏深层的因果理解，导致推理过程常常不可靠。

Method: 1. 构建CausalPhys基准：包含3000+个涵盖感知、预期、干预和目标导向的视听问题，并配备专家标注的因果图。2. 因果评估指标：制定基于因果图的度量方法，评估模型的思维链（Chain-of-Thought）是否符合真实的因果依赖。3. CRFT微调：提出因果理性驱动微调框架，将模型推理与因果结构显示对齐。

Result: 分析显示现有VLM在捕捉物理因果依赖方面存在系统性缺陷。实验证明，CRFT方案能显著提高各类模型backbone在推理准确率和可解释性方面的表现。

Conclusion: CausalPhys通过构建基准、提出因果评估指标以及CRFT微调框架，成功弥补了当前视觉语言模型（VLM）在物理因果推理方面的不足，为提升模型的物理因果理解能力提供了有效路径。

Abstract: Understanding and reasoning about the physical world is the foundation of intelligent behavior, yet state-of-the-art vision-language models (VLMs) still fail at causal physical reasoning, often producing plausible but incorrect answers. To address this gap, we introduce CausalPhys, a benchmark of over 3,000 carefully curated video- and image-based questions spanning four domains: Perception, Anticipation, Intervention, and Goal Orientation. Each question is paired with an expert-annotated causal graph capturing object-attribute-event dependencies, enabling interpretable and fine-grained evaluation of causal understanding. Building on this, we formulate a causal-graph-grounded metric that quantitatively measures how well a model's chain-of-thought reasoning aligns with the correct causal relations, moving beyond answer-only accuracy and enabling systematic diagnosis of VLMs' causal reasoning failures. Using this metric, we conduct a comprehensive analysis of leading VLMs, revealing systematic gaps in capturing causal dependencies and underscoring the need for causality-aware learning. To address these limitations, we further propose Causal Rationale-informed Fine-Tuning (CRFT), which explicitly aligns VLM reasoning with causal structures. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CRFT substantially enhances both reasoning accuracy and interpretability across multiple model backbones. By unifying dataset curation, causal evaluation, and causality-informed learning, CausalPhys establishes a strong foundation for advancing modern VLMs toward causally grounded physical reasoning.

</details>


### [117] [Validation of graph databases against PG-Schema](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06127)
*Jacek Ciszewski,Jakub Kłos,Maxime Jakubowski,Dominik Tomaszuk,Filip Murlak*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文分析了PG-Schema图数据库验证问题的复杂度，指出其组合复杂度为NP完全，数据复杂度为PTIME。通过限制特定结构，其组合复杂度可优化至PTIME。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在研究针对无完整性约束的PG-Schema图类型验证图数据库实例的计算复杂度问题。

Method: 对PG-Schema验证问题的组合复杂度与数据复杂度进行了理论分析，并探索了通过限制类型组合与并集交替来优化复杂度的方法。

Result: 该验证问题在组合复杂度上是NP完全的，在数据复杂度上是PTIME（多项式时间）的。

Conclusion: 在对类型组合与并集之间的交替进行适当限制的情况下，PG-Schema的图数据库实例验证问题的组合复杂度可降低至多项式时间（PTIME）。

Abstract: The problem of validating a given graph database instance against a given PG-Schema graph type without integrity constraints is NP- complete in terms of combined complexity and in PTIME in terms of data complexity. The combined complexity drops to PTIME when the alternation between type combinations and unions is suitably restricted

</details>


### [118] [TOKI: A Bitemporal Operator Algebra for Contradiction Resolution in LLM-Agent Persistent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06240)
*Ziming Wang*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 针对 LLM 智能体持久化内存更新中的逻辑冲突与异常问题，本文提出了 TOKI 框架，通过引入双时态算子和写时正确性契约，明确了启发式矛盾解决机制的数学边界与隔离要求，填补了现有生产系统在内存一致性保障方面的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体在处理持久化记忆时属于重写入（write-heavy）负载，现有的信念更新策略（如最后写入胜出等）缺乏对隔离级别和写时异常的明确定义，导致系统在处理矛盾信息时存在逻辑缺陷。

Method: 1. 将矛盾解决建模为写时并发控制（write-time concurrency control）；2. 使用双时态算子（bitemporal operators）对四种启发式策略进行类型化；3. 提出四条健全性定理（soundness theorems）连接隔离级别、模式与溯源；4. 引入基于审核行（audit row）的防御机制。

Result: 1. 证明了在关系调度模型下， keyed logging 是保持重放一致性的必要条件；2. 经测试，在 LoCoMo 基准测试中，TOKI 的审计行防御机制将性能指标提高了 0.86，消减内存层导致准确率下降 0.49；3. 验证了 TOKI 是唯一能同时规避三种写时异常（重放不一致、信念漂移、审计擦除）且保留语言模型作为决策者的系统。

Conclusion: TOKI 框架成功定义了 LLM 智能体持久化内存中的“写入时正确性契约”。通过将矛盾解决建模为双时态算子，TOKI 在保持 LLM 判断逻辑的同时，解决了现有系统普遍存在的重放不一致、信念漂移偏差和审计丢失等异常现象，为 LLM 智能体内存管理提供了理论规范。

Abstract: Persistent memory for an LLM agent is a write-heavy substrate: every belief update is a versioned write, and a new claim may contradict a stored one. Production systems use four resolution heuristics (last-writer-wins, evidence-weighted merge, await-confirmation, per-rule policy), yet none declares the isolation level it assumes or the write-time anomalies it admits. We show that contradiction resolution is write-time concurrency control and make the missing contract explicit. TOKI types the four heuristics as one family of bitemporal operators over a dual-row schema, each with an isolation precondition and a provenance annotation that preserves the losing fact in an audit row. Four soundness theorems close the contract across isolation, schema, and provenance, lift the guarantees to operator pipelines, and extend the fold operators to n-ary conflict sets. A tightness companion proves that, within the relational schedule model, keyed logging of the adjudicating judge is necessary for replay consistency, which every audited baseline omits. A verdict matrix over eight systems localizes the gap: every baseline that keeps a language-model judge on the write path admits at least one of three write-time anomalies (replay inconsistency, belief-drift skew, audit erasure); a content-addressed engine-layer comparator avoids them only by removing the judge, and TOKI alone excludes all three while keeping it. On its one natural-workload slice the audit-row defence moves LoCoMo by 0.86, and ablating the typed memory layer removes 0.49 accuracy on 1,444 answerable LoCoMo questions; the cross-system comparison stays underpowered and claims no superiority. The contribution is the contract: a write-time correctness specification, proved sound across isolation, schema, and provenance, pinning the guarantee every production heuristic assumes but no deployed system makes explicit.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [119] [BIDENT: Heterogeneous Operator-level Mapping for Efficient Edge Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05271)
*Hoseok Kim,Arghadip Das,Soumendu Ghosh,Arnab Raha,Vijay Raghunathan*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: BIDENT是一个针对边缘异构SoC的统一算子级协同编排框架，通过将算子映射到最优处理单元并支持细粒度并行调度，大幅提升了推理性能并降低了能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代边缘SoC集成多种异构处理器，但现有推理架构通常将整个模型映射到单一PU，导致严重的计算资源浪费；且新兴模型（如SSM、KAN、VLA）的算子多样性难以通过单一处理器高效处理。

Method: 提出BIDENT框架，将算子到PU（处理单元）的分配建模为加权执行图上的最短路径问题；支持算子级映射、模型内并行和多模型并发调度。

Result: 在Intel Core Ultra SoC上测试，BIDENT通过模型内并行实现最高1.60倍加速，在多模型组合中实现了3.42倍的几何平均加速；并发场景下能量消耗平均降低了48.2%。

Conclusion: 算子级编排（Operator-level orchestration）是充分发掘下一代边缘AI异构计算能力的关键抽象。通过细粒度的调度，BIDENT能够显著提升执行效率和能效。

Abstract: Modern edge System-on-Chips (SoCs) integrate heterogeneous processing units (PUs) such as CPUs, GPUs, and NPUs, yet current inference stacks map entire models to a single PU, leaving significant performance and energy efficiency on the table. This is exacerbated by emerging architectures such as state-space models (SSMs), Kolmogorov-Arnold networks (KANs), and multi-stage vision-language-action (VLA) pipelines, whose diverse operator characteristics are not uniformly suited to any single PU. We present BIDENT, a unified operator-level orchestration framework for heterogeneous edge inference that maps individual operators to the most suitable PU based on profiled execution characteristics. BIDENT formulates operator-to-PU assignment as a shortest-path problem over a weighted execution graph, enabling efficient and optimal scheduling under the cost model for both latency- and energy-minimization objectives. Unlike prior work relying on model-specific heuristics or coarse-grained partitioning, BIDENT is model-agnostic and jointly supports sequential execution, intra-model parallelism across independent operators, and multi-model concurrent scheduling in a single formulation. We implement BIDENT on an Intel Core Ultra SoC and evaluate it across 10 model families spanning CNNs, Transformers, SSMs, KANs, spiking networks, and multi-stage pipelines. BIDENT achieves up to 1.60x speedup via intra-model parallelism and a 3.42x geometric mean speedup across 190 multi-model combinations by utilizing otherwise idle compute. Sequential heterogeneous mapping yields more modest gains (up to 1.58x, 1.09x geometric mean), while energy-aware scheduling reduces energy consumption by 48.2% on average in concurrent settings. These results show that operator-level orchestration, not model-level mapping, is the key abstraction for fully exploiting heterogeneity in next-generation edge AI.

</details>


### [120] [MOSAIC: A Workload-Driven Simulation and Design-Space Exploration Framework for Heterogeneous NPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05362)
*Arghadip Das,Hoseok Kim,Soomin Lee,Arnab Raha,Deepak A Mathaikutty,Vijay Raghunathan*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 为了解决新兴 AI 架构（非 MAC 密集型）在传统同构 NPU 上效率低下的问题，作者提出了 MOSAIC 框架，通过支持细粒度的多维异构 tile 设计与联合优化，实现了显著的能效提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当今 AI 模型架构日益多样化（如 SSM、FFT、KAN 等），传统的同构、以 MAC 为中心的 NPU 对非 MAC 操作支持较差，而现有异构设计工具缺乏细粒度的探索能力。

Method: 开发了 MOSAIC 分析模拟器和设计空间探索（DSE）框架，支持对 tile 类型（Big/Little/特殊函数单元）、数据流、稀疏模式、MAC 类型等多维度的异构配置进行建模，并结合分层扫描与遗传算法寻找 Pareto 最优解。

Result: MOSAIC 能够高效搜索联合异构设计空间，其生成的 HPU 设计在 7nm 工艺节点下，与同构架构相比，在保证面积等效的前提下大幅降低了能耗。

Conclusion: MOSAIC 框架通过精细化的异构 tile 设计，显著优于传统的同构 NPU 设计；在 20 个负载的测试中，最优通用 HPU 实现了 46.91% 的平均等面积能效提升。

Abstract: AI model architectures are diversifying rapidly. Although dense matrix multiplication underlies today's CNNs and transformers, emerging architectures (state-space models, long convolutions via the fast Fourier transform (FFT), Kolmogorov-Arnold networks, and spiking networks) are not multiply-accumulate (MAC) dominated; they spend much of their computation on vector and non-MAC primitives that homogeneous, MAC-centric neural processing units (NPUs) serve poorly. This has motivated heterogeneous NPUs (HPUs) built from non-identical tiles. Prior heterogeneous designs vary only one or two coarse knobs (typically MAC precision or array size) and are evaluated on narrow workloads; no existing framework supports fine-grained HPU design, where tiles differ across many architectural dimensions at once. We present MOSAIC, an analytical simulator and design-space-exploration (DSE) framework for HPU microarchitecture design. MOSAIC searches the joint space of tile-level heterogeneity: beyond array size and precision, it varies tile-type composition (large Big, small Little, and non-MAC Special-Function tiles), dataflow, sparsity mode, MAC engine type, and special-function units for non-MAC operators (FFT, spiking-integrate, polynomial). Unlike prior simulators that model a single homogeneous tile type, MOSAIC models non-MAC tiles with their own energy, area, and timing models and maps operators across a mix of tiles with a heterogeneity-aware compiler. A multi-seed pipeline pairing a stratified sweep with genetic-algorithm refinement returns Pareto-optimal designs, with cost models calibrated to a 7 nm node and cross-validated against NVIDIA's Deep Learning Accelerator (NVDLA). Across a 20-workload suite, the best general-purpose HPU found by MOSAIC (~200 mm^2 Big+Little+Special-Function) achieves +46.91% mean iso-area energy savings over the best iso-area homogeneous baseline.

</details>


### [121] [FQA: A Full-Space Quantization-Driven Architecture for Hardware-Efficient Piecewise Approximation of Nonlinear Activation Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05627)
*Chenjun Hao,Feng Yan,Hongbing Pan,Yuxuan Wang*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种FQA架构用于非线性激活函数的高效硬件PPA实现，通过全空间量化优化、FWL解耦及TBW搜索加速，在显著降低功耗与面积的同时实现了最优近似精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的非线性激活函数分段多项式逼近（PPA）方案存在系数优化的局限性及资源开销较高的问题，缺乏对量化误差与截断误差的综合考虑。

Method: 提出了全空间量化驱动架构（FQA），综合考虑截断和量化误差；提出了FWL（分数位长）解耦策略；设计了TBW（目标引导二分窗口）加速搜索算法；开发了两种针对不同资源-性能权衡的硬件实现方案。

Result: 实验表明，FQA在达到最优MAE前提下显著减少了所需分段数；对于Sigmoid函数，硬件面积与功耗较现有最先进方案降低了50%以上。

Conclusion: FQA架构通过全空间探索和FWL解耦，实现了非线性激活函数分段多项式逼近的高效硬件部署，在保证最优MAE的同时，显著优化了资源开销。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose a full-space quantization-driven architecture (FQA) for the hardware-efficient piecewise polynomial approximations (PPAs) of nonlinear activation functions. FQA comprehensively considers both fractional-bit truncation error and quantization error that cause the deviation of the optimal approximation coefficients. Crucially, FQA can precisely determine and search the complete range of optimal coefficients. Based on the proposed FQA, we develop two distinct hardware implementation schemes to cater to different resource-performance trade-offs. Furthermore, we decouple all the fractional word lengths (FWLs) involved in the calculation process to enable the exploration of superior hardware architectures. To mitigate the increased software computation time caused by the expanded quantization space, we design an acceleration method named TBW (target-guided bisection window) to expedite the piecewise calculation and searching process. Experimental results demonstrate that, compared to existing architectures, FQA can significantly reduce the number of required segments while achieving the optimal Maximum Absolute Error (MAE). For the hardware design of the Sigmoid function, our approach achieves over 50% reduction in area and power consumption compared to the state-of-the-art PPA architecture. Finally, we present a complete design workflow for deploying PPA on configurable hardware, maximizing the utilization of existing hardware resources and minimizing MAE.

</details>


### [122] [Space-CIM: Enabling Compute-In-Memory Accelerators for Thermally-Constrained Space Platforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05741)
*Sohan Salahuddin Mugdho,Md. Shahedul Hasan,Cheng Wang*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本研究表明，在空间数据中心的散热制约下，采用存内计算（CIM）架构比传统GPU更能有效解决散热瓶颈，实现更高的太空算力输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 应对AI算力激增带来的地面能源危机，利用太空丰富的太阳能和低成本发射，探索部署轨道数据中心的可行性与挑战。

Method: 提出了“散热器闭环协同设计”（radiator-in-the-loop co-design）方法，建立了系统算力（TOPS）与空间辐射散热冷却能力之间的关联模型；对比分析了GPU（含HBM）与CIM架构在热分布及不同散热预算下的性能表现。

Result: 研究发现GPU由于die与HBM存在严重的局部热点，在空间散热限制下性能受限；而CIM架构表现出更均匀的热分布，在相同的散热预算下，其算力表现和单位功耗性能（TOPS/W）均优于GPU。

Conclusion: 在空间轨道数据中心的散热约束条件下，存内计算（CIM）架构相比传统GPU具有显著的性能优势和能效比优势，是空间计算基础设施的可行选择。

Abstract: The rapid growth in compute demand from artificial intelligence (AI) has driven a massive surge in data center construction, precipitating an energy and sustainability crisis. Motivated by the abundant solar energy in outer space and the recent sharp reduction in space launch costs, orbital data centers are emerging as a potential pathway for the future scaling of AI compute infrastructure. While the cold background in vacuum seems appealing for cooling, computing systems operating in space without convection ultimately rely on radiative cooling, requiring large-area radiators. Such limitations in thermal management pose a significant challenge for deploying the standard liquid/air-cooled computers in space. In this work, we investigate the impact of the thermal constraints in space on both graphics processing units (GPUs) with high-bandwidth memory (HBM) and the emerging compute-in-memory (CIM) accelerators. We develop a radiator-in-the-loop co-design methodology that directly links the permitted system TOPS (terra-operations per second) with the practical radiator cooling capacity in space. Our thermal simulations reveal that the separately located GPU die and HBMs create severe thermal hotspots under limited radiator capacity, necessitating GPU thermal throttling. In contrast, CIM accelerators exhibit a much more uniform heat distribution and consistently outperform GPUs in TOPS/W across a wide range of radiator budgets. We systematically evaluated the performance of CIM and GPU across various AI workloads and demonstrated that CIM has a magnified advantage for deployment in space under realistic thermal constraints.

</details>


### [123] [ITP-STDP: An Intrinsic-Timing Power-of-Two Learning Engine for On-Chip SNN Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06159)
*Haihang Xia,Xinyu Zhao,Xuecheng Wang,John Goodenough,Charith Abhayaratne,Panagiotis A. Panagiotou,Chunyi Song,Tiantai Deng*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文针对脉冲神经网络（SNN）训练中 STDP 算法计算开销大的问题，提出了一种 ITP-STDP 算法及其高效硬件实现，大幅降低了计算资源需求并提升了能效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 SNN 的突触权重更新计算会导致巨大的硬件资源消耗和能量开销，限制了其在片上学习中的应用，因此需要设计更高效的 STDP 实现方案。

Method: 提出了一种“固有定时 2 的幂次 STDP”（ITP-STDP）算法，并通过专门的学习引擎硬件架构实现；同时利用平均场突触漂移模型进行动力学分析，并在 FPGA 和 ASIC 平台上进行了验证与对比。

Result: 与现有最优方法相比，ITP-STDP 在 FPGA 上能量效率提升了 4.5 倍至 219.8 倍；在 ASIC 平台上实现了 4.8 倍至 22.01 倍的速度提升，且物理面积仅为对比方案的 1.2% 至 3.3%。

Conclusion: ITP-STDP 通过算法与硬件层面的协同优化，显著降低了 SNN 训练过程中的计算复杂度和资源消耗，证明了该方法在提升脉冲神经网络边缘计算效率方面的有效性。

Abstract: Spiking neural networks (SNNs) have the potential to emerge as the third generation of neural networks and have attracted increasing attention across a wide range of applications. However, the large number of synaptic connections in SNNs leads to intensive weight-update computation by on-chip learning algorithms during training, resulting in substantial hardware resource utilization and energy consumption. Among existing SNN learning algorithms, spike-timing-dependent plasticity (STDP) is one of the most extensively studied and widely adopted, serving as a fundamental learning component in SNNs. To address the hardware and energy overheads associated with SNN training, this paper presents intrinsic-timing power-of-two STDP (ITP-STDP) and its corresponding prototype learning engine hardware architecture. The proposed design is evaluated through a dedicated mean-field synaptic drift model for dynamical analysis and further validated across SNN networks of different scales and datasets. It is further implemented on both ASIC and FPGA platforms and compared with state-of-the-art approaches, including the original STDP and more complex STDP variants. The results demonstrate superior energy efficiency, higher operating speed, and substantially lower hardware resource utilization, as the proposed design eliminates most of the computational overhead of STDP through both algorithmic and hardware-level optimizations. On the FPGA platform, the proposed design improves energy efficiency by 4.5$\times$ to 219.8$\times$ over the compared designs. On the ASIC platform, the proposed design achieves a 4.8$\times$ to 22.01$\times$ speedup while consuming only 1.2% to 3.3% of the area required by prior works.

</details>


### [124] [Modeling, Optimizing and Exploring Multi-Die FPGA Routing Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06421)
*Amirhossein Poolad,Soheil Gholami Shahrouz,Andrew Boutros,Vaughn Betz*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文针对多芯片（2.5D/3D）FPGA缺乏路由架构设计工具的现状，扩展了开源VTR工具及其布线算法，并通过电路仿真验证了多芯片堆叠架构在提升容量的同时，能够有效控制性能损耗，为下一代FPGA架构设计提供了评估框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多芯片堆叠（2.5D/3D）FPGA虽然提升了容量与良率，但其特殊的物理特性限制了芯片间互连，且缺乏精确的建模工具来指导芯片间路由架构的设计。

Method: 1. 增强开源FPGA CAD工具VTR，使其能够灵活建模多芯片路由架构；2. 改进VPR的布局与布线引擎；3. 在7nm工艺下进行HSPICE电路仿真，建立跨芯片连接模型；4. 对不同芯片间连接技术、连接数量、扇入/扇出和转接板长度进行设计空间探索。

Result: 1. 3D FPGA相比2D设备可实现高达14%的线长缩短及6%的关键路径延迟（CPD）改善；2. 2.5D FPGA在保持32%芯片间连接度的情况下，仅需增加2%的线长和4%的CPD成本；3. 所有扩展已开源并集成到VTR主分支。

Conclusion: 通过适当的芯片间互连架构设计，2.5D和3D FPGA可以在不牺牲布线能力或性能的前提下有效提升容量。3D FPGA在性能和布线长度上优于2D FPGA，而2.5D FPGA在保持合理连接密度时仅有极小的性能开销。

Abstract: Die stacking has enabled 2.5D FPGAs by integrating multiple active dice on a passive silicon interposer for improved yield and capacity, and paved the way for 3D architectures that stack active dice directly atop one another. In these multi-die devices, the unique electrical and physical characteristics of the underlying die-stacking technology impose limitations on inter-die connection density and latency, necessitating a bespoke inter-die routing architecture. However, the absence of accurate and versatile modeling tools has left most questions about how to best design the inter-die routing architecture unanswered.
  To address this gap, we enhance the open-source FPGA CAD tool VTR to flexibly model a wide range of multi-die routing architectures, and augment VPR's placement and routing engines to improve optimization for both 2.5D and 3D FPGAs. We perform HSPICE-based circuit modeling of inter-die connections for active dice using a 7 nm process node and a 45 nm silicon interposer across several die-crossing technologies. Using this enhanced framework, we conduct a detailed design space exploration of inter-die routing architecture in 2.5D and 3D FPGAs, characterizing the impact of die-crossing technology, inter-die connection count, fan-in/fan-out, and interposer wire length on critical path delay (CPD), wirelength, area, and routability.
  Our results show that with suitable inter-die routing architectures, 2.5D and 3D FPGAs can increase capacity without significant routability or delay penalties. Specifically, 3D FPGAs achieve up to 14% wirelength reduction and 6% CPD improvement over 2D devices, and remain routable even with existing $10\,μ$m pitch technologies, while 2.5D FPGAs incur only a 2% wirelength and 4% CPD overhead at 32% inter-die connectivity. All extensions are open source and integrated with the VTR master branch.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [125] [Central Description Length (CDL) Clustering Validation Index](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05230)
*Mahdi Shamsi,Soosan Beheshti*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的聚类验证指标CDL-CVI，通过概率描述长度概念，无需核技术即可有效评估复杂形状数据的聚类质量，且在多项基准测试中表现优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的聚类验证指标（CVI）大多基于欧几里得距离，倾向于寻找紧凑、凸形的聚类，难以处理非凸、不规则或密度不均的数据，且通常需要额外的核变换或参数调整。

Method: 提出中央描述长度（Central Description Length, CDL）聚类验证指标。该方法通过结合簇内紧凑度、估计的簇中心及协方差，计算出关于不可观测真实簇中心的概率描述长度上界。仅依赖可观测数据量（数据点、分区、质心、协方差），无需地面真值。

Result: 在合成基准测试中，CDL-CVI在识别簇数量准确度和调整兰德指数（ARI）上优于传统CVI；在MNIST、CIFAR-10等图像基准测试中，能针对多种聚类算法稳定地估计出接近真实类别的簇数量。

Conclusion: CDL-CVI是一种在无标签情况下评估聚类质量的鲁棒性指标，能够有效处理非凸、不规则及变密度聚类，且无需复杂的核变换或预处理即可达到优于传统CVI的性能。

Abstract: Selecting a clustering algorithm and its hyperparameters without labels is a common difficulty in engineering machine learning pipelines that work with unsupervised analysis of sensor, image, or process data. Clustering validation indices (CVIs) provide internal scores for ranking candidate clusterings, but most popular CVIs are built from Euclidean compactness and separation terms and so tend to favour compact, convex partitions. Their performance is known to degrade on non convex, irregular, or variable density data, where kernel transformations or alternative distance measures are typically used at the cost of additional tuning and computation. This paper introduces the Central Description Length (CDL) clustering validation index. CDL uses the observed within cluster compactness, the estimated cluster centers, and the estimated cluster covariances to compute a probabilistic upper bound on the description length associated with the unobservable true cluster centers. The bound condenses intra cluster compactness and centroid displacement into a single computable quantity and is evaluated on the partition produced by any clustering algorithm. The implementation uses only observable quantities (the data, the partition, the estimated centers, and the estimated covariances) and does not use ground truth labels. On synthetic benchmarks with non convex and arbitrary shape clusters, CDL-CVI selected the reference number of clusters more often and reached higher Adjusted Rand Index (ARI) values than the conventional CVIs we tested, without an additional kernel preprocessing stage. On image benchmarks (MNIST, CIFAR-10, STL-10) clustered from frozen unsupervised embeddings, CDL-CVI returned cluster numbers close to the reference class counts across K-means, DBSCAN, and spectral clustering in the reported trials.

</details>


### [126] [HyFAD: Hybrid Time-Frequency Diffusion with Frequency-Aware Embedding for Time Series Imputation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05239)
*Hongfan Gao,Wangmeng Shen,Bin Yang,Jilin Hu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: HyFAD 是一种新型时频混合扩散模型，通过“先时域、后频域”的耦合去噪机制及频率感知引导，显著提升了时间序列补全中高频细节的恢复能力与全局趋势的拟合精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的时间序列扩散模型在处理频率敏感型去噪、高频细节重构以及平衡全局趋势与局部动态方面存在局限性，导致补全效果受限。

Method: 1. 构建耦合时频扩散模型：采用序列化的逆向去噪过程，先在时域生成全局趋势，再在频域细化局部高频特征。
2. 引入频率感知步长嵌入（Frequency-Aware Step Embedding）：建立扩散步数与频谱分量间的联系，为不同频率分量提供针对性的引导。
3. 粗细结合的重建策略：利用时频互补特性实现从全局到局部的协同生成。

Result: 在多个公开基准数据集上进行了广泛实验，结果表明该模型在时间序列补全任务上表现优于现有的主流方法，达到了当前最优（SOTA）水平。

Conclusion: HyFAD 通过结合时域（低频趋势）和频域（高频细节）的耦合扩散策略，配合频率感知的步长嵌入，有效解决了现有扩散模型在时间序列补全任务中频率敏感度不足及细节重构困难的问题，达到了领域内领先水平。

Abstract: Diffusion models have demonstrated strong performance in time series modeling due to their ability to progressively capture complex data distributions through iterative denoising. However, existing approaches struggle with frequency-sensitive denoising, high-frequency reconstruction and balancing global trends with local dynamics. To address these limitations, we propose \textbf{HyFAD}, a \textbf{Hy}brid time-frequency \textbf{D}iffusion model with \textbf{F}requency-\textbf{A}ware embedding for time series imputation. Built upon the DDPM paradigm, HyFAD adopts a coupled time-frequency diffusion framework, in which the reverse denoising proceeds sequentially from the time domain to the frequency domain, enabling coarse-to-fine generation. Specifically, the time-domain diffusion process captures low-frequency global trends, while the frequency-domain diffusion process refines high-frequency spectral components. We further introduce a frequency-aware step embedding that exploits the relationship between diffusion steps and spectral components, providing step-dependent spectral guidance and facilitates more accurate band-wise reconstruction. Extensive experiments on multiple benchmark datasets demonstrate that HyFAD achieves state-of-the-art performance. Our source code is available at https://github.com/hongfangao/HyFAD.

</details>


### [127] [Deterministic Envelopes for Tamed SGLD: Decoupling Stochastic-Gradient Noise and Localizing Taming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05242)
*Yiwei Zhou,Ziheng Chen*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该论文指出，传统随机梯度朗之万算法中的“驯化（taming）”分母往往因依赖随机噪声而引入稳态偏差。研究提出了一种结构保持框架，通过预先确定分母并使用混合型确定性包络，成功在保持算法稳定性的同时消除了因分母相关性导致的归一化偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机梯度朗之万算法（SGLD）在处理非全局Lipschitz漂移时常使用驯化技术，但现有的驯化方法在分母依赖于随机梯度实现时会产生不必要的稳态偏差，影响算法准确性。

Method: 1. 揭示了梯度依赖型分母引入稳态偏差的机理；2. 提出了一种结构保持（structure-preserving）驯化框架，即在采样噪声前固定分母；3. 引入局部确定性包络（deterministic envelopes）来平滑非全局Lipschitz漂移；4. 设计了结合软包络（典型区域）与硬尾控制（罕见区域）的混合型驯化器。

Result: 理论分析量化了由随机依赖分母引起的稳态偏差，并证明了新框架能有效降低该偏差。实验验证了随机分母的失真效应、确定性包络设计的偏差降低效果，以及混合型算法在稳定性和精确度上的优势。

Conclusion: 当分母与梯度依赖于相同的随机噪声时，传统的“驯化（taming）”技术会引入稳态偏差。本文提出的结构保持框架（通过固定分母并利用局部确定性包络）有效消除了这种偏差，且混合型包络（结合软包络与硬尾控制）在保持稳定性的同时实现了最优的收敛性能。

Abstract: Stochastic-gradient Langevin algorithms often use tamed denominators to stabilize non-globally Lipschitz drifts. This paper shows that when the denominator depends on the same stochastic-gradient realization as the numerator, the taming step changes the stochastic oracle itself and can create a stationary bias even if the original stochastic gradient is unbiased. We propose a structure-preserving framework for designing tamed denominators. It fixes the denominator before the oracle noise is sampled and uses localized deterministic envelopes to avoid unnecessary taming in typical regions. These kernels keep the stabilizing effect of taming while avoiding the bias introduced by a gradient-dependent denominator. Our theory explains how the stationary error splits into the bias caused by oracle-dependent taming and the remaining error introduced by deterministic stabilization. Within this deterministic-envelope family, the analysis identifies a far-tail condition that explains the limitation of local soft envelopes and motivates a hybrid member: soft in the typical region, but protected by hard-tail control on rare excursions. Experiments confirm the predicted stationary distortions of random denominators, the bias reduction of deterministic-envelope designs, and the stabilizing effect of the hybrid construction.

</details>


### [128] [Harnessing Source Heterogeneity for Cluster-Structured Transfer Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05258)
*Xiaohui Yin,Jun Jin,Shane J. Sacco,Robert H. Aseltine,Kun Chen*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 针对多源迁移学习中源数据存在异质性（集群结构）的问题，本文提出了Trans-GLMC算法，通过聚类、融合与去偏操作，实现了比传统方法更稳健、更准确的预测效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有迁移学习方法通常将辅助源简单二分为“有用”或“无用”，忽略了辅助源往往存在集群化的异质性。在自杀风险预测等实际应用中，简单合并数据会导致偏差，而单一模型又因数据稀缺而不稳定。

Method: 提出了Trans-GLMC（基于广义线性模型的集群结构迁移学习方法）。该过程分为三个步骤：1. 基于系数的距离度量进行潜在源聚类；2. 全局融合与组内精简；3. 目标域偏差校正（Debiasing）。

Result: 在仿真实验和CHIME自杀风险研究中，Trans-GLMC显著提升了针对特定设施的预测性能，成功识别出具有互通性的医院群体，并筛选出了具有临床意义的风险因子。理论上，其误差界限优于无集群假设的模型。

Conclusion: Trans-GLMC 能够有效发现辅助源中的潜在集群结构，在处理具有异质性的多源迁移学习问题时，相比无结构的方法具有更高的预测精度和统计效率。

Abstract: Transfer learning is a natural strategy when a target population has limited data but multiple related auxiliary sources are available. A central difficulty is source heterogeneity: auxiliary sources may not be equally useful, and their usefulness may vary in a structured, cluster-like fashion. Existing transfer-learning methods often reduce source selection to a binary informative/non-informative decision, overlooking subgroups of sources with differential transferability. Motivated by a suicide-risk study using data from the Connecticut Hospital Information Management Exchange (CHIME), comprising 636,758 patients across 27 hospitals, we propose Trans-GLMC, a cluster-structured transfer-learning procedure for generalized linear models. The CHIME setting illustrates the core challenge: hospital-specific risk models are unstable because suicide attempts are rare at any single facility, whereas indiscriminate pooling across hospitals can obscure facility-level differences in patient mix and risk profiles. Trans-GLMC first constructs a coefficient-based distance among the target and candidate sources to recover latent source clusters. It then combines global fusion, within-cluster refinement, and target debiasing to produce an estimator that adapts to the detected structure. We establish a non-asymptotic error bound that improves over its unclustered counterpart whenever a meaningful target cluster exists and matches the unclustered rate up to constants otherwise. In simulations and in the CHIME study, Trans-GLMC improves facility-specific prediction, identifies interpretable communities of hospitals with mutual transferability, and recovers clinically coherent suicide-risk factors.

</details>


### [129] [TabSODA: Tabular Diffusion based Imputation with Skip Pattern Detection and Ordinal Awareness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05361)
*Yuyu Chen,Taehyo Kim,Hai Shu,Yang Feng*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: TabSODA 是一种新型扩散模型，通过显式建模问卷的跳过逻辑及序数关系，解决了大规模调查数据缺失填补中结构误判和变量属性定义失效的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表格扩散模型在处理大型调查数据时存在局限：一是无法区分非适用性跳过（structural skips）与项目无回答缺失；二是简单将序数变量视为名义变量处理导致信息损失。

Method: 提出 TabSODA 模型，基于 Elucidated Diffusion Model (EDM) 框架，集成 EM 算法。具体创新包括：1) 将结构化跳过（Structural skips）纳入去噪损失和采样过程；2) 针对序数变量引入累积概率标量潜变量（cumulative-probit scalar latents）；3) 提出了基于 CART 的补全缺失掩码的 Skip Miner 工具（TabSODA+SKIP）。

Result: 在 PATH 和 NSDUH 数据集上，TabSODA 将序数 MACE 指标降低了多达 23.7%，分类准确率提升了 9%。TabSODA+SKIP 的自动化掩码生成能力表现出近乎完美的精度，能够达到与已知掩码方案相近的性能。

Conclusion: TabSODA 提出的结构化跳过检测和序数感知能力，有效提升了大规模调查问卷数据填补的准确性，在多种缺失机制（MCAR, MAR, MNAR）下均显著优于现有主流方法。

Abstract: Missing data imputation in large-scale surveys faces two challenges that are not well handled by current tabular diffusion methods. First, \emph{structural skips}, cells made inapplicable by questionnaire design, should not be imputed but are often conflated with item nonresponse. Second, \emph{ordinal} responses encode ordered categories, yet most pipelines treat them as nominal levels through one-hot or analog-bit encodings. We introduce \textbf{TabSODA} (\textbf{Tab}ular diffusion with \textbf{S}kip pattern detection and \textbf{O}r\textbf{d}inal \textbf{A}wareness), an Expectation-Maximization (EM)-based diffusion imputer built on the Elucidated Diffusion Model (EDM) framework. TabSODA propagates structural skips through the denoising loss and reverse-time sampler, and represents ordinal variables with cumulative-probit scalar latents while retaining analog-bit encodings for nominal variables. When a codebook skip mask is available, TabSODA uses it directly; otherwise, the TabSODA+SKIP variant estimates the mask from raw responses and questionnaire order using a CART-based skip-pattern miner. On Population Assessment of Tobacco and Health (PATH) study and the National Survey on Drug Use and Health (NSDUH), two nationally representative U.S.\ surveys, TabSODA reduces ordinal MACE by up to $23.7\%$ and improves categorical accuracy by up to $9\%$ over the strongest baseline across MCAR, MAR, and MNAR masking. The skip miner achieves near-perfect precision on both datasets, allowing TabSODA+SKIP to closely track the codebook-mask variant.

</details>


### [130] [Environment-Robust Representation Learning with Empirical Bayes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05365)
*Yuli Slavutsky,Matthew Shen,Bohan Wu,David M. Blei*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于潜变量的多环境预测模型，通过结合经验贝叶斯变分推断和跨环境平衡机制，显著提升了模型在未知环境下的预测性能与泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多环境下，尽管观测变量和目标变量的条件分布保持稳定，但潜变量的分布可能随环境变化。现有的方法难以解决这种环境偏移带来的预测泛化难题。

Method: 构建了一个贝叶斯模型，通过变分推断（variational objective）对潜在变量建模。目标函数包含环境内项（per-environment terms）和跨环境平衡项（cross-environment balancing term）。采用经验贝叶斯法（empirical Bayes）设置先验，并利用摊销变分算法（amortized variational algorithm）实现后验逼近。

Result: 在天文源识别、微生物群疾病检测和ICU脓毒症预测的模拟和实际应用实验中，该方法在未知环境中均优于现有预测方法。

Conclusion: 提出的基于潜变量的多环境预测方法在处理未知环境推广问题上具有显著优势，能够有效利用跨环境的结构平衡机制提升预测稳定性。

Abstract: We consider multi-environment prediction problems. We assume the environments change the distribution of a latent variable, while the mechanisms generating observed covariates and targets remain stable conditional on that variable. For example, hospitals or clinical cohorts may differ in the prevalence of latent patient states, even though the relationships between those states, physiological measurements, and outcomes remain unchanged. Given a dataset from multiple environments, we formulate a Bayesian model for such problems and derive the corresponding variational objective. We show that this objective decomposes into per-environment terms and an additional cross-environment balancing term induced by the model's structure. We use an empirical Bayes method to set the prior and incorporate it into the objective. Based on this objective, we develop an amortized variational algorithm for posterior approximation, and use the resulting learned latent variables to form predictions in new environments.We study our approach through simulations and real-world studies of astronomical source identification, microbiome-based disease detection, and ICU sepsis prediction. Across these settings, our method outperforms previous approaches for prediction in new environments.

</details>


### [131] [Sparse Functional Singular Value Decomposition for Biclustering and Triclustering Longitudinal Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05488)
*Yue Zhao,Thierry Chekouo,Sandra Safo*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: Tri-SfSVD 是一种用于纵向多维数据的聚类新方法，它通过直接处理稀疏、非均匀观测数据，在无需插补的前提下，通过同步优化实现多层级（主体、特征、时间）的特征选择与聚类，有效揭示了复杂疾病（如 IBD）和神经活动中的潜在异质性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的生物聚类和函数数据分析方法在处理IBD等多组学纵向数据时，由于数据的高维性、稀疏采样和非均匀观测，往往表现不佳且依赖于受限制的假设（如形状同质性）或预处理插补，因此需要一种能直接处理此类结构化复杂数据的分析方法。

Method: 提出了一种统一的稀疏函数奇异值分解框架（Tri-SfSVD）。该方法核心在于将连续轨迹估计与对主体（subject）、特征（feature）及时间子区域（temporal subregions）的同步稀疏惩罚优化结合，直接作用于观测数据，避免了传统的插补和形状同质性假设。

Result: 在仿真实验中，Tri-SfSVD 表现优于现有方法。在 IBD 多组学数据中，识别出了描述疾病异质性的主体-通路关联簇；在多通道 EEG 数据中，成功关联了酒精相关表型与特定的脑活动时空模式。

Conclusion: Tri-SfSVD 是一种处理高维、稀疏且非均匀采样纵向数据的有效框架。它通过整合连续轨迹估计与同步稀疏选择，无需插补，成功捕获了复杂数据中的多层级结构（双聚类和三聚类），为识别疾病亚型提供了强大的解释性工具。

Abstract: Identifying subtypes of complex conditions, such as Inflammatory Bowel Disease (IBD), often requires capturing latent patterns in longitudinal omics data. However, these data are typically high-dimensional, sparsely sampled, and irregularly observed over time, posing substantial challenges for conventional (bi)clustering and functional data analysis methods. We propose Tri-SfSVD, a unified sparse functional Singular Value Decomposition framework for discovering biclusters and triclusters in longitudinal data. Unlike existing functional biclustering methods that rely on ad hoc imputation or enforce restrictive shape-homogeneity assumptions, Tri-SfSVD integrates continuous trajectory estimation with simultaneous subject, feature, and temporal selection within a single optimization framework. By imposing sparse penalties across subjects, variables, and temporal subregions, the proposed method works directly on observed data to uncover localized structures at the subject, subject-feature, and subject-feature-time levels. Extensive simulations demonstrate that Tri-SfSVD outperforms existing approaches in high-dimensional settings. Applied to IBD multi-omics data, the method identified three biclusters linking sample clusters with distinct IBD-related clinical characteristics to microbial pathway groups associated with specific bacterial taxa, providing interpretable subject-pathway associations for characterizing disease heterogeneity. Applied to multi-channel EEG data, the method identified three triclusters linking sample clusters with distinct alcohol-related phenotypes to localized brain activity patterns, including subgroup differences separated by temporal subregions within the same spatial region.

</details>


### [132] [Conformal Risk-Averse Decision Making with Action Conditional Guarantee](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05551)
*Zihan Zhu,Shayan Kiyani,George Pappas. Hamed Hassani*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种动作条件一致性预测框架，通过精细化条件下的安全保证，优化了风险规避决策效率，实现了比传统方法更可靠的机器学习决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的一致性预测（Conformal Prediction）方法仅能提供“边缘安全保证”，无法满足高可靠决策系统中对特定动作的安全需求。需将预测集合转化为具备动作级安全保障的风险规避决策策略。

Method: 1. 提出动作条件一致性预测（Action-conditional conformal prediction）；2. 建立该预测集合与风险规避决策（对齐动作条件风险价值CVaR）的可行性空间联系；3. 提出基于针球损失最小化的有限样本算法。

Result: 在两个真实数据集上的实验表明，该方法在动作条件下的性能表现显著优于现有的边缘一致性基线。

Conclusion: 通过引入动作条件一致性预测，研究不仅在理论上强化了风险规避决策的安全性，还通过基于针球损失（pinball loss）的算法提供了有限样本下的实用保障，显著优于传统的边缘一致性基线。

Abstract: Reliable decision making pipelines powered by machine learning models require uncertainty quantification (UQ) methods that come with explicit safety guarantees. Conformal prediction provides such UQ by wrapping ML predictions into prediction sets, and recent work by Kiyani et al. (2025b) established that these sets can be translated into optimal risk-averse decision policies -- yet only inheriting marginal safety guarantees. We generalize and strengthen their results by (i) introducing action-conditional conformal prediction, which yields safety guarantees conditioned explicitly on each action taken by the decision maker, (ii) showing that action-conditional prediction sets serve as a proxy for the feasible decision space for risk-averse decision makers aiming to optimize action-conditional value-at-risk, and (iii) proposing a principled finite-sample algorithm based on pinball-loss minimization, connecting the framework of Gibbs et al. (2025) to action-conditional guarantees. Experiments on two real-world datasets confirm that our approach significantly improves action-conditional performance over conformal baselines.

</details>


### [133] [Finding Most Influential Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05919)
*Lucas D. Konrad,Nikolas Kuschnig*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 研究提出了一种高效算法，通过将复杂的 MIS 选择问题重构为一参数 top-k 问题，利用 Dinkelbach 方法实现了线性时间复杂度的全局优化或稳定近似，克服了传统方法无法处理大规模数据集的难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 识别最影响力子集（MIS）的传统做法需要遍历 $\binom{n}{k}$ 个子集，计算复杂度极高，在大规模数据集中不可行。该研究旨在寻找一种高效的方法来解决这一具有挑战性的组合优化问题。

Method: 1. 将最影响力子集（MIS）识别问题简化为一参数的 top-k 问题序列；2. 采用 Dinkelbach 方法进行求解，实现每迭代 $\mathcal{O}(n)$ 的计算复杂度；3. 在固定残差化输入下实现全局最优，对于估计的冗余函数，利用一致分母与生成的得分稳定性实现对一阶 oracle 正交得分目标的近似。

Result: 提出的算法在 univariate ratio 目标函数下能获得全局最优解；在估计的冗余函数前提下，该算法能近似 Oracle 正交得分目标，并在特定分离条件下实现精确的集合恢复；仿真实验证明该方法可处理以往在计算上不可行的 MIS 问题。

Conclusion: 该研究通过将具有线性分式 leave-set-out 效应的影响力子集（MIS）选择问题转化为参数化的 top-k 问题，利用 Dinkelbach 方法实现了高效的线性时间复杂度算法，在理论上保证了最优解或一阶近似，并通过模拟验证了其在处理大规模数据集时的有效性。

Abstract: Identifying most influential sets (MIS) - size-$k$ subsets whose removal maximally changes a target estimand - is typically infeasible because it requires searching over $\binom{n}{k}$ subsets. For estimands with linear-fractional leave-set-out effects, we show that MIS selection reduces to a one-parameter sequence of top-$k$ problems. Dinkelbach's method yields an algorithm with $\mathcal{O}(n)$ cost per iteration and finite termination. For fixed residualized inputs, the algorithm returns a globally optimal set for the univariate ratio objective, including the oracle-residualized partial linear model. With estimated nuisance functions, uniform denominator and generated-score stability imply approximation to the first-order oracle orthogonal-score objective; exact set recovery follows under a separation condition. Simulations and applications show that the method recovers exact MIS that were previously computationally inaccessible.

</details>


### [134] [EML-CD: Causal Mechanism Recovery via EML Symbolic Trees in Structure Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05942)
*Sota Asanuma*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 针对神经因果发现缺乏机制可解释性的问题，提出了 EML-CD 框架，通过门控 EML 二叉树自动发现封闭形式的因果方程，实现了因果结构发现与可解释反馈的同步优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的神经因果发现方法虽然能恢复有向无环图（DAG），但因其“黑盒”特性无法提取具体的因果机制，且从神经网络权重中反推机制被认为是病态问题。需要一种能在学习结构的同时直接给出显式、可解释方程的方法。

Method: 引入 EML 算子（Elementary-Mechanism-Learning），将每个因果边机制建模为门控 EML 二叉树，通过符号回归自动发现封闭逻辑形式的因果方程，并支持通过解析雅可比矩阵进行因果效应量化。

Result: 在 Sachs 蛋白质信号测试中 SHD 达 11.2，性能等同于 PC/GES；在双变量测试中准确恢复了 11 类基本函数中的 10 类；在符号合成基准测试中，其 f-MSE 表现显著优于固定字典方法的 SINDy；在 Causal Chambers 数据集上 F1 分数优于线性 OLS-BIC（0.444 vs. 0.273）。

Conclusion: EML-CD 提供了一个兼顾因果结构学习与机制可解释性的新范式。它通过将机制表达为封闭形式的方程，打破了传统黑盒神经网络在因果探索中的局限，在保持竞争性结构恢复精度的同时，提供了对因果关系的定量解析能力。

Abstract: Neural network (NN)-based nonlinear causal discovery methods recover DAG structure but leave each causal mechanism as a black box. Waxman et al. argued that extracting causal mechanisms from NN weights is ill-posed. We propose EML-CD, a framework that integrates the EML operator (capable of composing elementary functions from a single binary operator) into causal structure learning, with interpretable mechanism recovery as the primary objective. EML-CD represents each edge mechanism as a gated EML binary tree and automatically discovers closed-form causal equations. Analytical Jacobians can be directly computed from the output equations, enabling quantitative understanding of causal effects. On real data (Sachs protein signaling, d=11), EML-CD achieves SHD=11.2 +/- 0.4 (5-seed mean; baselines are single deterministic runs), on par with PC/GES within seed variance and below CAM, while attaching closed-form equations to each detected edge (precision 0.756, recall 0.365). In a controlled bivariate test with known mechanisms, EML-CD recovers 10 of 11 elementary function families faithfully (held-out shape correlation >= 0.96; only high-frequency sine is partial). On a symbolic synthetic benchmark, EML-CD attains a substantially lower and more stable held-out mechanism f-MSE than a fixed SINDy dictionary (mean 3.67 vs. 7644, the latter inflated by catastrophic extrapolation on one seed), although its structure recovery (SHD 14.0) only matches the dictionary and stays below specialized optimizers; on the Causal Chambers light-tunnel subset, a depth-2 model improves F1 over linear OLS-BIC (0.444 vs. 0.273).

</details>


### [135] [Fast and Robust Convergence Rate for TD(0) with Linear Function Approximation, Universal Learning Steps and I.I.D. Samples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05967)
*Ziad Kobeissi,Éloïse Berthier*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了线性函数近似下 TD(0) 算法的有限时间收敛性，通过 Polyak-Juditsky 平均法实现了对特征矩阵条件数不敏感的 O(1/k) 最优收敛速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 TD(0) 收敛性分析通常依赖于线性函数近似中特征矩阵的最小特征值，导致在病态条件下的界限过松或失效；作者旨在寻找一种更鲁棒、更优的有限时间收敛速率界限。

Method: 1. 分析了线性函数近似下的 TD(0) 算法，采用恒定学习速率和 Polyak-Juditsky 平均机制；2. 针对马尔可夫链的强混合假设，引入了 PCTD(0) 变体算法以优化收敛行为。

Result: 1. 证明了 MSE 的收敛速率为 O(1/k)；2. 该收敛界限不依赖于特征矩阵的最小特征值；3. 导出了一个常数因子小于 11 的尖锐界限；4. 引入了在强混合条件下性能更好的 PCTD(0) 算法。

Conclusion: 作者提出了一种针对线性函数近似（LFA）下 TD(0) 算法的改进分析框架，证明了该算法在 i.i.d. 采样和恒定步长条件下可实现 O(1/k) 的最优收敛速率，且该界限不依赖于特征矩阵的最小特征值，具有极好的鲁棒性。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the finite-time behavior of the TD(0) temporal-difference method with linear function approximation (LFA). We consider on-policy independent and identically distributed (i.i.d.) samples, a constant learning step, and the Polyak-Juditsky averaging method. We establish a new convergence rate, for the Mean-Square Error (MSE) on the approximated function, that is (i) fast in the sense that it admits an optimal dependency in the number of iterations k (i.e., of order 1/k), (ii) robust to ill-conditioning: it only depends on an initial error and modelindependent constants and (iii) sharp up to a multiplicative constant lower than 11. In particular, it does not depend on the smallest eigenvalue of the uncentered covariance matrix of the linear parametrization, unlike all pre-existing O(1/k) rates in the TD(0) literature. We also introduce PCTD(0), a variant of TD(0), which benefits from better convergence properties under an additional assumption of strong mixing on the Markov Chain.

</details>


### [136] [Adaptive Learning Rates with Surrogate Probability for Follow-the-Perturbed-Leader](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06043)
*Jongyeong Lee,Junya Honda,Shinji Ito,Chansoo Kim*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需精确概率计算的自适应学习率设计，解决了FTPL在实现BOBW性能保证方面的挑战，为该框架提供了更广泛的理论适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FTPL因其无需求解凸优化问题的计算高效性而备受关注，但由于其非优化性质，难以直接应用FTRL中基于选择概率的自适应学习率设计，限制了其在BOBW（Best-of-both-worlds）性能上的提升。

Method: 通过引入仅依赖于可用量而无需精确概率的“替代概率函数”（surrogate probability functions），为FTPL设计了自适应学习率，并结合Pareto扰动（shape parameter α > 1）进行理论分析。

Result: 证明了所提方法在Pareto扰动下（任意α > 1）能够实现BOBW保证，并成功将其应用于带专家建议的多臂赌博机问题，同时保持了FTPL原有的计算简单性。

Conclusion: 本文提出了一种基于替代概率函数的自适应学习率机制，使FTPL能够在保持计算高效性的同时，实现与FTRL相当的“两全其美”（BOBW）性能保证，并推广了Pareto扰动的适用范围。

Abstract: Follow-the-regularized-leader framework has shown effectiveness and flexibility in online learning problems, where the choice of learning rates are known to be crucial. Recently, adaptive learning rates defined in terms of the arm-selection probabilities, obtained by solving convex optimization, have achieved improved best-of-both-worlds (BOBW) guarantees in various bandit problems. In contrast, BOBW guarantees for its computationally efficient alternative, follow-the-perturbed-leader (FTPL), remain relatively limited since its optimization-free nature ironically makes the design of adaptive, probability-dependent learning rates non-trivial. To address this challenge, we propose an adaptive learning rate for FTPL by introducing surrogate probability functions that can be computed only from the available quantities, without requiring the exact probabilities. Based on these learning rates with surrogate functions, we provide the BOBW guarantee for FTPL with Pareto perturbations for any shape parameter $α>1$, generalizing prior results restricted to specific choices of $α=2$. We further show the BOBW guarantees for FTPL with adaptive learning rates in the bandit problem with expert advices. Our approach preserves the computational simplicity of FTPL while enabling probability-dependent adaptivity, and the surrogate-based methodology may be of independent interest in other algorithmic frameworks beyond FTPL and learning rate designs.

</details>


### [137] [Effective Dimensionality as an Operator Invariant for Physics-Preserving Constraint Adaptation in Physics-Informed Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06171)
*Cornelius Otchere,Michael Shields*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文分析了物理信息神经网络(PINNs)中的结构冲突，提出了通过Fisher信息矩阵定义的有效自由度(d_eff)来量化任务干扰，并基于此开发了一种子空间投影策略，实现了在不破坏预训练物理规律前提下的边界条件快速适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PINNs在处理物理控制方程和边界条件时，共享参数空间导致的任务干扰（task interference）限制了模型的性能和收敛性；缺乏量化这一结构冲突的诊断指标。

Method: 1. 利用Fisher信息矩阵定义并量化物理约束模型的有效自由度(d_eff)；
2. 分析有限维与无限维算子核空间与d_eff的关系；
3. 开发基于子空间投影的边界自适应算法（将参数更新限制在物理算子的零空间内）。

Result: 1. 对于有限维核算子，d_eff收敛于核维度，与网络架构无关，成为结构不变特征；
2. d_eff可作为评估问题良构性的先验诊断指标；
3. 子空间投影策略在处理边界条件变化时，相比梯度细调，能以秒级到分钟级的时间实现相当甚至更优的精度。

Conclusion: 本文提出的有效自由度（d_eff）量化方法揭示了PINN结构冲突的本质，并证明了基于子空间投影的边界自适应策略在解决任务干扰方面具有极高的效率和准确性，能够实现无需重训练的快速模型迁移。

Abstract: Physics-Informed Neural Networks inherently suffer from task interference because they rely on a shared parameter space to satisfy both governing differential equations and boundary conditions. We analyze this structural conflict using the Fisher Information Matrix to quantify the effective degrees of freedom ($d_{eff}$) in a physics-constrained model. Unlike the classical $d_{eff}$ which measures how many parameter directions are informed by data against a statistical prior, our $d_{eff}$ measures the dimension of the parameter directions unconstrained by the differential operator. For operators with finite-dimensional kernel, we show that $d_{eff}$ converges to the kernel dimension exactly, independent of network width, depth, or activation function, recasting it from a fit diagnostic into a structural invariant of the underlying continuous operator. For operators with infinite-dimensional kernel, $d_{eff}$ instead measures the network's finite-dimensional representational bandwidth for that kernel rather than recovering an integer invariant. Importantly, $d_{eff}$ also serves as an a priori structural diagnostic. Driving $d_{eff}$ of a well-posed problem to zero certifies that the physics and boundary constraints have absorbed the network's free directions. Building on this characterization, we introduce subspace projection strategies for boundary adaptation. Rather than retraining from scratch, we project parameter updates into the null space of the pre-trained physics operator so that new boundary conditions are satisfied without disturbing the learned physics. Gradient-based fine-tuning can match or exceed this but needs more wall-clock time and tuning, whereas subspace projection delivers near-equivalent quality in seconds to minutes. We validate on linear and nonlinear operators, demonstrating accurate adaptation to initial and boundary shifts and unencountered constraint types.

</details>


### [138] [Diffusion Models Observe Only Gradients: A Geometric Perspective on Score Matching Errors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06179)
*Naïl B. Khelifa,Richard E. Turner,Ramji Venkataramanan*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该论文指出传统的$L^2$得分匹配误差无法准确衡量扩散模型的分布质量。作者引入Helmholtz-Hodge分解，证明只有得分误差中的梯度分量才会影响目标分布，并提出了更准确的质量评价指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前主流的基于分数的扩散模型依赖$L^2$得分匹配误差来界定采样差异。但作者发现该指标在理论上存在缺陷：模型可能具有高$L^2$误差但仍能精确匹配目标分布，即$L^2$得分误差并非衡量分布质量的本质指标。

Method: 1. 利用Helmholtz-Hodge分解法将得分误差分解为梯度分量和旋度分量。
2. 证明不存在单调函数能将$L^2$得分误差统一作为分布差异的下界。
3. 推导仅依赖梯度分量的KL散度上界，并在路径空间与边际空间动力学之间建立联系。
4. 提出基于对偶Sobolev恒等式的梯度分量可追踪估计器。

Result: 1. 证明了以$L^2$得分误差作为分布散度的下界是不可能的。
2. 推导出更紧的KL散度上界，仅取决于得分误差的梯度分量。
3. 实验证明提出的梯度分量估计器与样本质量的相关性显著优于传统$L^2$误差。

Conclusion: $L^2$得分匹配误差并非衡量边际分布质量的合适指标。得分误差应通过Helmholtz-Hodge分解为梯度分量和旋度分量。仅梯度分量影响边际Fokker-Planck动力学。基于此改进的理论框架更准确地刻画了模型性能，并导出了与样本质量相关性更高的评价度量。

Abstract: Score-based diffusion models are typically trained by minimizing the $L^2$ score matching error, and standard theoretical analyses rely on this quantity to bound the sampling discrepancy between the learned and target distributions. We show the $L^2$ score error is not the right intrinsic measure of marginal distributional quality: a learned diffusion model can incur arbitrarily large $L^2$ score error while perfectly matching the target distribution. By decomposing score errors into a gradient and a solenoidal component (a Helmholtz-Hodge decomposition), we identify the geometric reason behind this: only the gradient component enters the marginal Fokker-Planck dynamics, while the solenoidal component is structurally invisible. We make this precise in three results. First, building on the corrected geometry, we prove an impossibility result: no monotone function of the $L^2$ score error can uniformly lower bound any divergence between the learned and target distributions. Second, we derive an upper bound on the Kullback-Leibler divergence that depends only on the observable gradient component of the error, tightening the standard Girsanov bound and identifying its looseness as the cost of operating on path-space rather than marginal-space dynamics. Third, we give a tractable estimator of the gradient component via a dual Sobolev identity, which is shown to empirically correlate substantially better with sample quality than the full $L^2$ error.

</details>


### [139] [Anchor PCA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06233)
*Benedikt Seiter,Anya Fries,Julius von Kügelgen,Jonas Peters*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 针对多域数据的 PCA 降维问题，本文提出了 Anchor PCA。该方法通过平衡总体方差与跨领域的一致性，提取共享的变异方向，从而有效地去除特定领域的干扰，提高了对未见领域数据的泛化能力和稳健性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对多域数据，传统的将所有数据池化（Pooling）后进行 PCA 的方法，容易受到某些特定领域中存在的高变异伪方向（spurious directions）的干扰，导致泛化能力下降。因此，需要一种能够找到跨领域共享变异方向，以实现更稳健的低秩嵌入的方法。

Method: 提出了一种名为 Anchor PCA 的方法。该方法通过在修改后的目标矩阵上进行 PCA，平衡了“总体解释方差”与“共享嵌入和特定领域嵌入之间的一致性”。该模型在数学上被证明可以恢复最大不变子空间，且在有界领域特定协方差膨胀下具有极小极大重构解释。

Result: 在模拟实验和带有时间漂移的真实世界气体传感器数据上，Anchor PCA 成功恢复了最大不变子空间，并在未见过的领域中展现出了比池化基准和其他替代方法更高的解释方差。

Conclusion: Anchor PCA 是一种在多域数据中进行稳健无监督降维的有前途的方法，能够比简单的合并数据方法更好地泛化到未见过的领域。

Abstract: Principal component analysis (PCA) is one of the most widely used unsupervised dimension reduction techniques. We study PCA for data from multiple related domains. Since principal components generally differ across domains, one way to obtain a shared low-rank embedding is to perform PCA on the pooled data. However, this approach can focus on spurious directions that exhibit high variation in only a few domains. To find a robust embedding that still explains most variance in unseen but similar domains, we propose instead to focus on shared directions of variation. To this end, we introduce Anchor PCA which trades off overall explained variance with agreement between the shared and domain-specific low-rank embeddings. Anchor PCA amounts to PCA on a modified target matrix and thus can be solved efficiently. Moreover, we show that Anchor PCA recovers a maximal invariant subspace and admits a minimax reconstruction interpretation under bounded domain-specific covariance inflations. On simulated and real-world gas sensor data with temporal drift, we demonstrate, respectively, that Anchor PCA recovers the maximally invariant subspace and yields embeddings that explain more variance on unseen domains than the pooling baseline and a worst-case alternative. Taken together, these findings establish Anchor PCA as a promising approach to robust unsupervised dimension reduction from multi-domain data.

</details>


### [140] [Discrete Causal Representations from Heterogeneous Domains: A Bayesian Approach with Social Survey Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06288)
*Ankur Garg,Michael Stettler,Aaron Schein,Julius von Kügelgen*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于贝叶斯方法的因果表征学习框架，利用多环境数据中的偏移信息，通过层级模型和序列蒙特卡洛推断，成功从离散潜变量中学习到了现实世界数据（如社会调查）中可解释的因果结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的因果表征学习虽然在可识别性理论方面有深入研究，但在处理含有分布偏移的多环境数据时，缺乏具备不确定性感知且适用于实际复杂场景（如社会科学调查数据）的实用方法。

Method: 提出了一个基于贝叶斯推断的层级模型框架：1. 将因果假设与可解释性需求转化为先验分布和参数选择；2. 专门针对离散隐含因果概念和多点软干预（soft interventions）进行建模；3. 采用序列蒙特卡洛（Sequential Monte Carlo）采样方法来近似计算多模态后验分布。

Result: 在社会调查数据实验中，模型成功推理出了有意义的高层次因果概念（如文化价值观或政治观点）及其合理的因果关系。该方法不仅证明了其从低层观测数据提取高层潜变量的能力，还通过贝叶斯框架有效捕捉了模型的不确定性。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了一种适用于处理多环境、离散因果概念的贝叶斯因果表征学习框架。通过结合层级建模与序列蒙特卡洛采样，该方法能够从复杂现实数据（如社会调查数据）中有效提取隐含的因果变量及其相互关系，并提供不确定性评估，验证了该方法在现实世界应用中的有效性和可解释性。

Abstract: Causal representation learning aims to infer the high-level latent causal concepts that give rise to observed low-level measurements. This is particularly relevant for heterogeneous data from different environments or domains since distribution shifts often arise through sparse, localized changes in some of the underlying causal mechanisms, while other parts of the generative process remain unchanged. Whereas identifiability of causal representations has been studied extensively, practical uncertainty-aware methods and real-world use cases remain less explored. In this work, we propose a Bayesian approach to learning causal representations from multi-environment data, focusing on the case of discrete causal concepts and unknown multi-node soft interventions. To this end, we translate causal assumptions and interpretability desiderata into suitable priors and parametric choices within a hierarchical model. We then devise an inference scheme based on sequential Monte Carlo sampling to approximate the resulting multimodal posterior. We showcase our approach through case studies on social survey data, where latent causal concepts correspond to cultural values or political opinions, measurements to survey responses, and environments to different countries or states. Our model infers meaningful high-level concepts and plausible causal relations among them, demonstrating its utility for learning causal representations of complex real-world data.

</details>


### [141] [Symmetric Divergence and Normalized Similarity: A Unified Topological Framework for Representation Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06342)
*Yan Wang,Tianyang Hu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 针对现有拓扑数据分析(TDA)工具在神经网络表征比较中存在的缺陷，本文提出了对称表示拓扑散度(SRTD)以实现精确诊断，并开发了归一化拓扑相似度(NTS)以实现尺度不变的鲁棒基准测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的配对拓扑散度（如RTD）存在启发式不对称性以及分数不受限制且依赖样本量的问题，导致其在跨场景基准测试中的不可靠性。

Method: 1. 提出对称表示拓扑散度(SRTD)及其高效变体SRTD-lite，通过单一交叉条形码签名解决理论不对称性；2. 提出归一化拓扑相似度(NTS)，利用层次合并顺序的秩相关性，构建范围在[-1, 1]之间的尺度不变指标。

Result: 实验表明，新工具包能捕捉到几何度量无法检测到的CNN功能转移，并能在距离饱和情况下稳健地映射大语言模型（LLM）的谱系关系，性能优于或补充了CKA等现有指标。

Conclusion: 该研究通过引入SRTD及其轻量化变体解决了现有拓扑散度度量的不对称性和计算复杂性问题；提出的NTS度量实现了规模和样本不变性，为不同神经网络表征之间的鲁棒基准测试提供了严谨的拓扑学视角。

Abstract: Topological Data Analysis (TDA) offers a principled, intrinsic lens for comparing neural representations. However, existing paired topological divergences (e.g., RTD) are limited by heuristic asymmetry and, more critically, unbounded scores that depend on sample size, hindering reliable cross-scenario benchmarking. To address these challenges, we develop a unified topological toolkit serving two complementary needs: fine-grained structural diagnosis and robust, standardized evaluation. First, we complete the RTD framework by introducing Symmetric Representation Topology Divergence (SRTD) and its efficient variant SRTD-lite. Beyond resolving the theoretical asymmetry of prior variants, SRTD consolidates diagnostic information into a single, comprehensive cross-barcode signature. This allows for precise localization of structural discrepancies and serves as an effective optimization objective without the overhead of dual directional computations. Second, to enable reliable benchmarking across heterogeneous settings, we propose Normalized Topological Similarity (NTS). By measuring the rank correlation of hierarchical merge orders, NTS yields a scale-invariant metric bounded between -1 and 1, effectively overcoming the scale and sample-dependence of unnormalized divergences. Experiments across synthetic and real-world deep learning settings demonstrate that our toolkit captures functional shifts in CNNs missed by geometric measures and robustly maps LLM genealogy even under distance saturation, offering a rigorous, topology-aware perspective that complements measures like CKA.

</details>


### [142] [Function-Space Priors for Bayesian Neural ODEs with Application to Vessel Trajectory Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06351)
*Jaeyeong Lee,Wonmo Koo,Heeyoung Kim*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 针对AIS船舶轨迹预测中存在的缺失数据与不确定性量化挑战，提出了一种结合高斯过程核先验与概率多重打靶的贝叶斯神经ODE方法，在保持物理一致性的同时实现了可靠的轨迹预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的AIS轨迹预测中，各向同性的高斯权重先验无法有效捕捉船舶运动的物理特性（如平滑性与局部相关性）；现有的函数空间贝叶斯方法难以直接应用于神经ODE，且直接对轨迹施加高斯过程先验在非线性求解中存在微分方程难以分析处理的难题。

Method: 1. 在神经ODE的向量场上引入基于高斯过程（GP）核函数的正则化项，以编码轨迹平滑性和局部性等物理结构特性；2. 使用概率多重打靶法（Probabilistic Multiple Shooting）解耦时间片段推理，在保证全局一致性的同时提升对长轨迹的处理能力；3. 通过变分推断（Variational Inference）进行训练，实现对船舶运动动力学的不确定性估计。

Result: 提出了一种能够有效结合物理结构先验与连续时间序列建模能力的贝叶斯神经ODE框架。该方法解决了在非线性动力学系统中进行贝叶斯推断的难点，在长时、不规则采样的AIS轨迹预测任务中，不仅提升了预测准确度，还提供了更校准（well-calibrated）的不确定性估计。

Conclusion: 本研究通过在神经ODE向量场上引入基于核函数的正则化方法，并结合概率多重打靶技术，有效实现了船舶轨迹的连续时间建模及其不确定性量化。该方法在处理不规则采样及缺失数据方面优于传统方法，并为海上决策提供了可靠的概率性预测框架。

Abstract: Vessel trajectory prediction from Automatic Identification System (AIS) data is essential for maritime situational awareness, yet it remains challenging due to irregular sampling, missing reports, and complex dynamics. Beyond accurate point forecasts, maritime applications also demand well-calibrated uncertainty estimates for reliable decision-making. Bayesian Neural Ordinary Differential Equations (ODEs) offer a principled framework for continuous-time trajectory modeling with uncertainty quantification by placing a prior over the neural vector field parameters. However, the commonly used isotropic Gaussian weight prior fails to encode informative structural properties of vessel dynamics, such as smoothness and locality. Existing function-space Bayesian neural network methods address this limitation for static mappings, but do not transfer directly to Neural ODEs, where the primary quantity of interest is the trajectory rather than the vector field itself. In principle, one could place a Gaussian process (GP) prior directly over ODE solutions, but this requires propagating distributions through a nonlinear ODE solver, which is analytically intractable. To address this challenge, we adopt a practical approach that imposes a GP-kernel-based prior directly on the vector field evaluated at a finite set of measurement points. Specifically, we augment the standard weight-space variational objective with a kernel-based regularizer that penalizes deviations of the vector field from the structure implied by a GP prior. To handle long and irregular AIS trajectories, we further combine this function-space regularization with probabilistic multiple shooting, which decouples inference across temporal segments while maintaining global consistency.

</details>


### [143] [Conformal Risk Sharing: Certified Cost Allocation with Participation Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06391)
*Ieva Kazlauskaite*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种基于共形预测的“共形风险分担”框架，在无需分布假设的情况下，为参与集体风险分担的个体提供可信的风险上限保障，有效解决了参与者因风险分担受损而退出机制的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在风险分担机制中，虽然分担稀有风险能减轻个体负担，但若参与者因机制受损，则该机制不可持续。需要一种能够在无需先验概率分布假设的前提下，为每个参与者提供可信的义务上限，并验证参与者不会因参与而遭受重度损失的机制。

Method: 提出「共形风险分担」(Conformal Risk Sharing) 方法：1. 形式化定义「认证分配问题」(Certified Allocation Problem)；2. 使用分裂共形校准(Split Conformal Calibration)对预留的校准数据进行处理；3. 为每个参与者生成经验证的义务上限，确保在保障个体利益的同时实现风险平摊。

Result: 在合成数据及真实世界数据（如降水量和能源合作社）的实验中，该框架显著降低了高风险主体的极端义务压力，同时有效控制了对其他参与者的负面影响，实现了个体保障与集体风险共担的权衡。

Conclusion: 本文提出的「共形风险分担」(Conformal Risk Sharing) 框架通过结合可解释的共享策略与分裂共形校准，在无需分布假设的前提下，成功平衡了风险分担与个人利益保障，为稀有灾难性事件的损失分担提供了一种可信、稳健的机制。

Abstract: Sharing the financial impact of rare adverse events across a group can soften extreme individual burdens, but any participant made worse off by the arrangement has reason to leave. A credible mechanism must therefore provide each agent with a trustworthy cap on their future obligation and should be deployed only if the aggregate harm across participants is bounded. We formalise this as the Certified Allocation Problem: from finite data and without distributional assumptions, find a redistribution rule, produce obligation caps for every participant, and verify that no participant is made materially worse off. We propose Conformal Risk Sharing, which solves this problem by pairing an interpretable sharing policy with split conformal calibration. The sharing intensity is tuned on training data, while held-out calibration data produces distribution-free per-agent guarantees (valid under exchangeability). Experiments on synthetic and real-world data, including precipitation and energy-cooperative data, confirm that the framework can substantially reduce extreme obligations for high-risk agents while controlling harm to others.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [144] [SET: Stream-Event-Triggered Scheduling for Efficient CUDA Graph Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05495)
*Zhengxiong Li,Tsung-Wei Huang,Umit Ogras*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 该文提出了一种优化CUDA运行时框架，通过多流任务并行模型与基于图的执行调度，有效解决了GPU同步延迟与调度开销问题，实现了显著的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的CUDA应用受限于主机-设备同步延迟和内核调度开销，即便经过优化，硬件资源（计算核心和拷贝引擎）仍常常处于欠利用状态。

Method: 提出了一种用于任务并行流水线的CUDA运行时框架，包含两大核心创新：1. 基于事件链（event-chaining）和工作窃取（work-stealing）的多流任务并行模型；2. 具有流级缓冲区的基于图的执行流，以确保多任务并发下的内存安全。

Result: 在代表性现实工作负载上的测试显示，与现有的CUDA图基准相比，速度提升了1.15-1.44倍，并将调度开销降低了18-54%。

Conclusion: 该研究提出的框架通过优化任务调度与同步机制，显著提升了GPU的执行效率和资源利用率，证明了在任务并行流水线场景下，减少同步开销和细粒度管理执行流的有效性。

Abstract: Achieving peak GPU performance remains a significant challenge as the system throughput is constrained by host-device synchronization delays and kernel scheduling overheads, even with aggressive kernel optimizations and batch processing. Furthermore, existing approaches often underutilize hardware resources such as compute cores and copy engines due to scheduling overheads. To address these problems, we propose a CUDA runtime framework for task-parallel pipelines to minimize the synchronization overheads and the gap between kernel executions. The proposed solution combines two innovations: (1) a multi-stream task-parallel pipeline programming model that leverages event-chaining and work-stealing mechanisms to fully utilize available hardware resources; (2) a graph-based execution flow with per-stream buffers to ensure memory safety for multiple in-flight jobs running concurrently. Extensive evaluations on representative real-world workloads show 1.15--1.44X speedup and reduce scheduling overheads by 18--54% compared to state-of-the-art CUDA graph baselines.

</details>


### [145] [Latent Reasoning Guidance for Parallel Code Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05518)
*Tomer Bitan,Erel Kaplan,Roee Bar-Yadin,Lian Ghrayeb,Le Chen,Samyak Jhaveri,Niranjan Hasabnis,Gal Oren*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出在代码生成前，通过潜空间过程奖励模型（PRM）筛选隐藏状态轨迹的方法，在无需重训大模型的情况下，显著提高了编码任务的可执行成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前自主代理在编码任务中过度依赖耗时的测试时计算（如多次运行反馈），且缺乏在生成代码前的早期引导机制。作者旨在探讨潜空间推理是否能作为一种有效的早期干预手段。

Method: 提出了一种测试时潜空间引导方法：训练一个小型过程奖励模型（PRM）作用于连续潜在前缀，在最终代码解码前对不同的隐藏状态轨迹进行评分与选择（与后续译码优化兼容）。

Result: 在ParaTrans基准测试中，平均验证率从32.89%提升至42.1%，表现优于微调及基线模型，且在迭代修复循环中优势依然持续。

Conclusion: 在不重新训练主生成模型的前提下，通过潜空间PRM（过程奖励模型）进行分支选择，能有效提升复杂编码任务的可执行成功率。证明了在模型提交代码前，潜空间中存在可利用的潜在逻辑轨迹。

Abstract: Tackling complex coding tasks often requires autonomous agents and iterative repair pipelines. These increasingly rely on large amounts of test-time computation, often spending many decoding and repair steps before discovering whether a program compiles, runs, or validates. Executable parallel-code translation is an effective setting for earlier guidance because success is behavioral rather than textual. However, most guidance methods act only after complete programs or textual traces are decoded. This motivates the question: can latent reasoning provide an earlier intervention point, before the model commits to code?
  We study a test-time latent guidance method for this setting that trains a smaller Process Reward Model (PRM) over continuous latent prefixes and uses it to select among alternate hidden-state trajectories before final code decoding, separately from but compatible with post-decoding optimization. On a 76-task ParaTrans benchmark evaluation, latent PRM guidance improves mean validation rate from 32.89% with unguided latent reasoning to 42.1%, outperforming fine-tuned and vanilla baselines in the same setting. These gains persist under the same three-iteration repair loop. These results provide bounded evidence that useful alternative latent continuations exist and that PRM-scored latent branch selection can improve executable outcomes in this setting without retraining the main generative model.

</details>


### [146] [PoCQ: Proof of Contribution Quality as a Lightweight Blockchain Consensus for Secure Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05642)
*Sudad Abed,Nasser Sabar,Abdun Mahmood,Mohammad Jabed Morshed Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 针对去中心化联邦学习的安全性与效率难题，本文提出了基于区块链的 PoCQ 框架，通过声誉驱动的共识和轻量级验证，显著提升了模型在投毒攻击下的健壮性与计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 去中心化联邦学习（FL）由于缺乏中央协调者，面临模型投毒、不可靠验证及高昂验证开销的挑战。

Method: 提出了一种名为“贡献质量证明”（PoCQ）的区块链共识框架，核心包括：1. 基于加密承诺和轻量级范数（norm-based）的验证方式；2. 基于历史贡献质量的声誉驱动共识机制；3. 仅在区块链上存储紧凑的审计元数据以保证扩展性。

Result: 在 poisoning 场景下，相较于现有最先进方法，PoCQ 在医疗数据集的非同源（non-IID）设置下准确率提升 34.1%，全局平均准确率提升 11%，且每轮验证时间平均降低 21.27%。

Conclusion: PoCQ 通过引入声誉机制和轻量级验证，有效地在去中心化联邦学习中平衡了安全性、健壮性和计算效率，是解决恶意投毒攻击和验证开销问题的有效方案。

Abstract: Decentralized Federated Learning (FL) removes reliance on centralized coordinators but remains vulnerable to model poisoning, unreliable validation, and high validation overhead. This paper introduces Proof of Contribution Quality (PoCQ), a blockchain-based consensus framework designed to secure decentralized FL through reputation-aware validation and aggregation. PoCQ evaluates client updates using cryptographic commitments and lightweight norm-based validation, enabling efficient detection of malicious contributions while limiting validation cost. A reputation-driven consensus mechanism dynamically adjusts the influence of participants based on their historical contribution quality, while the blockchain stores only compact audit metadata to preserve scalability. Extensive experiments under poisoning scenarios across three benchmark datasets demonstrate that PoCQ outperforms the strongest state-of-the-art methods, achieving accuracy gains of 34.1% on challenging medical datasets in highly non-iid settings and an 11% improvement in global average accuracy. In addition, PoCQ reduces validation time by 21.27% on average per round, highlighting its effectiveness in jointly enhancing robustness and efficiency for fully decentralized federated learning.

</details>


### [147] [IN2P3 Computing Center 2024 Workload Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05914)
*Guillaume Cochard,Bertrand Simon*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提供并分析了法国IN2P3计算中心2024年全年的大规模作业运行数据集（4400万条记录），旨在通过提供更长的时间跨度和详细的内存使用指标，提升调度算法仿真研究的真实性与准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有计算中心数据集往往存在时段较短、缺乏内存使用细节或来源单一等问题，无法全面反映真实的周期性用户行为，限制了调度算法评估的真实性和有效性。

Method: 对法国IN2P3计算中心2024年全年的作业执行日志进行数据收集、清洗与结构化处理，涵盖了约4400万个作业、1000个用户及大规模集群配置参数（312台机器、46k线程、105TB内存）。

Result: 构建了一个包含2024全年、约4400万条真实作业记录的高质量数据集，详细记录了内存使用情况及时间维度特征，支持对季节性、月度和周度用户模式进行深入分析与调度算法仿真。

Conclusion: 该数据集通过提供包含大规模作业执行特征、内存使用情况及较长时段（一年）的真实数据，弥补了现有数据集在时效性和多维度指标上的缺失，为评估调度算法在复杂用户行为模式下的表现提供了关键资源。

Abstract: This paper provides and analyzes a dataset detailing the characteristics and execution data of all jobs submitted to the IN2P3 Computing Center (Villeurbanne, France), a national research and support unit of the CNRS, in 2024. The main additional value of this contribution compared to previously available datasets consists in the combination of an extended time interval considered, the inclusion of memory usage data and its recency, on top on improving the diversity of datasets provenance. This allows researchers to simulate and evaluate scheduling algorithms on a real workload over a large time window. Thus, specificities due to seasonal, monthly, and weekly user behaviors can be taken into account, which is not possible with smaller or synthetic datasets. It is composed of 44M jobs submitted by 1k users running on a cluster of a maximum of 312 machines supporting 46k concurrent threads and providing 105To of RAM.

</details>


### [148] [Beyond Greedy Chunking: SLO-Aware Sliding-Window Scheduling for LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05933)
*Yuansheng Chen,Yue Zhang,Xuan Mo,Weigang Wu,Jialun Li*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: SlidingServe 是一款针对在线 LLM 推理的滑动窗口驱动型调度系统，通过精细化的延迟预测、动态分块和优先级管理，在确保响应延迟（SLO）的同时，显著提升了系统吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 推理系统在处理高并发交互式应用时，由于采用粗粒度的输出控制，导致资源调度效率低下，且难以满足精细化的服务质量（QoS/SLO）要求。

Method: 提出了一套 SLO 感知的调度系统 SlidingServe，包含四个核心组件：1) 重量级批处理延迟预测器（Lightweight Batch Latency Predictor）；2) SlidingChunker 用于动态分块；3) 多级优先级排序器（Multi-Level Priority Sorter）平衡公平性与效率；4) 基于动态规划的 BatchConstructor 来优化批处理请求的选择，以降低 SLO 违规风险。

Result: 实验表明，与现有先进调度系统相比，SlidingServe 在各种负载条件下提升了高达 30% 的服务容量，在重负载模式下将 SLO 违规率降低了 16% 至 53%。

Conclusion: SlidingServe 通过结合滑动窗口机制、动态分块、多级优先级排序及批量构建器，有效解决了 LLM 在线服务在保证 SLO 的前提下吞吐量受限的问题，是提升 LLM 推理系统效率的有效方案。

Abstract: With the rapid growth of interactive applications in large language model (LLM) online services, maintaining high system throughput while ensuring user-perceived latency has become a key issue in inference scheduling. Existing LLM service systems rely on coarse-grained output constraints, making it difficult to effectively handle resource contention among multiple requests, resulting in low resource utilization efficiency and limited support for fine-grained quality of service (QoS) differentiation. We present SlidingServe, a sliding-window-driven SLO-Aware scheduling system for online LLM inference. SlidingServe designed a lightweight batch latency predictor to estimate the execution time of a batch. Based on this, SlidingServe uses SlidingChunker to combine information from the current iteration and the next iteration to achieve dynamic chunking and improve the overall system throughput while maintaining strict QoS guarantees. SlidingServe introduces Multi-Level Priority Sorter to sort candidate requests in order to balance fairness and efficiency. Additionally, when multiple requests within the same batch are at risk of SLO violating,SlidingServe introduces BatchConstructor, which uses dynamic programming to select the set of requests to execute in the current round, mitigating the SLO violation risk of critical requests.Our evaluation demonstrates that SlidingServe can improve service capacity by up to 30% compared to advanced scheduling systems under various load conditions, and further reduces the rate of SLO violation by 16%-53% under heavy-load inference mode.

</details>


### [149] [Demystifying NVSHMEM: A System-Level Analysis on Symmetric Memory and Device-Initiated Operations in GPU Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05951)
*Yijun Ma,Siyuan Shen,Tiancheng Chen,Akhil Langer,Jiri Kraus,Benjamin Glick,Craig Belusar,Jeff Hammond,Torsten Hoefler*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文对英伟达 NVSHMEM 通信库进行了深度分析，探讨其对称内存模型与高性能通信机制，并证实了其在优化 GPU 驱动的通信负载方面的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管 NVSHMEM 广泛使用，但目前缺乏对其设计原理、系统行为和性能特性的系统性理解，相关信息分散于文档和源代码中。

Method: 对 NVSHMEM 的编程模型、系统实现以及性能特性进行了系统性研究，重点分析了对称内存、单边操作和设备端集合通信；并以 DeepEP 作为案例研究，评估其在稀疏深度学习工作负载中的表现。

Result: 研究表明，NVSHMEM 提出的设备端对称内存编程模型对于实现细粒度、GPU 驱动的通信并逼近硬件性能极限至关重要。

Conclusion: NVSHMEM 确立了作为 GPU 集群系统构建块的地位，展示了其在细粒度、GPU 驱动通信方面的优势，并指出了未来通信运行时优化的方向。

Abstract: NVSHMEM is NVIDIA's OpenSHMEM-based PGAS communication library for GPU clusters, enabling GPU-initiated, one-sided communication through symmetric memory. Despite its growing adoption, a system-level understanding of its design and behavior remains scattered across documentation, source code, and application experience. This paper presents a concise study of NVSHMEM's programming model, implementation, and performance characteristics, focusing on symmetric memory, one-sided operations, and device-side collectives. We also examine DeepEP as a case study of NVSHMEM in performance-critical sparse deep learning workloads. Our analysis shows that NVSHMEM pioneered a device-side symmetric-memory programming model that enables fine-grained GPU-driven communication and is important for approaching the hardware performance limit. Overall, this work defines NVSHMEM's role as a systems building block, highlights its design tradeoffs, and identifies opportunities for improving GPU communication runtimes.

</details>
