Conclusion: 本文首次定量评估了cGAN预测的流场信息在三维水下机器人路径规划中的价值。cGAN替代模型能够以极高的实时推理速度（微秒级），恢复高保真RANS CFD模拟中约45%-60%的性能增益（降低能耗及规避高速尾流区），证明了该方法在边缘计算设备上的可行性。

Abstract: Autonomous underwater vehicle (AUV) launch and recovery (LAR) into the hull of an advancing host platform requires traversal of a complex, three-dimensional propeller wake whose hydrodynamic structure cannot be characterised by a uniform current model. High-fidelity Reynolds-Averaged Navier-Stokes (RANS) Computational Fluid Dynamics (CFD) simulations resolve this structure with sufficient accuracy for path planning, but their computational cost renders them impractical for onboard use. We address this gap by integrating two conditional generative adversarial network (cGAN) architectures -- a regularised PatchGAN and a 2D3DGAN with self-attention -- as drop-in replacements for RANS CFD data within a three-dimensional, energy-weighted A* path planning framework. Both generators are driven by a hierarchical pipeline that synthesises full $128^3$ voxel flow field volumes from scalar operating condition inputs alone, with end-to-end inference times of approximately 28-146 $μ$s, compared to hours for a single RANS computation. We benchmark all four environmental knowledge levels: uniform current, ground-truth CFD, PatchGAN, and 2D3DGAN~SA across 19,800 independently generated trajectories spanning 550 distinct flow conditions. Full CFD wake knowledge reduces energy expenditure by 5.7-12.5% and high-velocity wake-core encounters by up to 77.8% relative to uniform-current planning, with both benefits scaling with operating severity. The cGAN surrogates recover approximately 45-60% of the CFD energy benefit and high-velocity cell avoidance benefit while operating at inference speeds compatible with edge device use. These results provide the first systematic quantification of the downstream path planning value of cGAN-predicted hydrodynamic fields in a three-dimensional maritime robotics application.

</details>


### [187] [Towards Realistic 3D Sonar Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06130)
*Youssef Attia,Davide Costa,Francesco Wanderlingh,Filippo Campagnaro,Enrico Simetti*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对传统水下仿真缺乏物理声学逼真度的问题，本文提出了一种基于GPU加速与物理声学建模的3D声纳仿真架构，并经由HIL测试验证了其在SLAM系统中的应用可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的水下仿真方案多基于几何渲染（类似于LiDAR），无法模拟水下声学的复杂物理特性（如折射、多径干扰和相位相关信号形成），限制了水下机器人复杂3D感知与自主导航算法的开发。

Method: 提出了一种结合GPU加速图形引擎与物理声学传播原理的模块化架构；在NVIDIA Isaac Sim中实现了基于Water Linked 3D-15传感器的体积化3D声纳模型；通过硬件在环（HIL）配置，结合修改后的FastLIO2 SLAM算法在NVIDIA Jetson Orin Nano上进行了验证。

Result: 成功构建了高保真3D声纳仿真器；在SLAM流程中实现了声纳、DVL、IMU和压力计数据的融合；通过与港口板桩检查的实测数据对比，验证了仿真器的有效性，并明确了进一步优化方向。

Conclusion: 本文提出的模块化架构成功缩小了仿真与现实（sim-to-real）的差距，为实现基于声学传播的仿真感测奠定了基础，并通过实验验证了其在SLAM任务中的有效性。

Abstract: As underwater robotics research increasingly addresses complex 3D perception and autonomous navigation, the fidelity of sonar simulation has become a key factor in algorithm development. Current simulation frameworks typically rely on geometry-driven rendering, approximating 3D sonar as an underwater equivalent to LiDAR, which fails to account for fundamental acoustic phenomena such as refraction, multi-path interference, and phase-dependent signal formation. This paper proposes a modular architecture for realistic 3D sonar simulation that integrates GPU-accelerated graphics engines with physically grounded acoustic propagation principles. We implement a volumetric 3D sonar model within the NVIDIA Isaac Sim environment, modeled after the Water Linked 3D-15 sensor, and integrate it into a comprehensive underwater simulation framework. The system is validated through a hardware-in-the-loop configuration, where a modified FastLIO2 SLAM pipeline, executed on an NVIDIA Jetson Orin Nano, performs sensor fusion using synthetic 3D sonar, DVL, IMU, and pressure data. Finally, a qualitative comparison between simulated outputs and real-world data from harbor sheet-pile inspections is provided, characterizing the remaining sim-to-real gap and establishing a roadmap toward fully acoustics-driven volumetric sensing.

</details>


### [188] [MotionDisco: Motion Discovery for Extreme Humanoid Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06139)
*Ilyass Taouil,Michal Ciebelski,Shafeef Omar,Haizhou Zhao,Angela Dai,Aaron M. Johnson,Majid Khadiv*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MotionDisco 是首个通过 LLM 引导的演化搜索自动发现并部署人形机器人长跨度移动操作技能的框架，无需人工示范。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有人形机器人长跨度移动操作技能获取依赖昂贵的人工遥操作或动作重定向，而该任务的空间复杂度随任务跨度和场景物体数量呈组合式增长，亟需一种无需人工介入的自动化发现方案。

Method: 采用“LLM 引导演化搜索”与“高效顺序运动学轨迹优化及剪枝策略”相结合的框架；LLM 用于引导交互序列搜索，轨迹优化器用于生成物理可行的动作，最后通过强化学习训练跟踪策略并部署至真实机器人。

Result: 在多个极具挑战性的长跨度任务中成功发现全身体运动轨迹，并成功将习得技能迁移至真实的人形机器人平台，验证了算法的有效性。

Conclusion: MotionDisco 证明了通过自动化演化搜索完全发现并部署长跨度人形机器人移动操作技能的可行性，无需人工示范。

Abstract: We present MotionDisco, a framework that discovers contact-rich, long-horizon humanoid loco-manipulation motions from scratch, without relying on teleoperation or motion retargeting from human demonstrations. This is challenging because the space of possible contact interactions grows combinatorially with the task horizon and the number of objects in the scene. MotionDisco enables rapid discovery of novel motions by coupling a large language model (LLM) guided evolutionary search over sequences of interactions with an efficient sequential kinodynamic trajectory optimizer and pruning strategy, enabling the rapid discovery of novel skills. Through extensive ablation studies, we show that our LLM-guided search discovers successful whole-body trajectories across several challenging long-horizon tasks. Finally, by training reinforcement learning tracking policies on the discovered trajectories, we transfer the motions to a real humanoid robot. This is the first work to discover and deploy long-horizon humanoid loco-manipulation skills entirely through automated evolutionary search. Supplementary videos of the experiments are available at: https://youtu.be/DHiVz34QYlw.

</details>


### [189] [AffordanceVLA: A Vision-Language-Action Model Empowering Action Generation through Affordance-Aware Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06155)
*Qize Yu,Jiadi You,Yuran Wang,Jiaqi Liang,Bowen Ping,Yang Tian,Yue Chen,Minghong Cai,Zeying Gong,Ruihai Wu,Yinchuan Li,Junwei Liang,Yingcong Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AffordanceVLA 通过引入结构化的可供性预测（Which/Where/How-to-Act）作为中间桥梁，解决了 VLA 模型在机器人操控中感知与动作映射不精确的问题，实现了更高效的指令遵循。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉-语言-动作 (VLA) 模型在将预训练 VLMs 的语义知识转换为精确的机器人动作控制时，存在空间和逻辑上的不匹配，导致精细化操控能力受限。

Method: 1. 提出 AffordanceVLA 框架，引入 (1) Which2Act（目标定位）、(2) Where2Act（可供性图估计）、(3) How2Act（3D 几何推理）三个模块；2. 采用 Mixture-of-Transformer (MoT) 架构处理多任务专家；3. 设计了三阶段渐进式训练策略；4. 开发了自动数据增强流程以解决标注稀缺问题。

Result: 在仿真环境和真实世界的多样化操纵任务中均表现出优异的性能，证明了该方法在感知-动作映射中的准确性与鲁棒性。

Conclusion: AffordanceVLA 提出的结构化可供性预测（Affordance Forecasting）作为中间表征，有效弥合了视觉语义与机器人具身控制之间的鸿沟，是实现复杂指令执行任务的有效途径。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models leverage the rich world knowledge of pretrained vision-language models (VLMs) to enable instruction-following robotic manipulation. However, the structural mismatch between VLM semantic spaces and embodied control policies often hinders the learning of precise perception--action mappings. To address this challenge, we propose \textbf{AffordanceVLA}, a unified framework that introduces structured affordance forecasting as a task-oriented intermediate representation to establish a more precise and robust perception--action mapping. Specifically, we progressively model manipulation priors through three complementary components: 1) \textbf{Which2Act} for object-centric grounding via visual latent prediction to suppress distractions; 2) \textbf{Where2Act} for 2D interaction localization via affordance map estimation; and 3) \textbf{How2Act} for 3D geometric reasoning to guide manipulation policies. These affordance cues provide spatially grounded, semantically conditioned, and action-coupled intermediate representations, thereby naturally bridging vision, language and action. We integrate these modules into a Mixture-of-Transformer (MoT) architecture with specialized experts and train the model using a three-stage training strategy with a progressive data curriculum. To overcome the scarcity of dense affordance labels in robotic datasets, we also develop a robust automated data augmentation pipeline. Extensive experiments on simulation and real-world demonstrate that AffordanceVLA achieves strong performance across diverse manipulation scenarios.

</details>


### [190] [ActiveMimic: Egocentric Video Pretraining with Active Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06194)
*Xingyao Lin,Guojin Zhong,Tianyi Lu,Ziyi Ye,Yichen Zhu,Zuxuan Wu,Yu-Gang Jiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ActiveMimic 框架在自我中心视频中通过显式建模视点运动（主动视觉）而非将其视为噪声，显著缩小了视频预训练与机器人数据预训练之间的性能差距，为高效的机器人策略习得提供了新范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于自我中心视频的机器人预训练模型效果不如真实机器人数据，核心原因在于忽略了视频中人类通过主动调整视点来获取信息的“主动视觉”行为，该行为常被现有算法视为噪声。

Method: 提出 ActiveMimic 框架：1) 利用单目穿戴设备恢复相机与手腕的同步轨迹；2) 将相机运动建模为一种视点动作（viewpoint action）；3) 实现主动视觉与操作任务的联合学习。

Result: 在多种需要主动视觉的任务中表现优于现有的视频预训练基准，并达到甚至匹配了使用真实机器人数据预训练的 SOTA 模型效果。

Conclusion: 主动视觉（Active Perception）是解锁自我中心视角视频用于机器人预训练的关键，ActiveMimic 证明预训练阶段习得的主动观测能力可直接迁移并提升机器人的操作性能。

Abstract: Egocentric human video offers a scalable alternative to robot data for pretraining, yet models pretrained on such video consistently underperform those pretrained on robot data. We attribute this gap to a missing signal, the active perception behavior in egocentric videos, where humans continuously reposition their viewpoint during manipulation, inducing camera motion that standard pipelines treat as noise. To address this, we present ActiveMimic, a pretraining framework that recovers synchronized camera and wrist trajectories from a single body-worn RGB camera, models camera motion as a viewpoint action, and jointly learns active perception and manipulation from in-the-wild egocentric human video before adapting to a target robot. Empirically, real-world experiments across tasks with diverse active perception demands show that ActiveMimic consistently surpasses baselines pretrained on human video and matches state-of-the-art models pretrained on robot data. Further analysis provides evidence that active perception capability originates from egocentric human video pretraining rather than robot-specific fine-tuning, confirming active perception as the key to unlocking egocentric human video for robot pretraining.

</details>


### [191] [TAM: Torque Adaptation Module for Robust Motion Transfer in Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06218)
*Dongwon Son,Florian Shkurti,Jason Lee,Naman Shah,Beomjoon Kim,Dieter Fox*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了扭矩适应模块（TAM），通过在仿真中学习残差扭矩补偿，实现不同机器人实例间的策略无缝迁移，无需真实环境下的进一步训练即可提升动态操纵的零样本性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决机器人策略在不同实体、负载或动力学环境下表现不一致的问题，尤其是接触丰富型任务中微小动力学差异导致的失败；克服传统域随机化（保守）和系统辨识（需重采集数据）的缺陷。

Method: 引入扭矩适应模块（TAM）。TAM位于底层控制器和机器人接口之间，包含历史编码器（处理本体感觉历史）和扭矩适配器（输出残差扭矩补偿），仅依赖本体感觉，与具体策略和动作空间解耦。

Result: 在Franka Panda机器人上的实验表明，TAM在多项动态操纵任务（推送、翻转、平衡）中的零样本表现优于在线系统辨识和RMA基线。

Conclusion: TAM通过在仿真中进行多机器人预训练和针对性微调，实现了无需真实机器人数据的跨平台零样本控制适配，显著增强了机器人执行接触丰富型任务的鲁棒性。

Abstract: A policy tuned for one robot often behaves differently on another, whether due to the sim-to-real gap, unknown payloads, or the differing dynamics of two instances of the same robot. In contact-rich, dynamic manipulation, even small motion discrepancies can result in failure to track reference motion, since they disrupt the timing and modes of contact. Common remedies, such as domain randomization or system identification, either produce overly conservative task policies or require data that must be recollected for each robot or payload. We introduce the Torque Adaptation Module (TAM), a learned module that adapts the torque commands sent to the robot to match the behavior of an ideal robot. TAM operates between the low-level controller that tracks the policy's actions and the robot's torque interface. It includes a history encoder that embeds proprioceptive history into a latent state and a torque adaptor that computes residual torque corrections. Because TAM depends only on proprioceptive history and not on policy observations, or the action space, the same TAM weights can be reused to adapt policies with different action spaces (joint targets, end-effector targets, or direct torques). The policies themselves do not need to be trained with domain randomization of robot parameters. Instead, we offload the need for domain randomization to TAM by training it entirely in randomized simulation, using multi-robot pretraining followed by a robot-specific fine-tuning step that still requires no real-robot data. We evaluate TAM zero-shot on a real Franka Panda robot across dynamic manipulation tasks that include a vision-based box pushing policy (from RL), a flip policy (from BC), and an MPC ball-on-plate balancing. Our experiments show that TAM improves zero-shot real-robot execution compared to online system identification and RMA baselines and enables robust dynamic manipulation performance.

</details>


### [192] [CLEAR: Cognition and Latent Evaluation for Adaptive Routing in End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06219)
*Yining Xing,Zehong Ke,Zhiyuan Liu,Yanbo Jiang,Wenhao Yu,Jianqiang Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CLEAR框架通过用单步VAE漂移替换传统的迭代扩散过程，并结合Qwen驱动的语义感知调度，在保证实时性的前提下实现了高质量的多模态自动驾驶轨迹规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端自动驾驶模型在复杂多模态轨迹生成与实时推理约束之间难以取得平衡，现有扩散模型的迭代去噪过程导致延迟过高，无法满足安全关键场景的部署需求。

Method: 1. 使用Drive-JEPA作为视觉编码器；2. 将扩散模型的迭代去噪简化为VAE潜空间中的单步条件漂移（Conditional Drift）；3. 引入Qwen-3.5-0.8B进行驾驶场景语义理解，提取隐藏状态；4. 设计自适应调度器（Adaptive Scheduler）动态调整条件系数与采样方案，并通过交叉注意力评分选出最优轨迹。

Result: 在NAVSIM v1基准测试上，CLEAR取得了93.7的PDMS（Planning-based Driving Metric Score），达到当前行业最高水平。

Conclusion: CLEAR通过创新的单步生成规划与深层语义推理相结合，克服了扩散模型在自动驾驶中的延迟瓶颈，实现高效且高精度的多模态规划，证明了无需密集几何标注即可达到SOTA性能。

Abstract: End-to-end autonomous driving models often struggle to balance multi-modal maneuver generation with real-time inference constraints. While diffusion models successfully capture diverse driving behaviors, their iterative denoising process incurs unacceptable latency for safety-critical deployment. To address this, we propose CLEAR (Cognition and Latent Evaluation for Adaptive Routing), a framework that combines ultra-fast generative planning with deep semantic reasoning. CLEAR employs Drive-JEPA as the visual encoder and replaces the multi-step denoising chain with a single-step conditional drift in a VAE latent space, introducing a conditioning coefficient to balance diversity and expert precision. Meanwhile, we fully fine-tune Qwen~3.5~0.8B on driving QA pairs to extract scene-aware hidden states. These states guide both an Adaptive Scheduler, which selects the conditioning coefficient $α$ and sample count $N$ from a discrete set of predefined schemes, and a cross-attention scorer that selects the optimal trajectory from candidates. On the NAVSIM v1 benchmark, CLEAR achieves a state-of-the-art PDMS of 93.7. Our results demonstrate that high-fidelity, multi-modal planning can be executed efficiently without dense geometric annotations or iterative sampling.

</details>


### [193] [MPCoT: Reward-Guided Multi-Path Latent Reasoning for Test-Time Scalable Vision-Language-Action](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06245)
*Boyang Zhang,Lianlei Shan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对VLA模型推理能力不足的问题，提出MPCoT框架，通过无需额外输出token的多路径潜在推理和奖励导向训练，在不增加推理延迟的前提下，大幅提升了长程复杂控制任务的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型在面对长程和高不确定性任务时表现脆弱，且传统的思维链（CoT）方法存在推理延迟（token latency）以及文本-动作接口间接的问题。

Method: MPCoT（多路径潜在推理）：1. 初始化M个假设；2. 进行K步权重共享（weight-tied）的迭代细化；3. 采用软聚合机制处理潜在表示；4. 引入基于奖励的路径偏好训练目标，利用专家对齐、世界模型进度和成功反馈来优化潜在路径评分器。

Result: 在LIBERO和CALVIN基准测试中，MPCoT显著提高了长程任务的表现；实验验证了模型的深度（K）和广度（M）效应、置信度加权聚合以及奖励引导路径监督的有效性。

Conclusion: MPCoT通过多路径潜在推理有效增强了VLA策略在复杂长程任务中的表现，同时避免了显式思维链（CoT）造成的延迟，实现了推理深度与运行效率的平衡。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies remain brittle in long-horizon and high-uncertainty control, where one-pass action decoding provides limited inference-time deliberation. Explicit chain-of-thought can increase reasoning depth, but introduces token latency and an indirect text-to-action interface. We propose MPCoT, a reward-guided multi-path latent reasoning framework that initializes $M$ hypotheses, refines them for K weight-tied steps, and softly aggregates them before action decoding. A training-only path-preference objective evaluates candidate action branches with expert-action consistency, world-model/VLM-based progress, and success feedback to align the latent path scorer with downstream execution quality. MPCoT preserves the original 8-step action interface, generates zero reasoning tokens, and exposes configurable inference controls (K,M). Under matched protocols on LIBERO and CALVIN, MPCoT improves long-horizon performance, with ablations confirming depth-width effects, confidence-weighted aggregation, and reward-guided path supervision.

</details>


### [194] [Breaking Time: A Fully Gaussian Framework for Distributed and Continuous-Time SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06250)
*Davide Ceriola,Simone Ferrari,Luca Di Giammarino,Leonardo Brizi,Giorgio Grisetti*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: G-solver 是一种利用高斯信念传播和高斯过程进行连续时间 SLAM 的分布式框架，能够高效处理异构异步传感器数据并实现可扩展的轨迹估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决传统 SLAM 在处理异构、异步传感器（如卷帘快门相机、激光/雷达扫描仪、事件相机）时存在的轨迹估计不连续、同步复杂及计算可扩展性不足的问题。

Method: 提出 G-solver 框架，将高斯信念传播（GBP）的分布式消息传递特性与高斯过程（GP）的运动先验相结合，实现对连续时间轨迹的建模与概率估计，并支持多摄像头场景的无缝扩展。

Result: 通过合成和真实数据集验证，G-solver 在处理卷帘快门和多相机协同优化任务中表现出高精度与稳定性，且运行效率与现有的连续时间 SLAM 方法相当，同时具备良好的分布式计算能力。

Conclusion: G-solver 通过结合高斯信念传播（GBP）与高斯过程（GP），提供了一种高效、可扩展且去中心化的连续时间 SLAM 解决方案，不仅简化了多传感器融合（尤其是非均匀采样传感器），还保持了与现有先进方法相当的精度和计算效率。

Abstract: Continuous-time SLAM provides a principled framework for fusing heterogeneous sensors while estimating smooth trajectories, and is particularly well-suited for handling heterogeneous, asynchronous sensor streams with non-uniform readout patterns, such as rolling shutter cameras, LiDAR scanners, radar sweeps, or event-based sensors. In this work, we introduce G-solver, a fully Gaussian and distributed framework that combines Gaussian Belief Propagation (GBP) with Gaussian Process (GP) motion priors for continuous-time trajectory estimation. Our GP model provides a probabilistic representation of the trajectory, enabling consistent interpolation and the use of data-driven hyperparameters, while GBP offers a scalable message-passing formulation well-suited for decentralized settings. The resulting solver naturally extends to multi-camera scenarios without specialized synchronization or engineering effort. We evaluate the approach on synthetic and real data, including rolling shutter and distributed multi-camera optimization, demonstrating accurate and stable estimation with runtimes comparable to existing continuous-time methods. An open-source implementation is released.

</details>


### [195] [Multi-Resolution Tactile Imitation Learning for Contact-Rich Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06281)
*Rickmer Krohn,Erik Helmut,Niklas Funk,Jan Peters,Vignesh Prasad,Georgia Chalvatzaki*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 MiTaS 框架，通过融合不同时间分辨率的异构触觉传感器（GelSight 和 Evetac）与视觉数据，大幅提升了机器人对复杂接触式操作任务的控制精度与成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的接触式操作任务研究多集中在单一或同构触觉传感器上，缺乏对多种异构、且具有不同时间分辨率的触觉传感器进行融合利用的研究，导致模型在复杂任务中表现不足。

Method: 提出了一种名为 MiTaS 的表示框架，采用模态专用卷积主干（Convolutional Stems）处理不同数据，并利用基于 Transformer 的融合机制，整合了 RGB 相机、GelSight Mini（视觉触觉传感器）和 Evetac（事件驱动的高频触觉传感器）的数据，最后通过流匹配（Flow-matching）策略进行策略训练。

Result: 在五个接触式操作任务中，MiTaS 的平均成功率达到 80%，显著高于视觉基线（31%）和视觉-触觉基线（54%）。实验证明多分辨率触觉特征在模仿学习中具有互补性，且即使在测试阶段缺失高频传感器数据，模型依然能保持优越性能。

Conclusion: MiTaS 验证了多分辨率触觉感知在复杂接触式操作任务中的有效性，证明了融合不同时间分辨率的触觉模态能够显著提升模仿学习的性能，优于单一视觉或传统视觉-触觉模型。

Abstract: Touch sensing is beneficial for solving a wide variety of manipulation tasks. While there exists a wide range of tactile sensors with different properties, exploiting the fusion of multiple heterogeneous tactile sensors to improve manipulation learning remains underexplored. We present Multi-Resolution Tactile Sensing (MiTaS), a representation framework that leverages multiple tactile sensors operating at different temporal resolutions in order to solve complex contact-rich manipulation tasks. We propose a novel architecture using modality-specific convolutional stems and transformer-based fusion that effectively fuses information from an RGB camera stream, a vision-based GelSight Mini sensor and a high-frequency event-based Evetac sensor. This multi-sensor representation then conditions a flow-matching policy for solving downstream tasks. Experimental results across five contact-rich manipulation tasks demonstrate the effectiveness of multi-resolution tactile features in imitation learning. MiTaS achieves an average success rate of 80 %, while vision-only (31 %) and visual-tactile (54 %) baselines cannot solve the task reliably. Co-training a visuo-tactile model with multi-tactile data boosts performance by over 10 \% in certain tasks, without having access to the Evetac sensor during policy evaluation. A detailed sensor-reading and attention analysis reveals the importance of different sensors throughout task execution, validating our multi-resolution tactile sensing approach. Project Page: http://mitas-touch.github.io.

</details>


### [196] [Attitude-Aided Linear Calibration of Triaxial Accelerometers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06308)
*Yongqiang Yu,Tian Huang,Yipeng Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ALAC 是一种新型的 MEMS 加速度计校准方法，通过构建组合误差矩阵将复杂的误差建模简化为线性最小二乘问题，实现了高效、高精度的闭式解校准，且仅需少量测量数据即可实现离线或在线校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的三轴 MEMS 加速度计校准方法往往依赖昂贵的参考设备，或使用复杂的非线性迭代优化，导致计算效率低下，难以应用于低成本平台或需要自校准的场景。

Method: 提出了一种名为“姿态辅助线性加速度计校准 (ALAC)”的方法。该方法通过构建组合误差矩阵 (CEM) 将传感器误差建模在统一框架下，利用线性最小二乘法进行求解。在静态重力条件下，将其转化为约束齐次最小二乘 (CHLS) 问题并通过闭式解求解；仅需五个任意方向的测量即可完成校准，并支持递归在线扩展。

Result: 实验表明，ALAC 在离线和在线模式下，其精度和噪声鲁棒性均优于现有的参考基准和在线基准。特别是在原始测量数据上，其表现超过了所有被测评估对象，并与过滤条件下的迭代式自校准效果相当。

Conclusion: ALAC 是一种鲁棒且高效的 MEMS 加速度计校准方案，特别适用于低成本 IMU 和在线环境。它通过线性化处理消除了对复杂非线性优化的依赖，在保证精度的同时显著提升了实用性。

Abstract: Triaxial MEMS accelerometers are widely used for inertial sensing, navigation, and sensor fusion, but existing calibration methods often rely on costly reference setups or nonlinear iterative optimization, limiting their efficiency and applicability to low-cost or self-calibrating systems. We present attitude-aided linear accelerometer calibration (ALAC), a method that operates on any platform providing orientation information, such as turntables, robotic arms, or inertial measurement units. ALAC constructs a combined error matrix (CEM) to represent sensor errors in a unified calibration model and enables linear least-squares estimation. The bias and gravity vector are jointly estimated, implicitly accounting for platform misalignment, and matrix decomposition of the CEM recovers scale, non-orthogonality, and alignment rotation parameters. Under static gravity, calibration is formulated as a constrained homogeneous least-squares (CHLS) problem and solved in closed form using standard linear algebra. Only five arbitrarily oriented measurements are required, and a recursive extension supports online or in-field calibration. Experiments on a stationary robot-mounted accelerometer and a quasi-static public IMU trajectory show that ALAC, in both offline and online modes, outperforms reference-based and online baselines in accuracy and robustness to sensor noise. On the same dataset, it matches iterative self-calibration under filtered conditions and surpasses all evaluated baselines on raw measurements. These results demonstrate a robust and practical calibration scheme for MEMS-based inertial platforms, especially low-cost IMUs and online calibration scenarios.

</details>


### [197] [Meridian: Metric-Semantic Primitive Matching for Cross-View Geo-Localization Beyond Urban Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06312)
*Mason Peterson,Qingyuan Li,Yixuan Jia,Fernando Cladera,Carlos Nieto-Granda,Camillo Jose Taylor,Jonathan P. How*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了Meridian，一种基于度量-语义原语匹配的跨视角定位算法，无需针对特定区域进行训练或微调，即可在复杂多变的自然环境中实现高精度全球定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于航空影像的定位方法主要针对结构化城市环境，在处理非结构化自然地形、缺乏通用性以及对特定环境训练的过度依赖方面存在局限。

Method: 提出了一种基于度量-语义原语（metric-semantic primitives）匹配的定位方法，结合一致性度量来估计机器人子图位姿分布，并通过鲁棒的位姿图优化过滤异常值，从而实现轨迹估计。

Result: 在包含自动驾驶数据集、公园、校园及野外营地等多种环境中进行了验证，总计19公里行程中，平均位姿优化轨迹误差为2.4米。

Conclusion: Meridian 通过匹配空中与地面感知的度量-语义原语，实现了在无需特定领域训练的情况下，在多样化环境中进行稳健、高精度的全球定位。

Abstract: Successful robot automation requires accurate global localization to support repeatability, task planning, goal specification, and safe operation. However, reliable localization in GNSS-denied environments remains an open problem. Overhead aerial imagery offers a promising solution, but existing approaches primarily target structured urban environments and have been rarely demonstrated in unstructured natural terrain. Limitations of the state-of-the-art include a reliance on models trained for specific environments, as well as difficulty handling repetitive geometries and featureless landscapes commonly found in natural outdoor areas. To overcome these challenges, we present Meridian, a method for matching high-level metric-semantic primitives across aerial images and ground robot RGB-D camera data that achieves accurate global localization and generalizes well across diverse environments, all without any training or algorithmic fine-tuning on area-specific data. We formulate novel consistency metrics to estimate a distribution over robot submap poses and to reject outlier hypotheses in a robust pose graph optimization step for accurate robot trajectory estimation. We demonstrate that our algorithm can localize a ground robot across a wide variety of environments, including an autonomous driving dataset, a park and campus area, and a wilderness camp, with an average optimized trajectory error of 2.4 m over 19 km of ground traversal.

</details>


### [198] [VOLT: Vision and Language Trajectory Segmentation for Faster-than-Demonstration Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06323)
*Robert Ramirez Sanchez,Daniel J. Evans,Dylan P. Losey,Siddarth Jain*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对机器人学中“从慢速人类演示中学习高速执行策略”的问题，本文提出了 VOLT 模型，通过多模态（视觉+语言）分割技术自适应平衡任务的加速需求与精细操作需求，有效解决了现有降采样带来的鲁棒性下降问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实应用中，人类演示速度通常较慢，而机器人需要以远快于人类的速度高效执行任务；简单地对全轨迹进行均匀降采样会导致关键操作失败，因此需要一种能区分动作类型并自适应调整速度的技术。

Method: 提出了一种名为 VOLT (Vision-and-Language Trajectory segmentation) 的方法，利用视觉和语言线索对视频演示进行语义分割，自动识别出需要精细操作的片段（保留原速）和非约束性运动片段（进行选择性降采样加速），最后结合扩散策略 (Diffusion Policies) 进行训练。

Result: 实验表明，相比于状态领先的基准方法，VOLT 能够有效识别可加速的片段，使得机器人在显著提高任务执行速度的同时，仍能保持高水平的任务成功率。

Conclusion: 分割质量对实现高效机器人策略至关重要；VOLT 通过智能判断任务阶段（决定何时加速、何时谨慎），在提升执行速度的同时保持了任务执行的稳健性和高成功率。

Abstract: Humans often take longer to demonstrate a task than a robot would need to execute it. Rather than learning to replicate the demonstration at the same pace, many industrial and practical applications require robots to perform tasks as quickly as possible. In this paper, we investigate several hypotheses for learning policies that operate faster-than-demonstrations. Our experiments show that the most effective strategy is to downsample recorded demonstrations and train the robot's policy on this accelerated data. However, uniformly downsampling an entire trajectory can be problematic. Some parts of a task can be safely sped up (e.g., unconstrained motion), while others demand slower, more precise motion (e.g., object interactions or fine manipulation). To address this challenge, we introduce VOLT, a vision-and-language trajectory segmentation method that reasons over video demonstrations, and leverages contextual cues to determine when acceleration is appropriate and when careful precision is required. VOLT identifies segments where slow, deliberate motion is necessary, then selectively downsamples the remaining segments. The resulting reformatted trajectories can be used with standard imitation learning approaches, such as diffusion policies. Our results highlight that segmentation quality is critical -- baseline methods often misidentify when acceleration is possible, leading to overly cautious or unreliable policies. Compared to state-of-the-art alternatives, VOLT allows robots to execute tasks faster while maintaining strong performance.

</details>


### [199] [Waypoints Matter: A Systematic Study for Sampling-Based Trajectory Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06366)
*Josep M. Barbera,Antonio Artuñedo,Jorge Villagra*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文揭示了路径点间距(spacing)是采样规划器性能的核心驱动力，证明了简单调优的均匀采样策略优于或等同于更复杂的几何适配策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 路径点的放置位置直接影响自动驾驶轨迹规划的可行性与质量，但目前学术界对于不同放置策略如何影响规划器性能缺乏深入研究。

Method: 在保持轨迹原语和采样候选预算固定的前提下，对三种路径点放置策略（均匀间距、RDP*变体、曲率条件分配）进行了系统性对比实验。实验涵盖了449种配置，并在5种不同几何复杂度的CommonRoad地图上进行了验证。

Result: 实验表明路径点间距($d_s$)是决定性能的主要变量。均匀采样在适当间距下表现与复杂策略持平甚至更好；曲率感知对策仅在高度复杂的路径上具有轻微优势，且RDP*在各项测试中均表现不佳。

Conclusion: 路径点间距($d_s$)是影响采样轨迹规划器性能的关键参数。均匀采样在调优后表现优异，而复杂的几何感知策略（如RDP*）在多数情况下并未体现出优势，仅在几何复杂度极高的路段，曲率感知策略才具有边缘优势。建议将$d_s$作为核心调优参数。

Abstract: Real-time autonomous driving commonly relies on sampling-based trajectory planners that link candidate trajectories to target waypoints along the road centerline. The placement of these waypoints directly impacts both the existence and quality of feasible trajectories. Yet, its effect on planner performance remains largely unexplored. In this paper, we treat waypoint placement as a first-class design variable. We hold the trajectory primitive and candidate budget fixed, and systematically sweep three placement strategies (uniform spacing, an augmented Ramer-Douglas-Peucker variant (RDP*), and a novel curvature-conditioned allocation) across 449 configurations and five CommonRoad maps of increasing geometric complexity. Our results show that the nominal inter-waypoint spacing $d_s$ is the primary performance driver, with large differences in planner reliability attributed to placement alone. Uniform sampling at a well-tuned spacing matches or surpasses both RDP* and the centered curvature variant. The curvature variant offers a small but consistent advantage on geometrically complex roads under reliability-first and balanced weightings, while RDP* never outperforms uniform sampling. These findings suggest that $d_s$ should be treated as the dominant tuning parameter, with geometry-aware strategies reserved for curvature-rich corridors where feasibility is the limiting factor.

</details>


### [200] [Ensuring Interaction Safety in Multitask Exoskeleton Control: A Simulation-Trained Variable Impedance Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06370)
*Muyuan Ma,Houcheng Li,Haotian Zhai,Lijun Han,Xinpan Meng,Xiuze Xia,Long Cheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于仿真训练与Lyapunov稳定约束的变阻抗控制框架，通过双模态策略实现外骨骼的多任务适应性控制，并在保证安全的前提下增强了外骨骼的辅助效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的可穿戴外骨骼在处理多任务时的适应性与人机交互安全性之间存在矛盾，缺乏一种既能跨任务适应又能确保系统稳定性的控制方法。

Method: 1. 建立了基于仿真的人机运动数据生成流程，利用PPO算法合成人体肌肉激活数据及外骨骼补偿力矩；2. 训练了一种融合语义指令与本体感受历史的双模态策略，用于预测参考轨迹与变阻抗增益；3. 引入基于Lyapunov稳定性理论的约束机制，对刚度变化进行实时界定以确保系统的渐近稳定性。

Result: 实验结果表明，该框架在多种运动任务中表现良好，且在实际场景中相较于标准基准方法，更有效地降低了人体的代谢成本。

Conclusion: 本研究提出的基于仿真训练的变阻抗控制框架，结合了Lyapunov稳定性约束与多模态策略，在保证人机交互安全性的前提下，能够有效降低使用者在多种复杂任务中的代谢成本，验证了该方案在多任务外骨骼控制中的可行性。

Abstract: Wearable exoskeletons can augment human phys ical capabilities during complex activities. However, ensuring adaptation across diverse tasks while guaranteeing interaction safety remains a critical challenge. To address this, a simulation trained variable impedance control approach with stability guarantees is proposed. First, a simulation-based human exoskeleton motion data generation pipeline is established, utilizing Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) to synthesize human muscle activations while the exoskeleton provides direct compensation for human biological joint torques. Subsequently, the generated dataset is used to train a dual modality policy that fuses semantic instructions with proprioceptive history, enabling the prediction of reference trajectories and variable impedance gains for nine different motion tasks. To guarantee safety, the network outputs are constrained by a stability criterion derived from Lyapunov stability theory, which bounds stiffness variations to ensure the asymptotic stability of the coupled system. Experimental results indicate that the proposed framework reduces metabolic cost in real-world scenarios com pared with standard baseline methods. These findings suggest the feasibility of the proposed framework for safe, multitask exoskeleton control.

</details>


### [201] [RiskFlow: Fast and Faithful Safety-Critical Traffic Scenario Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06423)
*Qi Lan,Yining Tang,Yu Shen,Yi Zhou,Yuhao Wei,Jie Li,Guofa Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RiskFlow 是一种针对自动驾驶的高效安全关键场景生成框架，它抛弃了缓慢的迭代去噪过程，通过单次前向传递的速度场学习实现快速轨迹生成，在提升物理真实性的同时有效提高了评估效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于扩散模型的自动驾驶场景生成方法存在计算成本过高、长轨迹生成过程中误差累积导致的伪影（如抖动、异常加速度、脱离车道）等问题，难以满足高风险、多智能体场景的需求。

Method: 1. 将未来轨迹生成定义为动作空间（加速度和横摆角速度）中的传输问题；2. 摒弃迭代去噪，通过学习有限区间内的平均速度场，实现单次前向传导生成；3. 采用基于雅可比向量积（JVP）的目标函数进行训练；4. 在推理阶段引入输出空间引导（output-space guidance），在确保生成物理可行性的同时实现风险引导。

Result: 在 nuScenes 数据集与 tbsim 评估框架下，RiskFlow 在保持竞争力的安全关键场景生成能力的同时，显著提升了生成轨迹的逼真度，并大幅降低了推理时间。

Conclusion: RiskFlow 通过将 trajectory 生成转化为动作空间中的传输问题，成功解决了扩散模型在长序列生成中的计算开销与不稳定性问题，实现了生成效率、真实性与对抗性（风险 criticality）之间的最优平衡。

Abstract: Safety-critical traffic scenario generation is essential for evaluating autonomous driving systems under rare but high-risk interactions. Existing diffusion-based methods offer strong controllability in closed-loop generation, but their iterative denoising process is computationally expensive and may accumulate sampling and guidance errors over long rollouts, causing unrealistic motion artifacts such as jitter, abnormal acceleration, and off-road behavior. To address these issues, we propose RiskFlow, a closed-loop safety-critical multi-agent traffic generation framework that formulates future trajectory generation as transport in the action space. Instead of relying on iterative denoising, RiskFlow learns an average velocity field over a finite interval to transform Gaussian action sequences into future acceleration and yaw-rate commands with a single forward pass, using a JVP-based objective for efficient and stable training. At test time, RiskFlow applies output-space guidance to the generated actions, steering selected critical agents toward risky interactions while regularizing off-road behavior, and reconstructs physically feasible trajectories through vehicle dynamics. Experiments on nuScenes with tbsim closed-loop evaluation show that RiskFlow achieves a strong adversariality-realism trade-off across multi-agent and long-horizon settings. Compared with representative baselines, RiskFlow consistently improves realism while maintaining competitive safety-critical generation capability, and substantially reduces inference time for evaluation.

</details>


### [202] [Flow-based Policy Adaptation without Policy Updates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06461)
*Luzhe Sun,Jingtian Ji,Haoran Chen,Jiawei Zhou,Matthew R. Walter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GLOVES 是一种轻量级共享控制方法，通过基于流的模型学习专家动作分布，选择性地对机器人的次优或异常动作进行实时修正，从而在提升任务成功率的同时保留了智能体的原始意图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从头开始训练机器人技能成本高昂，而利用预训练模型或人类操作等先验知识往往伴随着动作次优、噪声干扰或与专家行为不一致的问题。需要一种既能纠偏又不完全取代智能体主权的方案。

Method: 提出了一种基于流（flow-based）的动作适应方法。通过学习从非专家动作分布到专家动作分布的传输流，当智能体的输出被识别为异常或分布外（OOD）操作时，利用该流进行修正；反之，对符合专家分布的行为则保持不变（干预门控机制）。

Result: GLOVES 在仅需少量专家示范数据的情况下，有效提升了任务成功率，实现了对机器人动作的实时、精准、选择性适应。

Conclusion: GLOVES 提供了一种轻量级的共享控制模块，通过仅在必要时介入进行动作修正，有效地实现了辅助机器人执行任务，同时保留了智能体的原始意图，具有极强的跨任务和跨环境鲁棒性。

Abstract: Leveraging prior knowledge from pretrained policies, foundation models, or human operators offers an efficient alternative to learning robot skills from scratch. However, these agents often provide actions that are suboptimal, noisy, or misaligned with task-specific expert behavior. We propose GLOVES, a family of flow-based adaptation methods that correct non-expert actions by transporting them toward an expert action distribution. Rather than replacing agentic control with full autonomy, GLOVES performs selective action-level adaptation, improving task success while preserving agent intent. The learned flow also provides a natural in-distribution scoring mechanism through reverse flow evaluation. We use this signal as an intervention gate: actions that appear consistent with the expert distribution are passed through unchanged, while anomalous or out-of-distribution (OOD) actions are corrected. In this way, assistance is only provided when necessary. GLOVES requires only limited expert supervision, using a small number of demonstrations or reusable successful skill segments. By learning local expert action patterns and stitching them during execution, GLOVES provides a lightweight shared-control module for robust action adaptation across tasks and environments. Code and demos are available at ripl.github.io/GLOVES_web.

</details>


### [203] [TempoVLA: Learning Speed-Controllable Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06491)
*Dong Jing,Jingchen Nie,Tianqi Zhang,Jiaqi Liu,Huaxiu Yao,Zhiwu Lu,Mingyu Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TempoVLA提出了一种支持动态变速的VLA模型，通过轨迹增强（VSTA）和速度条件输入，让机器人能够根据环境风险自主调整执行速度（快进慢切）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型在训练后仅能维持固定执行速度，无法根据任务需求在低风险阶段加速或高风险接触阶段减速，缺乏对机器人执行过程中的速度动态控制。

Method: 1. 变量速度轨迹增强（VSTA）：通过重采样和处理训练数据，将演示轨迹转换为不同速度的样本。
2. 速度调节建模：在VLA模型中引入明确的速度控制条件输入。
3. 动态控制：结合大模型（LMM）实时感知环境风险，实现执行过程中的自适应变速。

Result: 实验表明，TempoVLA能够精确实现目标速度控制；VSTA技术通过增强数据利用率提升了模型基础性能；在大模型协作下，机器人能够根据任务风险动态调节速度，且保持了极低的运动误差。

Conclusion: TempoVLA通过引入速度条件机制，实现了在单一VLA模型中对机器人执行速度的灵活直接控制，克服了现有模型仅能以固定速度运行的局限性。

Abstract: Robot manipulation alternates between low-risk transit phases that call for fast execution and high-risk contact stages that demand slow, precise motion. Yet existing Vision-Language-Action models (VLAs) only inherit a single fixed speed from training demonstrations. Prior efforts to accelerate VLAs through model compression, KV-cache reuse, or reinforcement learning only shift the policy from one fixed speed to another, and leave deceleration almost unexplored. We observe that the magnitude of each predicted action already governs how fast the robot moves, opening a direct route to controllable execution speed. We turn this observation into TempoVLA, a single VLA whose execution speed is controlled by an explicit condition. TempoVLA combines two coupled components. (1) A data-side Variable-Speed Trajectory Augmentation (VSTA) that re-times demonstration to any target speed by merging or splitting actions while preserving its motion semantics. (2) A model-side conditioning mechanism that feeds the speed to the policy. Statistics show that VSTA reaches the requested speed with negligible motion error. Experiments in simulation and on real-world tasks demonstrate that TempoVLA achieves flexible speed control in both directions, while VSTA additionally boosts the default $1\times$ performance via better data utilization. Furthermore, by cooperating with a large multimodal model, TempoVLA realizes dynamic speed control, accelerating through low-risk phases and decelerating for high-risk ones.

</details>


### [204] [HANDOFF: Humanoid Agentic Task-Space Whole-Body Control via Distilled Complementary Teachers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06493)
*Lizhi Yang,Junheng Li,Nehar Poddar,Yiling Hou,Gio Huh,Robert Griffin,Georgia Gkioxari,Aaron Ames*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了HANDOFF，这是一个通过混合专家（MoE）蒸馏技术训练的人形机器人全身控制框架，引入了简单、通用且模块化的命令空间，解决了任务规划与控制之间的语义鸿沟，实现了无需微调即可通过自然语言指令完成多种现实操作任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人形机器人全身控制器需要密集的运动学或空间参考，这导致任务规划器难以将高层任务语义转化为具体的控制指令，限制了机器人处理复杂、多样化操作任务的能力。

Method: 提出了一种名为HANDOFF的全身控制器框架，采用多教师KL蒸馏（multi-teacher KL distillation）技术，通过上下文条件门控机制将运动追踪、位移和摔倒恢复三个领域的专家知识整合进混合专家（MoE）结构中，并构建了一个与任务规划器对接的紧凑命令空间。

Result: 在Unitree G1机器人上实现了与当前最先进水平相当的速度追踪性能，并拥有极大的鲁棒操作工作空间；在无需特定任务数据或控制微调的前提下，成功实现了基于自然语言的任务执行。

Conclusion: 通过引入一种紧凑、直观且通用的指令空间接口（HANDOFF），实现了人形机器人任务规划与全身控制的高效解耦，证明了在无需任务特定微调的情况下，通过由VLM驱动的代理规划器，即可实现多样的现实世界操作任务。

Abstract: For a humanoid robot to be deployed in the real world, the choice of command space (i.e., the interface between task planning and whole-body control) is crucial. Existing whole-body controllers typically demand dense kinematic or spatial references that planners struggle to synthesize from task semantics. We instead propose a compact, explicit interface that is intuitive, general, modular, and expressive enough for diverse manipulation skills. To this end, we introduce HANDOFF, a single humanoid whole-body controller that follows this interface and is distilled via multi-teacher KL distillation under a context-conditioned gating scheme into a mixture-of-experts student from three complementary specialists: whole-body motion tracking with safety-filtered data, locomotion, and fall-recovery. On the Unitree G1, HANDOFF matches state-of-the-art velocity tracking and offers one of the largest robust manipulation workspaces. We further demonstrate hardware feasibility through multiple natural-language-driven task roll-outs, powered by a VLM-driven agentic planner with no task-specific data or controller fine-tuning.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [205] [The Evaluation Blind Spot: A Stereological Theory of Benchmark Coverage for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05169)
*Jason Z Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文通过立体学构建了LLM基准评估的理论框架，证明现有榜单因有效维度过低存在巨大“结构性盲点”，导致排名极度脆弱且不稳定。研究提出可通过少量核心基准实现高效评估，并从理论上解决了Gardner 1.5问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM基准套件在评估模型真实能力时存在盲点和不稳定性，缺乏对Benchmark系统性覆盖范围及评价维度的理论支撑。

Method: 提出了一套基于立体学（Stereology）的LLM基准覆盖率理论，利用豪斯多夫距离（Hausdorff distance）界定能力分布的不可区分性，通过卡方投影模型进行模拟，并采用次模贪心算法寻找稳定基准核心。

Result: 实证表明主流榜单的有效维度较低（2.86-4.80），结构性盲点极大；模型排名极不稳定（top-1易位率达92%）；此外，通过算法确定了只需7个基准即可覆盖90%的有效信息，验证了其理论预测的有效性。

Conclusion: LLM评测不仅受统计噪声影响，更受限于低维效应（有效维度低）导致的“结构性盲点”，这远超单纯的分数波动；此外，通过最优恢复理论解决了Gardner 1.5问题，为Benchmark的有效性评估提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We give a stereological theory of LLM benchmark coverage. For any suite with effective dimensionality d_eff, the visible Hausdorff distance between two convex capability profiles consistent with the same scores is bounded by epsilon + C R m^(-1/(d_eff-1)), with matching Lipschitz lower bound. Empirically, three independent leaderboards (Open LLM v2, an extended 12-benchmark suite, LiveBench) all have d_eff in [2.86, 4.80] on their competitive frontier; the structural blind spot exceeds the observed runner-up score gap by two orders of magnitude and dominates statistical noise by 52-127x. Under a chi-squared projection model, the isotropic prior is the optimistic case; across six hidden-capability priors and four ambient dimensions the simulated half-split swap rate of the top two models stays in [0.38, 0.49], and a 500-trial random visible/held-out split shows that 92% of trials swap the top-1 ranking with on average 2.83 of 5 top-5 models changing. A submodular greedy algorithm with the Nemhauser (1 - 1/e) guarantee finds a stable core of 4 benchmarks; 7 of 12 suffice for 90% coverage, and the trained subset transfers across temporal quarters with 93-97% retention. A counterfactual validation across 12 internal benchmarks and 27 Chatbot Arena categories confirms that the eigenstructure predicts which evaluations are irreplaceable (rho = -0.69, p = 0.013 for removal disruption) and which external evaluations bring new information (rho = +0.38). As a second, independent theoretical contribution, we resolve Gardner's Problem 1.5 (1995) for C^2 support functions, establishing the minimax rate Theta(R/(kappa m^(2/(D-1)))) in general dimension via optimal recovery theory on S^(D-1).

</details>


### [206] [ERRORQUAKE: Heavy-Tailed Error Severity Distributions in Open-Weight Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05170)
*Jason Z Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 现有的 LLM 幻觉基准测试忽视了错误严重程度的差异；本文引入 Errorquake-10k 评估框架，证明了在准确率相同时，模型展现出的错误严重程度分布具有本质不同且不可约，必须将严重程度分布作为评估 LLM 质量的核心指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 LLM 幻觉基准测试倾向于将所有错误视为等价的，忽略了错误的严重程度差异（例如简单的日期错误与虚假事实的量级差异是完全不同的）。即使在准确率相同的情况下，不同模型产生严重错误倾向的分布模式也存在显著差异，导致现有的标量评价体系无法完全反映模型能力。

Method: 1. 构建 Errorquake-10k 基准测试：包含 10,000 个查询，跨越 8 个领域和 5 个难度级别，采用 0-4 分的连续严重程度评分量表。
2. 统计建模：为 21 个开放权重模型拟合严重程度分布指数（b，基于 Gutenberg-Richter 幂律分布的尾部斜率）。
3. 验证方法：进行大规模人类评审验证（ICC=0.85），并进行 LLM 判别器校准。
4. 理论分析：证明“非可约性定理”（Non-Reducibility Theorem），量化严重程度分布与错误率之间的信息非冗余性。
5. 错误归因分类：构建严重机制分类法（kappa=0.83）分析错误类型随严重程度的变化。

Result: 1. 统计规律：在 210 对模型比较中，40%（85 对）在准确率一致的情况下，严重程度分布存在显著统计学差异。
2. 非冗余性：64.5% 的模型严重程度分布差异无法由准确率解释（I(b; model | epsilon) = 1.56 bits）。
3. 动态特性：错误性质随严重程度发生类别跃迁：低严重程度主要是错误的检索（71%），而高严重程度主要是幻觉虚构（39%），且此模式受模型规模影响。

Conclusion: 在评估大语言模型时，仅报告准确率（标量误差率）是不够的。严重程度分布（Severity Distribution）提供了关于模型错误行为的独立、关键的信息。未来应将严重程度分布作为评价 LLM 性能的标准指标。

Abstract: At matched accuracy, open-weight LLMs differ substantially in the shape of their error severity distribution -- a difference invisible to the scalar error rate. Hallucination benchmarks report a single error count and treat all errors as equivalent, yet a wrong date and a fabricated court ruling differ by orders of magnitude. We introduce Errorquake-10k, a 10,000-query benchmark scoring each response on a continuous 0-4 severity scale across 8 domains and 5 difficulty tiers, and we fit per-model severity distributions for 21 open-weight models. For each model we estimate a severity distribution index (b, the Gutenberg-Richter upper-tail slope) with 95% bootstrap confidence intervals. Headline: across the 210 model pairs, 85 have disjoint 95% b confidence intervals at matched accuracy (|Delta epsilon| < 0.05) on human-consensus scoring, e.g. deepseek-v3.2 vs. ministral-14b at epsilon = 0.586 and Delta b = 0.47. A 519-item three-rater human validation study confirms measurement reliability (ICC(2,k=3) = 0.85), validates the LLM-judge ranking (rho = 0.89), and confirms the dense-model scaling correlation on human data (rho_s = -0.86). We prove a Non-Reducibility Theorem showing that severity profile and error rate are informationally non-redundant (I(b; model | epsilon) = 1.56 bits; 64.5% of cross-model b variance is unexplained by epsilon). A severity mechanism taxonomy (kappa = 0.83) reveals that error type shifts categorically with severity: low-severity errors are retrievals (71%); high-severity errors are fabrications (39%) -- and this composition differs by model size (p < 0.0001). Severity distribution should be reported alongside accuracy; it carries discriminative information that the error rate cannot.

</details>


### [207] [Staged Factorial Screening for Budget-Constrained Micro-Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05186)
*Felipe Chavarro Polania*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究了在预算受限的微预训练中，如何通过受限实验设计（fractional-factorial）高效筛选候选方案；结果表明短周期筛选配合桥接模型策略对后续长周期训练具有统计学意义上的指导价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在预算受限的微预训练（micro-pretraining）场景下，如何在投入大量计算资源前，通过共享加速器筛选众多的模型及配方候选方案，并确保这些早期实验结构稳定且具有代表性。

Method: 利用受限的自动化研究（autoresearch）训练循环，进行了613组实验，涵盖：不同时间预算（2, 5, 10分钟）的分阶段因子筛选实验；全条件种子重跑；定向锚点检查；贪婪与随机基准对比；60分钟桥接模型评估；以及持续至24小时的跨硬件（Windows A100/Linux L40S）锚点延续验证。应用了Benjamini-Hochberg校正来处理多次假设检验。

Result: 模型训练的惩罚（如批次、深度、宽度）在短预算下影响最大，随预算增加而放缓。在特定筛选下，部分因子效果具有统计显著性。随机搜索虽能找到较优解但缺乏因子归因。跨硬件的长周期测试表明，桥接模型表现最优，且排序结果对硬件敏感，并不存在通用的最优排序。

Conclusion: 研究建议采用一种分阶段的受限方法：利用短时间的实验设计（fractional-factorial）筛选出高惩罚方向，在重复运行中确认有潜力的锚点，并在缩小后的空间内进行局部精炼。最终支持采用“以桥接模型（bridge model）为中心”的策略，而非追求硬件无关的排序或通用的超参数优化方案。

Abstract: Budget-constrained micro-pretraining often requires triaging many candidate recipes on a shared accelerator before larger search budgets are spent. We study whether a staged fractional-factorial workflow can recover stable early effect structure in this setting. On a fixed autoresearch-derived single-GPU training loop, we run 613 experiments across pilot and follow-up screens at 2, 5, and 10 minutes; full 16-condition seeded reruns at 5 and 10 minutes; targeted seeded anchor checks; same-host greedy and matched-cost random baselines; a 60-minute bridge package; and bounded Windows A100 and Linux L40S anchor continuations through 24 hours. Main penalties from total batch, depth, and width are largest at short budgets and relax as budget increases. Within the predeclared seeded full-screen families, D, A, B, and C retain non-zero estimates at 5 and 10 minutes after within-budget Benjamini-Hochberg correction, while E does not. Random search can reach strong incumbents in this 32-condition space, but repeatedly in the same low-penalty region and without factor attribution. The 60-minute bridge anchor has the lowest mean, although that package does not separate workflow refinement from the larger bridge model's capacity advantage. In bounded 12-hour and 24-hour three-anchor continuations on both hosts, the bridge has the lowest sample mean while the non-bridge ordering stays host-sensitive. We therefore present a bounded methods result: use short designed screens to identify high-penalty directions, confirm promising anchors under repeated runs, and refine locally inside the reduced space. The evidence supports a bridge-centered recommendation through 24 hours on two hosts, not hardware-invariant ranking or general hyperparameter-optimization superiority.

</details>


### [208] [PyCC.id: A package for hypothesis-driven equation discovery with structural identifiability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05191)
*Federico J. Gonzalez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了 Python 库 PyCC，这是一个基于特征曲线（CCs）的方程发现工具，通过结合领域先验和结构化“骨架”，解决了传统数据驱动逆问题中模型病态和人工筛选繁琐的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据驱动的方程发现本质上是一个病态逆问题，往往产生多个拟合效果相似的候选模型。现有的约束方法仍会导致候选模型过多，依赖人工后期筛选，且缺乏良好的结构可辨识性保证。

Method: 提出并实现了一个基于“特征曲线（CCs）”的假设驱动型框架，该框架支持多种方程发现范式（如神经网络、符号回归和稀疏回归），并允许用户通过定义“骨架（skeletons）”和引入领域先验来迭代发现 ODE。

Result: 开发了 Python 库 PyCC，该工具使研究人员能够灵活地定义系统“骨架”和假设，从而高效、稳健地从时间序列数据中发掘常微分方程（ODEs）。

Conclusion: PyCC 作为一套开源 Python 库，为基于特征曲线（CCs）的方程发现提供了一个模块化、易用的框架，有效简化了从时间序列数据中发现微分方程的过程，并提高了模型识别的结构化程度。

Abstract: Data-driven equation discovery is fundamentally an inverse problem that seeks to infer the governing differential equations of a system directly from time-series measurements. A known issue is the ill-conditioned nature of the inverse problem, which frequently produces multiple mathematical models that fit the data similarly well. One path to address this issue is by incorporating known hypotheses and constraints into the training phase beforehand. While this approach effectively reduces the search space, it still results in multiple candidate models, forcing practitioners to rely on post-hoc manual filtering based on their own domain expertise. A recent approach incorporates structural `skeletons' inspired by characteristic curves (CCs), defining a hypothesis-driven methodology. In this methodology, practitioners define a skeleton, which is associated with a family of ordinary differential equations (ODEs), and then add their hypotheses and priors based on their domain knowledge to refine the obtained model iteratively. An important advantage of this approach is that some skeletons have demonstrable structural identifiability properties, which are useful for checking whether the skeleton is correct or should be discarded. Furthermore, this formalism enables the use of multiple equation discovery paradigms due to its modularity (such as neural networks, symbolic regression, and sparse regression). In this work, we present the Python library PyCC, which condenses these efforts into a flexible tool that allows researchers and engineers to seamlessly define their skeletons and hypotheses to discover ODEs from time-dependent data.

</details>


### [209] [Temporal Preference Concepts and their Functions in a Large Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05194)
*Ian Rios-Sialer,Shantanu Darveshi,Shuai Jiang,Avigya Paudel,Anastasiia Pronina,Ipshita Bandyopadhyay,Justin Shenk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探索了LLM如何进行跨期决策，通过机械可解释性方法定位了其中的时间偏好机制，并证实了可以通过控制向量对模型受时间影响的决策行为进行调节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型（LLM）被越来越多地用于处理涉及短期利益与长期后果权衡的决策任务，目前尚不清楚LLM内部是如何表示和解决这些权衡的。因此，研究需要揭示这一隐蔽的内部机制，以实现对模型规划和推理的可靠控制。

Method: 1. 因果定位法：在蒸馏版LLM（Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507）中通过梯度归因和激活修补定位时间偏好相关子图；2. 几何分析：在残差流（residual stream）中分析时间跨度的编码方式；3. 行为分析：对比分析LLM与人类的时间折现率（temporal discounting）；4. 干预实验：通过构建控制向量（steering vectors）尝试调节模型的时间偏好。

Result: 1. 成功定位了模型中负责时间偏好的关键子图（主要分布在中上层）；2. 发现LLM的残差流确实编码了时间跨度的几何结构；3. LLM在处理时间折现时比人类更“有耐心”（折扣更陡峭程度较低），但该偏好受上下文影响较大且不稳定；4. 实验表明可以通过控制向量对模型的时间决策偏好进行直接干预或调节。

Conclusion: 本研究证明了通过机械可解释性技术（如梯度归因和激活修补）定位LLM的时间偏好子图是可行的，并展示了通过控制向量（steering vectors）调节模型时间偏好的潜力，强调了对模型决策过程进行显式控制比依赖隐式训练更为重要。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly being deployed to make decisions that require trading off near-term gains against long-term consequences, yet little is known about how they internally represent or resolve these tradeoffs. In this work, we causally localize an underlying subgraph for temporal preference in a distilled LLM (Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507), identifying mid-to-upper-layer nodes through converging evidence from gradient-based attribution and activation patching. We find that the geometry of time horizon is encoded in the residual stream at the expected localized layers. A behavioral analysis reveals that unintervened LLMs discount the future several times less steeply than humans, yet this preference is unstable across contexts, motivating explicit control rather than implicit reliance on training. Finally, we find suggestive evidence that steering vectors can shift temporal preference. Our work demonstrates how mechanistic interpretability can bring us closer to reliable control over how LLMs plan and reason

</details>


### [210] [State commitment learning: training language models to distinguish computation from memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05201)
*Fei Ding,Yongkang Zhang,Runhao Liu,Yuhao Liao,Zijian Zeng,Huiming Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 现有的推理模型因无法区分“计算过程”与“持久知识”导致输出受失败的思维过程干扰。本文提出了 CERL，通过反事实擦除训练迫使模型将重要结论与中间过程解耦，在保证精度的前提下显著提升了模型推理的质量与可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理模型无法区分用于推理的临时计算标记与应作为最终结果保留的持久状态标记。这导致下游决策常受制于错误的推理路径或无效的中间干扰，削弱了推理的可靠性。因此，需要使模型具备将“思维过程”与“结论状态”解耦的能力。

Method: 1. 提出“状态承诺学习”（State Commitment Learning）训练目标，旨在区分持久状态与临时计算；
2. 定义“反事实持续状态充分性”（Persistent-state sufficiency）准则，作为衡量模型在删除隐藏推理痕迹后答案是否依然有效的标准；
3. 提出“反事实擦除强化学习”（Counterfactual Erasure RL, CERL），通过在奖励机制中考核擦除思维链后模型输出的准确性；
4. 引入“擦除依赖协议”（Erasure Dependence Protocol）用于评估模型对隐藏思维的依赖程度。

Result: 在数学、长链逻辑、科学问答及多轮工具调用等多项任务中，CERL 显著降低了模型对隐藏思维链的依赖，且在提升稳健性的同时并未牺牲预测精度，表现优于仅关注正确性的 RL 和长答案监督微调（SFT）基准。

Conclusion: CERL 成功实现了推理模型中计算过程（“思维链”）与持久状态信息的分离。通过区分这两者，模型在保持高准确率的同时，显著增强了推理结果的鲁棒性，减少了对错误或冗余中间思考过程的依赖，为长链条推理优化提供了一种有效且通用的新范式。

Abstract: Reasoning language models do not distinguish tokens used for computation from tokens that constitute persistent state: once generated, all hidden thoughts remain in context and influence future predictions. As a result, downstream reasoning may depend on failed attempts, dead ends, and private scratch work that should not be safely relied on later. We recast this phenomenon as a new training objective, state commitment learning: training models to explicitly distinguish information that should be committed as persistent state from temporary computation that can be discarded. We define a counterfactual criterion, persistent-state sufficiency, which makes it trainable and measurable whether an answer remains usable after hidden thoughts are erased. We then propose Counterfactual Erasure RL (CERL), which evaluates, under the same prefix, both a path that keeps hidden thoughts and a path that erases them, and gives reward only when the erasure path remains correct. We also introduce the Erasure Dependence Protocol and show across mathematics, long-chain logic, scientific QA, and multi-turn tool-use evaluation that CERL substantially reduces answer dependence on hidden thoughts without sacrificing accuracy, consistently outperforming correctness-only RL and long-answer SFT baselines.

</details>


### [211] [Adaptive Oscillatory-State Alignment for Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06010)
*Zhangyao Song,Ziqiong Li,Xiangfei Qiu,Chao Zha,Yinfei Xu,Tao Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对传统时间序列模型难以处理动态演化周期的问题，提出了基于 Hilbert 变换的 AOSNET 框架，通过自适应振荡状态对齐替代固定模板，显著提升了在非平稳环境下的预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的周期性时间序列预测方法多依赖固定的周期或模板，无法处理现实世界中普遍存在的非平稳性演化（如幅度调制、相位漂移和局部频率变化），导致模型与真实动态失配。

Method: 提出了 AOSNET（Hilbert-guided forecasting framework），利用 Hilbert 变换提取解析信号描述符，通过可学习的全局振荡先验（global oscillatory prior）替代固定模板，并设计了描述符条件门控机制（descriptor-conditioned gate）实现局部状态的自适应对齐与修正。

Result: 在八项基准测试中实现了最优或极具竞争力的预测精度，同时保持了较快的推理速度；受控合成实验证明，随着非平稳性程度增加，该方法的优势愈发明显。

Conclusion: AOSNET 通过引入自适应振荡状态对齐机制，有效解决了传统周期性时间序列预测模型中固定模板难以处理动态演化（如频率/相位漂移）的问题，在多项基准测试中达到了 SOTA 或具有竞争力的表现。

Abstract: Long-term time series forecasting benefits from inductive biases that expose recurring temporal structure. Existing periodic forecasting methods typically model recurrence through predefined periods, global spectral components, or fixed learnable templates. However, real-world temporal dynamics are rarely rigidly periodic: oscillatory behavior often evolves through amplitude modulation, phase drift, and local frequency variation. Under these conditions, fixed-template periodic modeling can become fundamentally mismatched to the underlying temporal states. We propose AOSNET, a Hilbert-guided forecasting framework that reformulates periodic forecasting from fixed template matching to adaptive oscillatory-state alignment. AOSNET extracts analytic-signal descriptors from both the observed sequence and a learnable global oscillatory prior, then adaptively aligns local states through a descriptor-conditioned gate that selectively preserves reliable observations while softly correcting mismatched regions. The learned prior serves not as a rigid repeated template but as a flexible oscillatory reference interpreted through local state dynamics. Experiments on eight benchmarks demonstrate state-of-the-art or highly competitive accuracy with fast inference speed. Controlled synthetic studies isolating amplitude modulation, phase drift, and local frequency variation confirm that the advantage of oscillatory-state alignment consistently increases as non-stationarity intensifies.

</details>


### [212] [Gradient Descent with Large Step Size Restores Symmetry in Deep Linear Networks with Multi-Pathway](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05219)
*Hee-Sung Kim,Sungyoon Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究通过对比离散梯度下降与梯度流，揭示了大步长GD如何通过稳定性边缘的振荡机制，克服模型深度带来的单路径过度特化，从而引导网络学习到分布式的共享表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的梯度流理论预测多路径网络会发生“胜者全拿”的对称性破缺，但这与实际训练中观测到的现象不符，需要从离散更新角度揭示深度和步长对路径分化与共享的影响。

Method: 通过理论分析和动力学研究，对比梯度流（GF）与大步长梯度下降（GD）在多路径线性网络中的训练表现，特别是对权重锐度（sharpness）及“稳定性边缘”（Edge of Stability）现象的量化研究。

Result: 证明了单路径解为锐极小值，而分布式信号的权重锐度更低；训练初期表现出对称性破缺，但在“稳定性边缘”产生的振荡推动下，网络进入再平衡阶段，最终实现信号在路径间的重分配。

Conclusion: 离散梯度下降（GD）在较大步长下表现出与梯度流（GF）截然不同的动态，深度和步长共同作用促使网络形成信号分布的共享表示，而非单路径占优。

Abstract: Recent analyses of multi-pathway Deep Linear Networks use Gradient Flow to predict a "winner-takes-all" specialization in which path symmetry breaks and each feature concentrates in a single pathway. In this work, we show that discrete Gradient Descent (GD) with a large step size tells a different story. We prove that single-path solutions are sharp minima, whereas distributing signals across pathways reduces sharpness by a factor that decreases with both the number of pathways and depth. Consequently, while early training reproduces the depth-driven symmetry breaking predicted by GF, oscillations at the Edge of Stability subsequently override this tendency and drive the network into a re-balancing phase, where signals redistribute across pathways. Together, these results clarify how depth shapes pathway competition and explain why large-step GD favors shared representations rather than persistent single-pathway dominance.

</details>


### [213] [Differentiable Efficient Operator Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05232)
*Xiaohuan Pei,Jiyuan Zhang,Yuanfan Guo,Weiguo Feng,Tao Huang,Cho-Jui Hsieh,Chang Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本论文提出了一种通用的可微算子搜索框架，通过将多种 token 缩减技术建模为统一空间，自动寻找最优的多模态 token 处理策略，从而取代了低效的手动算子设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的高效多模态模型依赖于手动设计的 token 缩减算子（如剪枝、合并、池化等），这些算子往往是孤立的且缺乏最优性和通用性。作者旨在将这些算子统一到一个通用的算子空间中，通过自动化手段寻找更高效的缩减策略。

Method: 提出“高效算子搜索”（Efficient Operator Search），通过构建一个包含层激活、保留预算和算子行为的可微搜索空间，联合优化 token 减少位置、数量及处理方式。该方法利用梯度下降策略在满足计算预算和成本约束的情况下搜索最优算子配置。

Result: 该框架能够覆盖主流的手工设计基准，并能发现超越现有方案的混合型算子。在多模态基准测试中，所搜索到的算子在保持高精度的同时，在激进的视觉 token 缩减场景下展现了极佳的准确率-效率平衡。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了高效多模态推理可以从人工设计算子转向可微的自动算子搜索，提出的框架能够发现优于传统手工设计的混合算子，并在高压缩比下实现卓越的性能。

Abstract: Efficient multimodal foundation models often rely on manually designed token-reduction operators, such as pruning, merging, pooling, and adaptive reweighting. Although these operators appear different, we show that they can be interpreted as distinct regimes of a shared operator space. Based on this view, we introduce Efficient Operator Search, a differentiable framework that jointly searches where to reduce tokens, how many tokens to retain, and how reduced token information should be processed. The proposed search space parameterizes layer activation, retention budget, and operator behavior, while the search policy optimizes task performance under one-sided budget and cost constraints. This formulation recovers representative hand-designed baselines as special cases and further discovers hybrid operators beyond isolated manual designs. Experiments on multimodal benchmarks show that the searched operators achieve competitive accuracy-efficiency trade-offs, especially under aggressive visual-token reduction. These results suggest that efficient multimodal inference can be reframed from manual operator design to differentiable operator search.

</details>


### [214] [DiffSlack: Learning under Nonlinear Inequality Constraints via Learnable Slack Variables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05247)
*Ziqian Wang,Chenxi Fang,Zhen Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DiffSlack 是一种可微投影层，通过引入学习到的松弛变量和高斯-牛顿投影，解决了神经网络中大规模非线性不等式约束难以硬执行的问题，提升了复杂工程任务中的规划成功率与安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的硬约束方法通常对约束集有结构限制，或者在处理大规模非线性问题时计算开销过大，难以应对耦合约束。

Method: 提出 DiffSlack 架构：将不等式约束转换为含可学习松弛变量的等式约束，并引入基于阻尼高斯-牛顿（Damped Gauss-Newton）法的可微投影层，配合两阶段课程学习（Curriculum Learning）来稳定训练。

Result: 在包含 200 个非线性约束的车辆路径规划任务中，DiffSlack 相比现有基线方法在规划成功率和几何约束满足度方面表现更佳，且对标注质量的依赖性更低。

Conclusion: DiffSlack 证明了一种将硬非线性不等式约束嵌入神经网络的实用且可扩展的方法，在复杂工程场景（如自动驾驶路径规划）中表现优异，具备强约束满足能力和计算高效性。

Abstract: Enforcing nonlinear inequality constraints in neural networks remains challenging, especially when the output is subject to many coupled constraints. Existing hard constraint methods often impose structural restrictions on the constraint set or introduce substantial computational overhead for large-scale nonlinear problems. Here, we propose DiffSlack, a differentiable projection layer for nonlinear inequality-constrained neural prediction. DiffSlack reformulates inequalities as equalities with learnable slack variables, which are predicted as part of the augmented network output and provide a data-driven warm start for damped Gauss-Newton projection. The projection layer maps raw predictions onto the augmented feasible manifold while preserving end-to-end differentiability. A two-stage curriculum further stabilizes training and improves constraint satisfaction. We evaluate DiffSlack on vehicle path planning with 200 nonlinear inequality constraints from collision avoidance, curvature limits, and waypoint spacing. Compared with existing learning-based baselines, DiffSlack achieves a higher planning success rate and stronger geometric constraint satisfaction under a comparable inference budget. Ablation studies further show that the hard projection layer reduces sensitivity to supervision quality. Closed-loop tracking in CARLA and real-world vehicle experiments confirms the executability of the generated trajectories. These results demonstrate that DiffSlack provides a practical and scalable approach to embedding hard inequality constraints into neural networks for engineering applications.

</details>


### [215] [Alpha-RTL: Test-Time Training for RTL Hardware Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05253)
*Peilong Zhou,Zhirong Chen,Cangyuan Li,Haoyu Gao,Kaiyan Chang,Ziming Qu,Ying Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TTT-RTL，一种针对RTL设计的测试时训练框架。通过将LLM与EDA工具链实时闭环，并利用自适应KL预算控制和状态复用机制，实现了比现有冻结策略模型更优的电路物理性能（PPA）优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM驱动的RTL生成方法多依赖于离线训练或测试阶段的完全冻结策略，难以针对特定硬件设计问题进行深度定制与物理优化，限制了在实际工程场景下的表现。

Method: 提出TTT-RTL框架，该框架在测试阶段通过以下核心机制实时优化LLM策略：1) 进行EDA闭环反馈（语法与仿真验证）；2) 引入基于PUCT索引的“设计状态池”，复用高奖励候选方案；3) 采用带有熵正则化的策略梯度目标函数更新策略；4) 引入“自适应KL预算控制机制”，通过参考KL、有效样本量和奖励饱和信号稳定训练。

Result: 在RTLLM v2.0基准测试（Nangate 45nm）中，PPA乘积几何平均值比参考基准降低了65.1%，优于当前最强冻结策略基线26.1%；在工业级XuanTie C910 FPU模块（Sky130）中实现了59.4%的ADP降低。

Conclusion: TTT-RTL开创了在测试时利用执行EDA反馈进行强化学习的新范式，证明了基于测试时训练的方法能显著提升LLM生成RTL设计的物理优化效果，超越了传统的冻结策略（Frozen-policy）方法。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown increasing promise in generating
  functionally correct register-transfer-level (RTL) hardware designs.
  Recent systems improve further through EDA-integrated reinforcement
  learning with syntax, simulation, and PPA rewards, but train a general
  RTL generator before deployment while test-time approaches search with
  a frozen policy. We instead perform reinforcement learning at test time,
  allowing the LLM policy to adapt to executable EDA feedback for the
  specific RTL problem at hand. We propose TTT-RTL, to our knowledge the
  first per-design test-time training framework that closes the loop
  between an LLM policy and an EDA pipeline for RTL optimization. TTT-RTL
  samples candidate implementations, verifies them through syntax checking
  and simulation, scores valid designs using synthesis-derived PPA product,
  reuses high-reward variants through a PUCT-indexed design-state pool,
  and updates the policy with an entropic policy-gradient objective. To
  stabilize policy updates under sparse or plateaued rewards, we introduce
  an adaptive KL-budget controller that adjusts the entropy constraint
  using reference KL, effective sample size, and reward saturation signals.
  On RTLLM v2.0 under Nangate 45nm, TTT-RTL reduces the geometric-mean
  PPA product by 65.1% over the reference, outperforming the strongest
  published frozen-policy agent baseline at 26.1%. On an industrial
  XuanTie C910 FPU leading-zero-anticipation unit under Sky130, TTT-RTL
  achieves a 59.4% ADP reduction, and ablations confirm that policy
  adaptation, state reuse, and KL-budget control each contribute. These
  results suggest that test-time training with executable EDA feedback can
  move LLM-based RTL generation beyond functional correctness toward
  physically optimized hardware.

</details>


### [216] [Scaling Laws for Behavioral Foundation Models over User Event Sequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05257)
*Rickard Brüel Gabrielsson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探讨了行为序列模型（推荐/行为数据）的扩展定律，发现模型架构的“计算最优”配置受评估指标和算力规模的强烈影响，并揭示了嵌入器与上下文模型的特定参数缩放规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前应用于推荐、支付和欺诈等领域的行为基础模型缺乏像语言模型那样明确的“计算校准”（Compute calibration）和扩展定律指导。

Method: 通过约600次跨度为 $10^{15}$ - $10^{19}$ FLOPs 的实验，对两阶段架构（事件嵌入 + Transformer 解码器）进行实证分析；联合调整了参数分配、关键批大小（Critical batch size）、模型/数据分配以及负采样数量四个关键轴。

Result: 1. 小型嵌入器（约占参数的 2%）在所有测试规模下均为计算最优；2. 数据/模型分配比（D/N Ratio）在算力增大时趋向 Chinchilla 最优启发式；3. 训练损失函数与实际业务指标存在随规模变化的偏差；4. 随着算力增长，负采样数量需求增加，直至受限于内存而非算力。

Conclusion: 在行为基础模型中，评估指标是扩展定律（Scaling Laws）的核心组成部分；改变目标指标会直接修正计算最优配置（Compute-optimal recipe）。研究明确了参数分配、训练数据比例及负采样策略随算力规模变化的规律。

Abstract: Foundation models are increasingly trained on sequences of user actions in recommendation, payments, fraud, and commerce, but these models still lack the kind of compute calibration that scaling laws provide for language models. We study a common two-part behavioral-model architecture: a feature-based event embedder maps each multi-modal item to a vector, and a decoder-only transformer predicts the next event from the resulting sequence. Across roughly 600 runs on real interaction data, spanning $10^{15}$-$10^{19}$ training FLOPs, we jointly vary four deployment-relevant axes: the two-part parameter split, critical batch size, model/data allocation, and the number of sampled negatives used after freezing the embedder. A small embedder ($s^{\star}\!\approx\!2\%$ of parameters) is compute-optimal at every budget we test because embedder parameters are both more expensive per step and exposed to far more repeated items than contextualizer parameters. Compute-optimal training is data-heavy relative to text at low compute, but its $D/N$ ratio moves toward the Chinchilla heuristic as compute increases. The sampled training objective and deployed ranking metrics disagree in ways that themselves scale: critical batch size, optimal negative count after freezing, and the agreement between loss and ranking quality all shift with compute and with the chosen evaluation metric. For negative sampling, larger budgets increasingly prefer more negatives; by $10^{19}$ FLOPs the active constraint is candidate-axis memory rather than FLOPs. In behavioral foundation models, the evaluation metric is therefore part of the scaling law: changing it can change the compute-optimal recipe.

</details>


### [217] [Sharp Low-Degree Thresholds for Planted-vs-Planted Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05266)
*Anda Skeja,Daniel Gutiérrez Espinoza,Fiona Skerman,Alexander S. Wein*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了 planted-vs-planted 问题中低度多项式测试的分析框架，确定了其尖锐检测阈值，并发现该阈值与恢复阈值在常数级高度吻合，且区分了强弱检测的不同相变特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多关注单一 planted 模型的检测，缺乏对两种结构化 planted 机制之间进行区分（planted-vs-planted）的理论分析，特别是关于低度多项式测试的尖锐阈值性质。

Method: 开发了一种基于潜在变量展开（latent-variable expansion）的 planted-vs-planted 测试框架，并引入了用于识别和剔除无信号成分（non-signal contributions）的新方法。

Result: 1. 确立了 planted-vs-planted 环境下低度多项式测试的首个尖锐阈值；2. 在 planted 子矩阵和 planted 稠密子图模型中提供了匹配的低度上下界；3. 证明了该测试阈值与低度恢复阈值在常数级上一致；4. 识别并区分了强检测（尖锐阈值）与弱检测（平滑过渡）的行为表现。

Conclusion: 研究建立了一个用于分析 planted-vs-planted 设置中低度多项式测试的通用框架，并证明了该框架下统计测试阈值与其恢复阈值在常数级的吻合，揭示了检测与恢复任务之间的本质联系及其相变性质。

Abstract: We establish the first sharp thresholds for low-degree polynomial tests in planted-vs-planted settings, where the goal is to determine with vanishing error which of two structured planted mechanisms generated the observed data. We prove matching low-degree upper and lower bounds for counting communities in the planted submatrix and planted dense subgraph models. The resulting testing threshold coincides, down to the sharp constant, with the known low-degree recovery threshold. In contrast, the task of weak testing, where the goal is to outperform random guessing, does not have a sharp threshold but rather a smooth transition, which we identify. To prove our results, we develop a framework for planted-vs-planted testing that builds on a latent-variable expansion originating in low-degree recovery and employs new methods to identify and prune non-signal contributions.

</details>


### [218] [Flash-WAM: Modality-Aware Distillation for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05254)
*Arman Akbari,Ci Zhang,Arash Akbari,Lin Zhao,Yixiao Chen,Weiwei Chen,Xuan Zhang,Geng Yuan,Yanzhi Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Flash-WAM 是一种模态感知的多步扩散蒸馏框架，通过为视频和动作流定制不同的参数化策略，将世界动作模型的推理压缩至单步，实现了 23 倍加速并达到实时控制水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界动作模型（WAMs）在机器人控制中表现优异，但多步扩散推理导致延迟过高无法满足实时控制需求；现有的蒸馏方法无法处理视频与动作模态间不同的噪声调度和分布不对称性。

Method: 提出 Flash-WAM 框架，将一致性蒸馏方法推广至多模态设置。针对动作流采用线性梯度缩放参数化（适合低噪声机制），针对视频流采用方差保持参数化（适合高噪声机制），并通过结构分析确保一致性函数的收敛。

Result: 在 NVIDIA L40S 上将每块推理延迟从 8.1 秒降低至 348 ms（23倍加速）；在仿真基准测试中保持了高成功率（RoboTwin 2.0 达 85.5%，LIBERO 达 95.7%）；在真实机器人实验中实现了 60% 的平均成功率，远超朴素的一致性蒸馏。

Conclusion: Flash-WAM 提出了一种模态感知的蒸馏框架，通过针对视频和动作流匹配不同的噪声机制和参数化方法，成功实现了单步推理，在大幅提升推理速度的同时保持了甚至优于传统方法的任务成功率，实现了机器人控制的实时化。

Abstract: World-action models (WAMs) jointly generate future video and robot actions through iterative diffusion, achieving strong performance on manipulation benchmarks but requiring tens of denoising steps, a cost that precludes real-time control. Step distillation has emerged as the natural remedy, but off-the-shelf methods break down in the joint video-action setting because video and action streams use different SNR-shifted noise schedules and reach training with substantially different marginal noise distributions, an asymmetry that single-modality distillation methods cannot accommodate. We introduce \textbf{Flash-WAM}, a modality-aware step-distillation framework inspired by consistency distillation that selects the consistency function for each modality to match its noise regime: a linear-gradient-scaling parametrization for the action stream's low-noise regime, paired with a variance-preserving parametrization for the video stream's high-noise regime, grounded in a structural analysis of the consistency-function family that characterizes the achievable gradient scaling under the consistency boundary condition. Instantiated on LingBot-VA, Flash-WAM compresses inference to a single step in each modality. On RoboTwin 2.0, this reduces per-chunk latency from $8.1$ seconds to $348$ ms on NVIDIA L40S, a $23{\times}$ speedup that enables real-time inference. Flash-WAM preserves task success on simulation benchmarks ($85.5\%$ RoboTwin 2.0, $95.7\%$ LIBERO) and substantially recovers real-world performance ($60\%$ average on a Unitree G1 humanoid robot), while naive consistency distillation drops to $24\%$ at the same step budget.

</details>


### [219] [Statistically Reliable LLM-Based Ranking Evaluation via Prediction-Powered Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05308)
*Abhishek Divekar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PRECISE框架通过结合少量人工标注与大规模LLM判断，利用PPI技术实现了排名评价指标的无偏估计与精度提升，不仅在高难度指标上降低了计算复杂度，更在实际业务中成功提升了决策准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的排名评价指标依赖于人工标注，成本高且规模受限；直接使用LLM作为裁判存在潜在偏差，导致评估结果不可靠。因此需要一种既能利用大规模LLM资源，又能通过少量人工标注进行偏差校正的评估方法。

Method: 提出PRECISE框架，将预测支持推断（Prediction-Powered Inference, PPI）应用于排名评价；通过算法优化将计算复杂度从 O(2^|C|) 降至 O(2^K)，使其适用于分层评价指标；利用PPPI机制校正LLM判断的偏差，确保即便LLM存在误差，推断结果依然保持无偏。

Result: 在ESCI基准测试中，使用30个人工标注结合Claude 3 Sonnet判断，将Precision@4的标准误差从4.45降低至3.50（降低21%）；在生产环境应用中，以100个标签正确识别了最优系统，A/B测试验证显示每日销售额提升了407个基点。

Conclusion: PRECISE框架通过结合少量高质量人工标注和大规模LLM判断，显著提升了排名评价指标（如Precision@K）的统计精度与评估可靠性，并在实际生产环境验证中表现优异。

Abstract: With PRECISE, we extended Prediction-Powered Inference to produce bias-corrected estimates of ranking evaluation metrics by combining a small human-labeled set with a large LLM-judged set. PPI is provably unbiased regardless of the LLM judge's error profile. We make it applicable to hierarchical metrics like Precision@K, where annotations are per-document but the metric is per-query, by reducing the output-space computation from O(2^|C|) to O(2^K). On the ESCI benchmark, augmenting 30 human annotations with Claude 3 Sonnet judgments reduces the standard error of Precision@4 estimates from 4.45 to 3.50 (a 21% relative reduction). In a production system, our framework correctly identified the best of three system variants from 100 human labels and 2 hours of domain-expert annotation; A/B testing confirmed this ranking with +407 bps in daily sales.

</details>


### [220] [Policy-Conditioned Counterfactual Credit for Verifiable Reinforcement Learning of Long-Horizon Language Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05263)
*Renwei Meng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了 CVT-RL 算法，通过引入反事实贡献估计和多维约束，解决了长程语言智能体在强化学习中因过程奖励缺陷而产生的虚假推理和策略捷径问题，显著提升了智能体的推理可信度和安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的长程语言智能体在强化学习中常产生虚假证据链、信念漂移及为了满足终端检查而采取“捷径行为”的问题，且大多过程奖励仅为相关性奖励，无法衡量特定步骤对最终成功的实际因果贡献。

Method: 提出 CVT-RL 算法，包含：1. 带有密集可验证奖励的约束策略梯度；2. 干预有效性门控（Intervention-validity gating）；3. 策略条件化反事实贡献（PCCC）估计器，利用多种可控干预（如证据置换）来衡量决策的实际贡献；4. 使用增强拉格朗日方法对不支持的声明、跳过验证和工具篡改等行为进行约束。

Result: 在长文本问答、ALFWorld、ScienceWorld 及 Web/工具任务中，任务成功率从 75.4% 提升至 78.9%，证据 F1 值从 78.9 提升至 82.8，系统性“黑客行为”（即通过捷径达成目标）显著降低（从 8.1% 降至 4.6%），且对探测逃避攻击表现出更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 通过结合反事实信用分配（PCCC）、干预有效性门控和增强拉格朗日约束，CVT-RL 为长程语言智能体提供了一种更可靠、更具鲁棒性的强化学习路径，有效减少了幻觉和捷径行为。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards improves reasoning and tool use, yet long-horizon language agents still learn unsupported evidence chains, belief drift, and shortcut actions that satisfy terminal checks. Existing process rewards are mostly correlational: they reward retrieval-, reflection-, or verification-like steps without estimating whether the step contributes to final verified success under a specified intervention. We propose CVT-RL, a constrained policy-gradient algorithm with dense verifiable rewards, intervention-validity gating, and a policy-conditioned counterfactual contribution (PCCC) estimator. Deletion, semantic substitution, evidence substitution, and tool-output perturbation define separate controlled interventions; continuations are sampled from a frozen reference policy, and a selection-adjusted doubly robust estimator augments the advantage. Belief control uses only prefix-observable labels, while an augmented Lagrangian constrains unsupported claims, skipped verification, tool tampering, and unsafe calls. On long-context QA, ALFWorld, ScienceWorld, and web/tool tasks, CVT-RL improves average task success from 71.8% for compute-matched non-causal RL and 75.4% for an information-matched counterfactual-process baseline to 78.9%, improves evidence F1 from 78.9 to 82.8 over the information-matched baseline, and reduces measured hacking from 7.2% to 3.9%. Independent human audit estimates 4.6% hacking for CVT-RL versus 8.1% for the information-matched baseline, and adaptive detector-evasion attacks raise hacking only to 7.1%. Stratified bootstrap and mixed-effects tests give p<0.01 after Holm correction for all primary metrics. Carefully scoped counterfactual credit, paired with validity gating, diagnostics, and verifiable constraints, provides a reproducible route toward more reliable long-horizon RL for language agents.

</details>


### [221] [MolE-RAG: Molecular Structure-Enhanced Retrieval-Augmented Generation for Chemistry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05693)
*Joey Chan,Wonbin Kweon,Ashley Shin,Niharika Bhattacharjee,Pengcheng Jiang,Yue Guo,Jiawei Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MolE-RAG 是一种无需微调的检索增强框架，通过动态整合文献、分子属性和结构相似性数据，显著增强了大语言模型在分子性质预测中的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型（LLM）处理 SMILES 等分子表示形式时，由于其与自然语言的语义差异，导致化学推理能力受限，需要一种方法来弥补这种知识与语义鸿沟。

Method: 提出一种名为 MolE-RAG 的训练时无关（training-free）检索增强生成框架，在推理阶段为每个预测任务引入三类补充背景信息：化学文献、分子特定属性（如同义词、描述符、官能团等）以及结构相似分子。

Result: 在九项分子性质预测任务中，MolE-RAG 表现优异：分类任务 ROC-AUC 最高提升 28 个百分点，回归任务 RMSE 最高降低 67%（相比仅使用 SMILES 的基线）。研究还发现不同信息来源对不同模型和任务的贡献度存在差异。

Conclusion: MolE-RAG 是一种无需微调且高效的框架，通过引入多源背景信息，显著提升了 LLM 在分子性质预测任务中的性能，证明了在推理阶段整合异构化学知识对弥补 LLM 化学推理能力不足的有效性。
