### [257] [Representation Learning Enables Scalable Multitask Deep Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05555)
*Johan Obando-Ceron,Lu Li,Scott Fujimoto,Pierre-Luc Bacon,Aaron Courville,Pablo Samuel Castro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 为了解决多任务强化学习的可扩展性问题，本文证明了预测性表征学习是核心驱动力，并通过提出MR.Q算法，展示了在无需规划的情况下，仅靠预测性表征与高容量价值函数即可实现优于现有模型基方法的多任务控制效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前模型基强化学习（model-based RL）通常依赖复杂的规划和训练流程，导致其多任务扩展性不明确，作者旨在探究实现可扩展多任务RL的核心要素。

Method: 提出了一种名为MR.Q的模型无关（model-free）算法，将其与辅助预测目标结合，构建于可扩展的Actor-Critic架构中进行训练。

Result: 在多项连续控制任务中，MR.Q的表现优于现有的世界模型（world-model）方法及多种深度强化学习基线，同时降低了计算开销并提升了训练效率。

Conclusion: 多任务强化学习扩展的关键在于“预测性表征学习”，而非复杂的规划（planning）。将预测性表征与高容量价值函数结合，无需规划即可实现卓越性能。

Abstract: Scaling reinforcement learning (RL) to diverse multitask settings remains a central challenge. While recent advances in model-based RL achieve strong performance, they rely on planning and complex training pipelines, making it unclear which components are essential for scalability. We revisit this question and argue that the primary driver of scalable multitask RL is not model-based control, but \emph{representation learning}. In particular, we show that combining predictive, model-based representations with high-capacity value function approximation is sufficient to achieve strong performance, even without planning. We evaluate a simple model-free algorithm, MR.Q, coupled with auxiliary predictive objectives into a scalable actor-critic architecture. This approach outperforms a recent world-model-based method and a range of deep RL baselines across a diverse suite of multitask continuous control tasks, while significantly reducing computational overhead and improving wall-clock efficiency. We observe consistent improvements with increased model capacity and show through ablations that predictive representation learning is critical for performance.

</details>


### [258] [Field Validation of a Multi-Resolution ConvLSTM Framework for Retaining Wall Deformation Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05556)
*Jihoon Kim,Heejung Youn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究提出并验证了一种多分辨率ConvLSTM堆叠框架，通过数值模拟训练即可在实际基坑工程中实现高精度的挡土墙变形预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在基坑分级开挖过程中，准确预测挡土墙的变形对于工程安全至关重要，但传统的预测方法难以兼顾复杂工程条件的时空特征，且高质量现场标注数据稀缺。

Method: 提出了一种多分辨率卷积长短期记忆（ConvLSTM）堆叠集成框架；使用高斯噪声增强的数值模拟数据进行训练；通过34个测斜仪的实测数据进行现场验证，并分析了时间不规则性对预测的影响。

Result: 该框架可预测高达5.0米额外开挖深度的挡土墙变形，平均绝对误差（MAE）仅为1.4 mm，判定系数（R²）达到0.93。

Conclusion: 该多分辨率ConvLSTM框架证明了仅通过数值模拟训练的深度学习模型能够有效地迁移至实际工程，在挡土墙侧向位移预测中表现出极高的可靠性和泛化能力。

Abstract: This study presents a comprehensive field validation of a multi-resolution Convolutional Long Short-Term Memory (ConvLSTM) framework for predicting retaining wall deformation during staged excavation. The framework is trained on Gaussian noise-augmented numerical simulations and integrates ConvLSTM models operating at different temporal resolutions through a stacking ensemble strategy. The proposed framework is validated using field monitoring data from 34 inclinometers across 11 excavation sites in South Korea. Site-wise prediction performance is systematically evaluated using multiple evaluation metrics, with analyses of the influence of temporal deformation irregularity and spatiotemporal prediction characteristics on model performance. The results demonstrate that the framework predicts retaining wall deformation associated with up to 5.0 m of additional excavation with an average mean absolute error of 1.4 mm and a coefficient of determination of 0.93 across the excavation sites. These results indicate that the framework, although trained exclusively on numerically simulated and augmented database, can be effectively applied to diverse field excavation conditions and achieve a reliable level of prediction accuracy in practical retaining wall deformation prediction.

</details>


### [259] [Autoregressive Diffusion World Models for Off-Policy Evaluation of LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05558)
*Kaixuan Liu,Guojun Xiong,Weinan Zhang,Shengpu Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了ADWM（自回归扩散世界模型），通过模拟环境响应而非实际交互，利用策略条件的扩散生成过程，实现了对LLM代理的高效、安全、离线评估，克服了传统方法在处理离散动作及误差积累上的局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多轮交互环境中评估LLM代理通常面临高昂成本和高安全风险，且传统的基于扩散的离线策略评估（OPE）方法难以处理LLM代理中离散、依交互而动的文本动作。

Method: 提出了一种自回归扩散世界模型（ADWM）：1. 利用潜在扩散模型对各状态转移进行建模，避免了自回归模型常见的误差累积问题；2. 采用策略条件评分函数（policy-conditioned score function），使LLM代理能在模拟过程中实时引导生成过程，确保轨迹与代理的决策模式一致。

Result: 实验证明ADWM在多种多轮代理任务中达到了较高的价值估计准确度和评估可靠性，证实了其作为实用离线评估框架的有效性。

Conclusion: ADWM通过在离线轨迹中引入策略条件扩散模型，成功实现了无需在线交互的LLM代理评估，是在资源受限或高风险环境下评估LLM代理的一种可靠且实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Evaluating large language model (LLM) agents in multi-turn interactive environments is expensive and risky, as it requires online environment interaction. We propose ADWM (Autoregressive Diffusion World Model), an evaluation framework that estimates the performance of a new LLM agent policy purely from pre-collected trajectories. The core idea is to learn a latent diffusion world model that simulates how the environment responds to the evaluation policy, without ever executing it in the real environment. Existing diffusion-based OPE methods guide full trajectories in a single pass by jointly diffusing states and actions, an assumption that breaks down for LLM agents whose actions are discrete text that must be sampled from the policy after observing the environment. Unlike autoregressive world models that suffer from compounding errors, ADWM models each transition as an independent denoising process, enabling reliable step-by-step rollouts where the world model and agent alternate in causal order. Crucially, the LLM agent under evaluation directly guides the diffusion generation at each step via a policy-conditioned score function, ensuring that simulated trajectories accurately reflect its decision-making patterns. Empirically, ADWM achieves accurate value estimates and evaluation reliability across diverse multi-turn agent tasks, demonstrating its promise as a practical framework for offline LLM agent evaluation.

</details>


### [260] [CLaaS: Continual learning as a service for sample efficient online learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05559)
*Kion Fallah,Silen Naihin,Barak Widawsky,Qingqing Mao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对 LLM Agent 在动态环境中面临的样本匮乏和分布偏移挑战，本文提出了 CLaaS 系统，通过异步经验重放实现参数持续微调，验证了其在保持旧能力的同时优于上下文学习的迁移效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM Agent 在动态的现实世界中部署时面临分布偏移（Distribution Shift）问题，且由于环境不可重置，交互样本获取昂贵，亟需一种既能从既往经验中学习又能保持旧能力的持续学习范式。

Method: 提出了一种名为 CLaaS (Continual Learning as-a-Service) 的系统，支持 Agent 在动态环境中持续在线学习。该系统利用经验重放机制存储交互历史，并通过异步梯度下降更新模型参数，从而提升样本效率。

Result: 在对抗性任务测试中，CLaaS 表现优于传统的上下文学习方法。实验表明，引入经验重放机制对于在有限样本下的在线持续学习至关重要，显著提升了前向迁移性能，并减少了性能损耗。

Conclusion: CLaaS 架构证明了在LLM Agent部署中，通过异步经验重放（Experience Replay）进行参数更新，在处理分布偏移时，比单纯依靠上下文学习（In-context Learning）具有更好的前向迁移能力，且能有效缓解灾难性遗忘。

Abstract: Deployed large language model agents must adapt to distribution shift in dynamic environments. Ideally, adaptation can be performed from accumulated agent experiences and retain prior capabilities while transferring to future tasks. However, agent actions and environmental transitions can only be sampled once per scenario, as real-world environments cannot be trivially reset. To this end, we investigate an experiential and online continual learning setting in which agents learn from a stream of scenarios. We propose continual learning as-a-service (CLaaS), a system which enables agents to improve during deployment, abstracted behind a chat API. To increase sample efficiency, CLaaS stores rollouts in an experience replay buffer for gradient reuse during asynchronous training. We evaluate CLaaS on an adversarial task, demonstrating that parametric updates lead to superior forward transfer and less forgetting than in-context learning, with replay being a critical choice for sample efficiency.

</details>


### [261] [AsyncWebRL: Efficient Multi-Step RL for Visual Web Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05597)
*Hao Bai,Rui Yang,Chenlu Ye,Spencer Whitehead,Aviral Kumar,Tong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AsyncWebRL 提出了一种异步 RL 训练架构和改进的 GRPO 归一化策略，在大幅提升训练吞吐量的同时，有效缩短了智能体的任务轨迹长度，并在 Web 自动化任务中取得了 SOTA 性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言 Web 智能体使用同步强化学习训练时，存在 GPU 利用率低（同步延迟）以及生成轨迹冗余（步数和 token 过长）导致的算力浪费问题。

Method: 1. 系统层面：采用异步设计（重叠 rollout、梯度更新与策略刷新），结合“永久卷动池（everlasting rollout pool）”和轻量化截图处理。
2. 算法层面：改进多步 GRPO 算法，将原有的 per-trajectory 归一化项 $1/|τ_i|$ 替换为常数 $1/k$，消除冗余长轨迹的梯度抑制问题。

Result: 1. 训练吞吐量：相比 WebGym 同步流水线提升达 2.9 倍。
2. 性能指标：在 WebGym OOD 测试集上的成功率提升 5.8%（相对值），并在中等难度和高难度任务上分别取得 42% 和 48% 的相对性能提升。

Conclusion: 通过系统设计（异步架构）与算法改进（常数归一化）的协同优化，AsyncWebRL 有效解决了 Web 代理强化学习训练中的算力冗余和轨迹冗余问题，显著提升了训练效率与任务成功率。

Abstract: Training vision-language web agents with multi-step RL is compute-intensive, with two dominant forms of inefficiency: idle GPUs in synchronous RL, and trajectories that use more steps and tokens than necessary. We present AsyncWebRL, which addresses both. On the system side, an asynchronous design overlaps rollout, gradient update, and policy refresh across iterations, paired with two web-agent-specific adaptations, namely an everlasting rollout pool and lightweight screenshot handling, that together deliver up to a $2.9\times$ end-to-end training-throughput speedup over the previously fastest open synchronous pipeline (WebGym). On the algorithmic side, we identify the per-trajectory normalizer $1/|τ_i|$ in multi-step GRPO as the root cause of trajectory-level and token-level inefficiency: because failures are systematically longer than successes, it down-weights the negative gradient on failed tokens, so the policy keeps producing verbose memory schemas. Replacing $1/|τ_i|$ with a constant $1/k$ breaks this coupling, contracting trajectories while preserving aggregate success. Together, these contributions set a new open-source state of the art on the WebGym out-of-distribution test split (+5.8% relative over the 42.9% prior best), with the largest gains on the harder slices (+42% relative on Medium, +48% relative on Hard).

</details>


### [262] [From Prediction to Self: Developmental Conditions for Agency in Minimal Neural Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05605)
*Evan Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究通过受控发展实验发现，自我意识的产生依赖于四个阶梯式条件；提出了“主体性增益”度量指标，证实了通过前向采样动作选择能使神经网络有效地将自身因果行为从复杂的环境感知中剥离出来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索一个单纯的预测系统如何产生自我意识（即区分自身因果影响与外部世界因果影响的能力）的起源与机制。

Method: 构建了一个具有192维GRU的简约模型，通过40个受控实验组成的“发展序列”逐步添加组件，观测系统区分“自我引起的改变”与“世界引起的改变”的能力，并对比了三种动作选择策略。

Result: 系统在满足四个特定阶段后能成功实现自我表征；定义“主体性增益”指标验证了具备主体性的模型优于盲目预测模型；发现传统的梯度下降动作选择策略在该任务中表现不佳，而前向采样策略能有效产生主体性增益；同时揭示了单纯的预测编码无法独立产生自我表征。

Conclusion: 主体性并不仅是预测能力的副产品，而是通过四个特定发展阶段建立起来的：稳定状态建立、因果动作循环、本体感受反馈以及感知与动作学习的异步化。提出“主体性增益”(Agency Gain)作为衡量主体自我意识发展的度量指标，并证明了基于前向采样(forward-sampled)的动作选择对于产生主体性至关重要。

Abstract: How does a system that merely predicts the world come to distinguish its own causal influence from everything else? We trace this transition in a minimal 192-dimensional GRU through 40 controlled experiments arranged as a developmental sequence, adding components one at a time and tracking whether the system can distinguish self-caused from world-caused changes.
  The developmental path reveals four conditions that must be satisfied in strict order: (1) persistent state forming stable attractors, (2) a causal action loop linking output to input, (3) proprioceptive feedback that makes implicit causal knowledge explicit, and (4) asynchronous awakening - perceptual learning must consolidate before action learning begins. We propose agency gain (A = Err_world - Err_self), the predictive advantage of knowing one's own action, as a metric to track this process. The self-aware predictor consistently outperforms the self-blind predictor across periodic (sinusoidal) and chaotic (Lorenz) environments, and the metric survives ablation of all auxiliary components. Only forward-sampled action selection produces meaningful agency gain; two gradient-based alternatives degenerate.
  Equally significant are 12 falsified hypotheses mapping where development stalls: predictive coding alone does not produce self-represent

</details>


### [263] [Cross-Epoch Adaptive Rollout Optimization for RL Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05606)
*Yiming Zong,Yige Wang,Jiashuo Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对 LLM 后训练中采样资源浪费问题，本文提出了 CERO 方法，通过在线自适应分配 rollout 预算，显著提升了训练的样本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 后训练中的强化学习通常为每个 prompt 分配固定的 rollout 数量（采样预算），未考虑不同 prompt 提供的训练信号质量差异，导致计算资源浪费且训练效率低下。

Method: 提出 CERO（Compute-Efficient Rollout Optimization）方法：将问题建模为具有收益递减规律的在线资源分配问题；利用 Beta 后验分布估计每个 prompt 的成功概率及其方差，构建凹效用函数；采用 Fenchel 对偶重构解决非时间可分的目标函数，并通过投影在线梯度下降（OGD）更新对偶变量。

Result: 在数学推理任务中，CERO 在多个开源 LLM 和基准测试上均优于 GRPO，证实了自适应分配策略在提升样本效率方面的有效性。理论上证明了其具有 O(√K) 的遗憾界限。

Conclusion: CERO 证明了在 LLM 后训练中，动态分配采样资源（rollout）不仅在理论上具有可控的遗憾界限，在实践中也能显著优于传统的固定预算分配策略，是提升大模型强化学习训练效率的有效路径。

Abstract: LLM post-training often relies on reinforcement learning methods that sample multiple rollouts per prompt, yet most existing approaches use a fixed rollout budget for every prompt, despite large differences in the training signal different prompts provide. In this paper, we study adaptive rollout allocation under a fixed global budget and formulate the problem as online resource allocation with prompt-level diminishing returns. Our method, CERO, maintains a Beta posterior over each prompt's success probability and uses the posterior expected Bernoulli variance as a Bayesian estimate of the value of additional rollouts. We use this estimate to construct a concave, saturating utility over cumulative allocations, yielding an objective in which decisions across prompts and epochs are coupled by the global budget. Since the resulting objective is temporally nonseparable, we derive a Fenchel-dual reformulation and update both prompt-level and budget-level dual variables via projected online gradient descent. Under fixed prompt utilities, we prove an $O(\sqrt{K})$ regret bound against the offline allocation benchmark. Experiments on mathematical-reasoning problems show that CERO consistently outperforms GRPO across multiple open-weight LLMs and benchmarks, demonstrating that adaptive rollout budgeting can improve sample efficiency.

</details>


### [264] [StableRCA: Robust Graph-Agnostic Mechanism-Level Root Cause Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05636)
*Xiaoyu Lin,Nicholas Tagliapietra,Kehan Li,Lavdim Halilaj,Juergen Luettin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: StableRCA 是一种新型因果根因分析框架，它绕过了全局因果图发现的难题，通过局部马尔可夫边界分析有效定位复杂系统中的异常驱动变量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有根因分析（RCA）方法存在局限：基于图的方法依赖于已知或精确估计的因果图（难以大规模实现），而无图统计方法难以区分结构性因果与边缘异常，且往往基于过强的假设。

Method: 提出 StableRCA 框架，通过局部机制级分析（local mechanism-level analysis），利用独立因果机制（ICM）原理，通过估计局部马尔可夫边界并检测其中的条件分布偏移来定位干预目标。

Result: 在合成基准和五个真实世界数据集上的实验表明，StableRCA 对图误设具有鲁棒性，能够处理多重干预目标，且在大规模系统中具备良好的扩展性。

Conclusion: StableRCA 通过局部马尔可夫边界估计和条件分布偏移检测，实现了无需全局因果图发现的有效因果根因分析，在多种复杂场景中表现出稳健性、可扩展性和可靠性。

Abstract: Root-Cause Analysis (RCA) seeks to identify the variables responsible for abnormal system behavior in complex domains such as manufacturing, cloud computing, and healthcare. Existing approaches face a critical bottleneck: graph-based causal methods can identify intervention targets but typically require a known or accurately estimated causal graph, while graph-free statistical methods either localize marginal anomalies rather than structural causes, or rely on restrictive assumptions about graph structure or functional form. We propose StableRCA, a local mechanism-level RCA framework that avoids global graph discovery by estimating local Markov boundaries and detecting conditional distribution shifts within them. Leveraging the Independent Causal Mechanism principle, we show that intervention targets can be identified with probability converging exponentially in sample size under faithful Markov boundary recovery and non-degenerate mechanism shifts. Experiments on synthetic benchmarks and five real-world datasets demonstrate that StableRCA is robust to graph misspecification, effective under multiple intervention targets, scalable to large systems, and reliable across diverse application domains. Code is available at: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/StableRCA-E362

</details>


### [265] [Q-GNN: Query-Conditioned Graph Neural Networks with Type Awareness for Knowledge Graph Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05639)
*Dongxiao He,Ruqiong Zhang,Zhizhi Yu,Ling Ding,Di Jin,Guangquan Xu,Zhiyong Feng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对现有的知识图谱补全方法忽视查询实体信息的问题，本文提出了Q-GNN，通过融入查询实体的结构上下文和LLM推断的语义类型，实现了更精准的推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于GNN的KGC方法在进行推理时，仅将查询实体视为子图提取的结构锚点，而忽略了查询实体本身携带的关键信息（如结构属性和语义类型），导致推理引导信号不完整。

Method: 提出了一种名为Q-GNN的方法，包含两个关键机制：1. 结构上下文编码器，用于捕捉查询实体周围的邻居结构与关系模式并调节消息传递；2. 基于大语言模型（LLM）的语义类型推断，将实体的语义约束引入到注意力机制和最终打分过程中。

Result: 在标准KGC基准测试数据集上的实验结果表明，Q-GNN相较于现有基于GNN的方法取得了更好的性能。

Conclusion: 本文提出的Q-GNN模型通过整合查询实体的结构上下文与语义类型信息，有效解决了现有基于GNN的KGC方法忽视查询实体信息的问题，提升了推理性能。

Abstract: Knowledge Graph Completion (KGC) aims at predicting missing triplets from incomplete knowledge graphs, which is crucial for downstream applications. Recently, Graph Neural Network (GNN)-based methods have achieved remarkable success by performing message passing over query-centered local subgraphs. However, in practice, a query is jointly defined by both the entity and the relation, with both carrying information indispensable for reasoning, yet these methods rely solely on the query relation as the guiding signal, while the information inherent in the query entity is not leveraged to guide inference - the entity serves merely as a structural anchor for subgraph extraction. To this end, we incorporate query entity information into the reasoning process from two perspectives: the first is structural context, i.e., the neighboring structure and relation patterns around the entity, which is encoded by a dedicated context encoder and used to modulate messages; the second is semantic type of the entity, inferred by a large language model, which is incorporated into attention computation and final scoring to provide type-level prior constraints. Together, these two sources of information enable the reasoning process to be guided by both the query relation and the query entity. Experimental results on standard benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed Q-GNN.

</details>


### [266] [Two-Way Is Better Than One: Bidirectional Alignment with Cycle Consistency for Exemplar-Free Class-Incremental Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05675)
*Hongye Xu,Bartosz Krawczyk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对无样本类增量学习中投影漂移补偿造成的偏差与循环不一致性问题，BiCyc 通过双向循环对齐策略实现特征传输与表征协同优化，有效缓解了灾难性遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的无样本类增量学习（EFCIL）中的投影补偿方法存在系统性偏差，表现为扭曲当前特征几何结构或导致循环不一致性，从而随着任务增加积累误差，导致灾难性遗忘。

Method: 提出了一种名为 BiCyc 的双向投影对齐方法，引入循环一致性目标，联合优化旧到新（old-to-new）和新到旧（new-to-old）两个映射。通过停滞梯度门控（stop-gradient gating）机制，使传输和表征协同演化，并利用循环损失约束白化空间中的奇异谱。

Result: 在分析层面证明了循环损失能将奇异谱向单位值收敛，优化了类均值和协方差的传输，从而减小了分类对数几率（log-odds）的扰动。在标准 EFCIL 基准测试中，显著降低了遗忘并提高了整体准确率。

Conclusion: BiCyc 通过双向投影一致性和循环损失有效缓解了无样本类增量学习中的灾难性遗忘，在保持类增量学习准确性的同时，不仅在从零训练场景下表现优异，在预训练微调场景下也具有竞争力。

Abstract: Continual learning (CL) seeks models that acquire new skills without erasing prior knowledge. In exemplar-free class-incremental learning (EFCIL), this challenge is amplified because past data cannot be stored, making representation drift for old classes particularly harmful. Prototype-based EFCIL is attractive for its efficiency, yet prototypes drift as the embedding space evolves; therefore, projection-based drift compensation has become a popular remedy. We show, however, that existing one-directional projections introduce systematic bias: they either retroactively distort the current feature geometry or align past classes only locally, leaving cycle inconsistencies that accumulate across tasks. We introduce BiCyc, a bidirectional projector alignment approach with a cycle-consistency objective. BiCyc jointly optimizes two maps, old-to-new and new-to-old, with stop-gradient gating so that transport and representation co-evolve. Analytically, we show that the cycle loss contracts the singular spectrum toward unity in whitened space, and that improved transport of class means and covariances yields smaller perturbations of classification log-odds, preserving old-class decisions and mitigating catastrophic forgetting. Empirically, across standard EFCIL benchmarks, BiCyc substantially reduces forgetting and improves accuracy in from-scratch settings, while remaining competitive in the pretrained fine-grained regime.

</details>


### [267] [Causal Modeling of Selection in Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05689)
*Haoyue Dai,Zeyu Tang,Peter Spirtes,Kun Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种区分静态选择与进化选择的新型因果图模型，并设计了相应的识别算法，解决了现有因果发现方法在处理演化数据时的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前因果发现研究往往混淆了“静态选择”（一次性筛选）与“进化选择”（多轮差分适度繁殖），导致现有图模型在处理进化数据时会产生错误的发现结果。

Method: 提出了一种专门针对“进化选择”过程的图模型，并开发了一套能够在单环境或多环境/代际数据下进行模型识别的稳健且完备的算法。

Result: 实验证明，该方法能准确从观察数据中还原出进化背后的潜在因果机制，纠正了传统模型在处理演化过程时的偏差。

Conclusion: 该论文指出，传统因果发现方法在处理进化选择数据时存在基础性缺陷，提出的新模型和算法能够有效区分进化选择机制，提升了复杂演化系统中的因果推断准确性。

Abstract: Understanding potential selection in data is crucial for causal discovery; we argue that "selection" in common narratives takes two forms, which we term static and evolutionary selection, respectively. Static selection refers to a one-shot filtering process where observed data consist of a subset of the population of interest, as in survey volunteer bias. Evolutionary selection, in contrast, operates through repeated rounds of differential fitness in reproduction, where observed data constitute the latest generation shaped by a historical trajectory, as in immune adaptation, antibiotic resistance, and social norm emergence. Existing methods largely conflate these two forms and rely on an identical graphical model of selection. We show that this model is valid for static settings but fails to characterize data under evolution, yielding false discovery results. To address this, we introduce a new model that specifically characterizes evolutionary selection, and develop a sound and complete procedure for identifying such models from data across one or multiple environments or generations. Experimental results validate the method's ability to uncover the relevant mechanisms underlying evolution from data.

</details>


### [268] [Benchmarking Counterfactual Prediction in Epidemic Time Series with Time-Varying Interventions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05692)
*Wenhao Mu,Facundo Yan,Anik Mumssen,Marisa Eisenberg,Alexander Rodríguez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对现有因果推理缺乏真实反事实基准的问题，本文提出了一个基于智能体模型、涵盖150+美国郡县的大规模时序因果推理基准，并验证了其在评估复杂策略干预下的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的时间序列因果推理研究缺乏具有观测反事实结果（Ground-truth counterfactuals）的真实基准，现有数据集要么缺乏真实标签，要么模拟过于简化，无法刻画复杂的因果动态。

Method: 利用基于人口统计、流动性、流行病学和政策数据校准的智能体模型（Agent-based Model），在全美150多个郡县生成具有真实性的反事实轨迹数据。支持静态与时变处理，并涵盖单一及多种策略的干预设定。

Result: 构建了一个大规模、高真实性的时序因果预测基准，并对多种先进的因果推理方法进行了评估，量化了它们在复杂动态干预场景下的性能瓶颈。

Conclusion: 该基准测试揭示了现有因果推理方法在处理复杂时序动态时的性能差异，强调了在真实、复杂场景下进行因果推理的必要性与挑战。

Abstract: Deep learning has enabled significant advances in time-series causal inference, yet progress remains constrained by the lack of realistic benchmarks with observable counterfactual outcomes. Existing datasets either rely on real-world observations without ground-truth counterfactuals or on simplified simulations that fail to capture complex causal dynamics. To address this gap, we develop a large-scale benchmark for counterfactual prediction in epidemic time series under dynamic interventions. Unlike existing benchmarks, it supports static and time-varying treatments, as well as both single-policy and multi-policy intervention settings, enabling evaluation of causal inference methods across a broad range of causal inference scenarios. Leveraging a calibrated agent-based model grounded in real-world demographic, mobility, epidemiological, and policy data, we generate realistic counterfactual trajectories across more than 150 U.S. counties. Using this benchmark, we evaluate widely used and state-of-the-art causal inference methods, revealing substantial performance differences and highlighting the challenges of realistic time-series causal reasoning.

</details>


### [269] [Revisiting Prototype Rehearsal for Exemplar-Free Continual Learning: Manifold-Aware Boundary Sampling with Adaptive Class-Balanced Loss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05695)
*Hongye Xu,Bartosz Krawczyk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过提出流形感知重演策略（结合边界感知采样与自适应损失），克服了EFCIL中原型重演法的传统缺陷，实现了性能的显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对无样本类增量学习（EFCIL），现有观点认为原型重演（Prototype Rehearsal）因简单处理原型而存在局限，其性能被漂移补偿方法超越。本文旨在通过解决原型重演中忽视敌对类信息及类不平衡的问题，重构其竞争力。

Method: 1. 约束扩张过采样（Constrained Expansive Over-Sampling）：将旧类原型向新类中的最近邻“敌对”样本进行插值，生成具有边界感知能力的重演样本；2. 自适应类平衡损失（Adaptive Class-Balanced loss）：引入基于时间的类加权机制，根据任务阶段动态调整旧类原型的梯度贡献。

Result: 该方法在多个EFCIL基准测试上实现了最先进（SOTA）的性能，成功缩小并反超了现有的漂移补偿方法。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了原型重演范式并未过时，通过引入流形感知策略和自适应类平衡机制，能够有效解决其固有的局限性，从而在EFCIL任务中超越现有的漂移补偿方法，达到SOTA性能。

Abstract: Exemplar-free class-incremental learning (EFCIL) aims to acquire new classes over time without storing raw data. Historically, prototype rehearsal, which samples around stored class prototypes and mixes them with current-task data, has been a popular strategy to reduce catastrophic forgetting. However, recent drift-compensation methods that explicitly realign prototypes in the evolving feature space consistently outperform prototype-based rehearsal, raising the question of whether rehearsal itself is fundamentally limited. We argue that the performance gap stems not from the idea of prototype rehearsal per se, but from how it is typically instantiated: existing approaches treat prototypes as isolated class summaries that ignore information from nearby enemy classes, and fail to correct the emerging class imbalance between a handful of synthetic old-class samples and hundreds of real instances from newly introduced classes. Building on this hypothesis, we revisit prototype rehearsal and propose a manifold-aware variant that restores its competitiveness in EFCIL. First, we introduce Constrained Expansive Over-Sampling, which interpolates each old-class prototype toward its nearest enemy features from new classes, generating boundary-aware rehearsal samples that better follow the underlying data manifold while preserving inter-class separation. Second, we design an Adaptive Class-Balanced loss that performs time-based class weighting, amplifying gradients from older prototypes when they are most informative and gradually annealing their influence as richer supervision from later tasks accumulates. Together, these components turn prototype rehearsal into a drift-resilient, imbalance-aware mechanism that closes, and often reverses, the gap to recent drift-compensation methods, achieving state-of-the-art performance across multiple EFCIL benchmarks.

</details>


### [270] [Intercomparison of Machine Learning Algorithms for Remote Sensing-based In-season Crop Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05731)
*August Posch,Jitendra Kumar,Forrest M. Hoffman,Auroop R. Ganguly*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究通过结合HLS卫星影像和历史轮作数据，成功实现了在作物收获前（6月初）对其进行高精度测绘，并通过对比实验确定SVM为最佳预测算法，为实时作物监测提供了科学依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的USDA作物数据层（CDL）滞后于收获季节，无法满足作物紧急情况的实时响应需求；且目前缺乏针对跨年度变化环境下不同算法性能的全面对比研究。

Method: 1. 数据：使用Harmonized Landsat-Sentinel (HLS) 表面反射率时间序列数据与作物轮作历史信息；2. 实验：对比了10种机器学习算法及其数千种模型配置；3. 验证：采用跨年度（year-wise）交叉验证，并使用多种指标评估模型性能。

Result: 在6月初（收获前），该方法对加州杏仁和爱荷华州玉米的测绘取得了显著成果，支持向量机（SVM）表现最佳，在不可见年份的验证中，杏仁和玉米的平均F1分数分别为0.74和0.59。

Conclusion: 研究表明，利用HLS影像时间序列和作物轮作信息，可以在作物收获前（6月初）实现作物类型的准确测绘，SVM算法表现最优。尽管跨年度变差是主要不确定性来源，但该方法具备进一步提升潜力，未来可扩展至更大规模和多作物类型应用。

Abstract: In-season crop type mapping is critical for food security in the face of increasingly extreme climate-related threats to crops. Currently, the USDA Cropland Data Layer provides crop type labels at 30m resolution and is available the February after harvest, but no product exists that maps crop types before harvest with satisfactory accuracy that would allow emergency managers to respond to crop threats in near real time. Furthermore, the relative advantages of a wide range of algorithms have not been evaluated in a way that accounts for interannual variability, until this study. Here, Harmonized Landsat-Sentinel surface reflectance imagery time series and crop rotation history information are combined to map corn in Iowa and almonds in California at 30m resolution accurately by early June in unseen years, with robust quantification of uncertainty due to phenology and crop distribution. Thousands of model configurations across ten machine learning algorithms were compared using a year-wise cross-validation and a suite of metrics. Hyperparameter search revealed Support Vector Machines to be the most successful algorithm overall, with a mean F1 score of 0.74 (0.59) across five unseen validation years for almonds by early June in California (corn by early June in Iowa). Interannual variation was a large source of uncertainty, but patterns showed the potential to further improve performance with ensemble approaches or ancillary data. Future work may extend these methods to include multiclass maps of all crop types, CONUS-wide maps, and in-season crop yield forecasting.

</details>


### [271] [Beyond Soft Masks: Hard-Perturbation Mixup Explainer for Robust GNN Explainability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05756)
*Jialiang Yin,Zheng Zhao,Linsey Pang,Bo Dong,Bin Shi,Jiaxing Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 为解决 GNN 可解释性中因软掩码引发的无关信息泄露和 OOD 问题，本文提出 HPME 框架，利用图池化压缩无关信息并结合结构级 Mixup，实现了更精准、鲁棒的图模型解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 GNN 后验解释方法多使用软掩码（soft masks），难以完全消除标签无关信息的干扰，导致“分布外（OOD）”问题，限制了模型的可信度和解释保真度。

Method: 提出了硬扰动 Mixup 解释框架（HPME）：(1) 基于广义图信息瓶颈（GIB）利用图池化提取离散的解释子图，彻底压缩无关信息；(2) 引入结构级替换的 Mixup 策略，通过生成分布内（In-distribution）解释来缓解分布偏移。

Result: 在多个合成数据集和真实世界数据集上的实验证明，HPME 在生成鲁棒且具有高保真度的解释方面显著优于现有主流方法。

Conclusion: HPME 通过硬掩码和结构级 Mixup 有效解决了现有解释方法中标签无关信息干扰和分布外（OOD）问题，在鲁棒性和可解释性方面达到了 SOTA 水平。

Abstract: Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) have demonstrated remarkable performance across a range of applications involving graph-structured data, particularly in high-stakes domains. However, the opaque nature of their decision-making processes limits their trustworthiness and broader adoption. Existing post-hoc explanation methods aim to improve explainability by identifying subgraphs that influence GNN predictions and adopt mixup strategies to alleviate the out-of-distribution (OOD) issue caused by using subgraphs for prediction. Yet, these approaches typically rely on soft masks, which are inherently unable to fully eliminate label-irrelevant information, allowing redundant structures to leak into the mixup process and hindering the resolution of the OOD problem, thereby degrading explanation fidelity. In this work, we propose HPME, a Hard-Perturbation Mixup Explanation framework grounded in a generalized Graph Information Bottleneck, which leverages graph pooling to extract discrete explanatory subgraphs and to yield an information-capacity bound to thoroughly compress label-irrelevant components. Furthermore, we introduce a novel mixup strategy built upon structure-level replacement, generating in-distribution explanations to effectively mitigate the distribution shift. Extensive experiments on diverse tasks demonstrate that HPME achieves state-of-the-art performance in generating robust and interpretable explanations across both synthetic and real-world datasets.

</details>


### [272] [Domain-Adapted Small Language Models with Hybrid Post-Processing: Achieving Cost-Efficient, Low-Latency Multi-Label Structured Prediction via LoRA Fine-Tuning on Scarce Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05781)
*Srinivasan Manoharan,Dilipkumar Nallusamy,Sachin Kumar,Haifeng Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种小模型+规则导向的混合架构，在成本、速度和隐私保护方面大幅优于大模型，同时保证了结构化合规评估的高准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿大模型（LLM）在处理特定领域结构化评估任务时，面临高延迟、高成本以及数据隐私泄露的挑战。

Method: 提出了一种混合框架：使用 LLaMA 3.1 8B 作为小语言模型，应用 LoRA（仅 2.05% 可训练参数）进行微调；引入领域特定的确定性规则后处理层；采用硬负样本增强（hard-negative augmentation）技术来强化关键决策边界。

Result: 在 53 个样本的盲测中：1. 实现了 100% 的 JSON 结构有效性；2. 整体准确率达 83.0%，关键分类字段准确率达 100%；3. 推理速度比前沿模型 API 快 2-5 倍；4. 成本降低了 46-76%（每条数据仅 $0.013）。

Conclusion: 在领域特定的结构化评估任务中，通过结合参数高效微调的小模型（LoRA）与确定性规则后处理的神经符号混合方法，可以在显著降低成本、延迟和隐私风险的同时，达到甚至媲美前沿大模型的表现。

Abstract: Deploying frontier large language models (LLMs) for domain-specific structured evaluation tasks often incurs substantial latency, cost, and data privacy overhead. We present a hybrid framework that combines a fine-tuned small language model (LLaMA 3.1 8B, with only 2.05% trainable parameters via LoRA) and a deterministic rule-based post-processing layer. Trained on just 219 curated examples, the system is applied to multi-label compliance evaluation of conversational transcripts spanning 18 heterogeneous output fields. In blind evaluation on 53 previously unseen production transcripts, it achieves 100% JSON structural validity, 83.0% human-validated overall accuracy, and 100% accuracy on the most critical classification field. The proposed approach formalizes a hybrid neural-symbolic decomposition and introduces targeted hard-negative augmentation to improve performance on critical decision boundaries. Running on a single NVIDIA A100 GPU, inference completes in approximately 2 seconds, which is 2-5x faster than frontier-model APIs. The system costs only $0.013 per evaluation compared with $0.025-$0.055 for proprietary alternatives, resulting in 46-76% cost savings. These results demonstrate that domain-adapted small language models, when combined with deterministic post-processing, can match frontier-model accuracy for structured compliance evaluation while substantially reducing operational cost, latency, and privacy risk.
  Keywords: small language models, parameter-efficient fine-tuning, LoRA, domain adaptation, hybrid inference, compliance evaluation, structured output.

</details>


### [273] [CaliDist: Calibrating Large Language Models via Behavioral Robustness to Distraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05799)
*Mohammad Anas Jawad,Cornelia Caragea*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为CaliDist的方法，通过量化LLM在面对语义干扰时的预测稳定性来调整模型置信度，从而显著提升了模型校准效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM校准方法忽略了模型在面对无关或误导性信息时的“行为稳健性”（behavioral robustness）；真正的模型信心应反映其在认知压力下的稳定性。

Method: 提出了一种名为CaliDist的事后校准方法。该方法通过在输入提示中添加语义干扰项，定量评估模型预测和不确定性的变化，并将这种稳定性信号作为权重，对模型初始置信度得分进行自适应缩放。

Result: 在7个自然语言理解分类基准和6种不同的LLM上进行了测试，CaliDist在ECE和Brier Score指标上均优于基线方法。平均而言，将ECE从23%降低至7%，相对提升达70%。

Conclusion: 行为稳定性是LLM校准的一个强有力的信号。通过引入衡量模型对无关干扰信息敏感度的校准方法，可以显著提升模型的置信度准确性。

Abstract: Existing calibration methods for Large Language Models (LLMs) often overlook a critical dimension of trustworthiness: a model's {\em behavioral robustness} to irrelevant or misleading information. In this paper, we argue that a model's true confidence should reflect its stability under cognitive pressure. We introduce \textsc{CaliDist}, a novel post-hoc calibration approach that directly measures and penalizes a model's susceptibility to distraction. \textsc{CaliDist} quantifies how an LLM's predictions and uncertainty change when its input prompt is perturbed with semantic \textit{distractors}. This stability (or lack thereof) signal is then used to adaptively scale the model's initial confidence score. Our extensive experiments on seven Natural Language Understanding classification benchmarks using six distinct LLMs show that \textsc{CaliDist} consistently achieves lower Expected Calibration Error (ECE) and Brier Score compared with strong baselines. Remarkably, our method reduces the ECE from 23\% to 7\% on average--a relative improvement of 70\%--demonstrating that behavioral stability is a powerful signal for calibration. We make our code and datasets available at github.com/m-anas-j/CaliDist.

</details>


### [274] [SALT: When More Rollouts Don't Help in Group-Based Policy Optimization and How to Make Them Matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05800)
*Powei Chang,Jinpeng Zhang,Chaoqun Sun,MiniWell Tsao,Lianrui Li,Jianxiang Xiang,Chenyu Wang,Yukang Gao,Dongying Kong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对 GRPO 在强化学习中因梯度低秩坍缩导致的无效抵消问题，提出 SALT 插件，通过对梯度空间进行自适应分解与重加权，优化了参数更新效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通过 GRPO 进行强化学习时，增加采样数量往往因策略梯度特征集中在低秩符号几何中，导致聚合时出现严重的无效抵消，从而限制了学习效率。

Method: 提出了 SALT (Subspace-Adaptive geometry pLug-in componenT)，该插件通过分析小批量样本的 Gram 矩阵几何特征，将组内相对更新梯度分解为共享子空间和残差分量，并对残差部分进行动态放大。

Result: 在多个推理任务基准测试及不同模型规模下，SALT 在不增加采样成本或修改奖励模型的情况下，显著改善了更新几何结构并提升了模型推理性能。

Conclusion: SALT 通过对梯度几何形状进行自适应重加权，解决了 GRPO 在大规模采样时出现的梯度抵消和低秩坍缩问题，有效提升了强化学习策略的优化质量。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) often adopts GRPO-style group-relative updates, sampling multiple rollouts per prompt to construct normalized learning signals. However, merely increasing the number of rollouts does not reliably strengthen learning: under GRPO-style group normalization, per-rollout policy-gradient features can concentrate into a low-rank, signed geometry, causing substantial cancellation during aggregation and weakening the effective update. We address this failure mode with SALT, a Subspace-Adaptive geometry pLug-in componenT that uses sample-wise gradient geometry to reweight the coefficients of group-relative updates. SALT estimates a dominant shared subspace from the mini-batch Gram geometry, decomposes group-relative coefficients into shared and residual channels, and adaptively amplifies the residual channel when signed cancellation is severe. Across diverse reasoning-oriented RLVR benchmarks and model scales, SALT improves effective update geometry and performance without modifying the reward model or the rollout sampling procedure

</details>


### [275] [Robust and sparse support vector machine via hybrid truncated loss for supervised classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05814)
*Yuliang Yang,Chen Chen,Yuxiang Liu,Huiru Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Lht-SVM的混合截断损失函数模型，解决了SVM对异常值敏感及计算代价高的问题，并通过高效的ADMM算法在单、多视图学习任务中实现了精度与鲁棒性的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的SVM损失函数（如Hinge损失和最小二乘损失）对异常值敏感，而非凸有界损失函数往往伴随着较高的计算开销，难以兼顾鲁棒性与计算效率。

Method: 1. 提出了混合截断损失函数（Lht-SVM）；2. 引入P-平稳点并确立了一阶最优性条件；3. 设计了基于工作集策略的交替方向乘子法（ADMM）；4. 扩展至多视图学习（MvLht-SVM），结合了结构信息和视图权重的学习策略。

Result: 在单视图实验中，Lht-SVM比现有方法具有更好的抗噪性和更少的支持向量；在多视图实验中，MvLht-SVM在精度、召回率和F1分数等指标上均优于现有的6种主流多视图学习方法。

Conclusion: 提出了一种兼具稀疏性和有界性的混合截断损失函数（Lht），通过理论证明了其最优性条件，并设计了高效的求解算法。该方法在单视图和多视图分类任务中均表现出优异的鲁棒性和识别精度。

Abstract: The support vector machine (SVM) is a widely used classifier, but choosing an appropriate loss function remains difficult. Convex losses such as the hinge loss and least-squares loss are sensitive to outliers, while bounded non-convex losses often lead to high computational cost. To address this, we propose a hybrid truncated loss function ($L_{\mathrm{ht}}$) that is both sparse and bounded, and build the $L_{\mathrm{ht}}$-SVM model for single-view classification. We introduce the P-stationary point and use it to establish the first-order necessary and sufficient optimality conditions. Based on these conditions, we design an alternating direction method of multipliers with a working-set strategy that reduces computational cost and achieves global convergence. We further extend $L_{\mathrm{ht}}$-SVM to multi-view learning by adding structural information and view weights, resulting in Mv$L_{\mathrm{ht}}$-SVM, which follows both the consensus and complementarity principles. Experiments on synthetic, real-world, and image datasets show that $L_{\mathrm{ht}}$-SVM achieves higher accuracy with fewer support vectors and better noise robustness than five single-view methods, while Mv$L_{\mathrm{ht}}$-SVM outperforms six multi-view methods in accuracy, precision, recall, and F1-score.

</details>


### [276] [Consistency Training Along the Transformer Stack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05817)
*Sukrati Gautam,Neil Shah,Arav Dhoot,Bryan Maruyama,Caroline Wei,Rohan Kapoor,Robert Sidey,Prakhar Gupta,Zi Cheng Huang,David Demitri Africa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过引入MLP和注意力一致性目标，将一致性训练扩展至多种新安全威胁，证实了该框架在统一应对模型病态方面的有效性、泛化能力及其底层机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管一致性训练在减少模型失准方面表现出潜力，但现有研究多局限于谄媚和越狱场景。本文旨在验证一致性训练在更广泛的安全威胁中的有效性，并探索其更底层的机制。

Method: 1. 引入两种内部一致性目标：MLP 一致性训练 (MLPCT) 和注意力一致性训练 (AttCT)；
2. 将一致性训练扩展至四种新的安全威胁：角色扮演上下文学习攻击、对抗性挫败、预填充攻击和条件性失准；
3. 结合既有的行为一致性训练（BCT），进行跨模型和威胁场景的实证研究，并分析其背后的机制（残差流）。

Result: 1. 一致性训练能显著减少多种新的安全威胁，效果超越了传统的谄媚和越狱防御；
2. 观察到“跨威胁泛化”现象，即针对一种失效模式的防御能增强对其他问题的鲁棒性；
3. 发现ACT、MLPCT和AttCT共享残差流机制，而BCT在机制上有所不同。

Conclusion: 一致性训练（Consistency Training）是一个灵活且可扩展的对齐框架，能够统一应对多种模型病态，具有跨威胁泛化能力。其核心机制主要依赖于残差流操作，但在实现层面（如BCT与ACT/MLPCT/AttCT）存在机制差异。

Abstract: Consistency training encourages models to behave similarly across different contexts, and has shown promise for reducing misalignment. We broaden the scope of consistency training in two ways. First, we introduce two new internal consistency targets: MLP Consistency Training (MLPCT), which matches post-activation MLP states, and Attention Consistency Training (AttCT), which matches per-head attention distributions. Second, we apply consistency training to four additional safety threats: persona in-context learning attacks, adversarial frustration, prefill attacks, and conditional misalignment. Across several models and threat settings, we find that consistency training reduces misalignment well beyond the sycophancy and jailbreak settings studied in prior work. We also find cases of cross-threat generalization, where training against one failure mode improves robustness to another, and identify a shared residual-stream mechanism underlying ACT, MLPCT, and AttCT, while distinguishing BCT as mechanistically distinct. Our results suggest that consistency training is a flexible and extensible framework for alignment, capable of unifying defenses against a broader class of model pathologies.

</details>


### [277] [GenAutoML: An Agentic Framework for Dynamic Architecture Generation and Optimization in Time-Series Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05860)
*Oleeviya Babu Poikarayil,Cédric Schockaert,Abdulrahman Nahhas,Christian Daase,Mursal Dawodi,Jawid Ahmad Baktash*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了 GenAutoML，一个利用 LLM 作为神经网络架构师的代理框架，能够自动生成高效、安全且具有强鲁棒性的时间序列预测模型，特别适用于边缘 AI 的轻量化落地场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 AutoML 系统受限于预定义的搜索空间，无法灵活适应多样化的时间序列数据，且时间序列神经网络架构的设计过程耗费资源且依赖专家经验。

Method: 1. 架构构建：利用大语言模型 (LLMs) 作为神经网络架构师；2. 迭代改进：采用“沙箱反射循环 (Sandboxed Reflection Loop)”进行代码自动精炼；3. 运行时管理：使用“签名感知运行时 (Signature-Aware Runtime)”确保架构一致性与安全性；4. 鲁棒性优化：引入动态可逆实例归一化 (Dyn-RevIN) 处理非平稳时间序列数据。

Result: 1. 在 ETTh1、ETTm1 和 Weather 数据集上表现出色；2. 生成的 WaveInterferenceNet 模型单样本推理延迟低于 0.01 ms，且具有极高的预测性能；3. 成功实现了针对特定任务的架构自动生成与部署。

Conclusion: GenAutoML 通过利用大语言模型实现自动化的神经网络架构生成，在保持高性能的同时实现了极高的计算效率（如 WaveInterferenceNet），为资源受限的边缘 AI 部署提供了一种可行且高效的自动化方案。

Abstract: Designing neural architectures for time-series forecasting and anomaly detection remains a resource-intensive task that often requires substantial domain expertise. Traditional Automated Machine Learning (AutoML) systems typically rely on static, predefined search spaces, limiting their ability to adapt to diverse data characteristics. We present GenAutoML, an agentic framework that leverages Large Language Models (LLMs) as neural architects to bridge natural-language requirements and executable PyTorch implementations. The framework incorporates a Sandboxed Reflection Loop for autonomous code refinement and a Signature-Aware Runtime that enforces architectural consistency and execution safety. To improve robustness under non-stationary conditions, we further introduce a Dynamic Reversible Instance Normalization (Dyn-RevIN) wrapper. Experiments on the ETTh1, ETTm1, and Weather benchmarks demonstrate that GenAutoML can dynamically generate task-specific neural architectures tailored to dataset characteristics. Among the generated models, WaveInterferenceNet achieves inference latency below 0.01 ms per sample while maintaining competitive predictive performance. By emphasizing computational efficiency, architectural adaptability, and stable optimization behavior, GenAutoML enables the creation of ultra-lightweight neural networks suitable for resource-constrained and latency-sensitive Edge AI deployments.

</details>


### [278] [Deciphering Two Training Clocks in Grokking via Deep Linear Network Theory with Conditional ReLU Reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05863)
*Hu Tan,Kuo Gai,Shihua Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究将Grokking现象形式化为“双时钟”模型，揭示了神经网络训练中快速分类拟合与缓慢表征简化之间的时间尺度分离，并为ReLU MLP中的该现象提供了理论支撑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究神经网络中“Grokking”现象的本质，即为什么模型在完全拟合训练数据很久之后，其泛化能力还会出现“突然”的提升。旨在解释复杂非线性网络学习动态中，拟合（fitting）与表征简化（simplification）之间的时间尺度分离。

Method: 通过引入“双时钟（two training clocks）”概念，结合深度线性网络理论、Schatten范数正则化分析、Kurdyka-Lojasiewicz尾部条件，以及针对ReLU MLP的主动坐标（active coordinates）分析和链式法则梯度估计。

Result: 证明了深度线性网络中交叉熵损失会在对数时间内降至epsilon，而结构能量（正则化项）在多项式时间内收敛。在ReLU模型中，通过分析激活模式的稳定性证明了分类器层比嵌入层获得更大有效梯度的机制，从而支撑了“先拟合、后简化”的两阶段学习模型。

Conclusion: “Grokking”现象是由于学习过程存在两个不同时间尺度的时钟所致：分类器快速拟合数据（对数时间尺度），而表征的简化则较慢（多项式时间尺度）。这种机制在深度线性网络中是严格成立的，在ReLU MLP中表现为分阶段的简化过程。

Abstract: Grokking suggests that fitting the training data and learning a simple underlying rule may occur on different time scales. We formalize this phenomenon by separating the fast decay of the classification loss from the slower simplification of the learned representation, and we call the resulting pair of stopping times two training clocks. For deep linear networks, we show that a post-margin gap-growth or one-step tail-contraction condition reduces the cross-entropy loss to level epsilon on a logarithmic time scale. In contrast, when layerwise weight decay is present, the induced regularization on the end-to-end map can be expressed as a Schatten-type penalty; under a sharp late-time Kurdyka-Lojasiewicz tail, this structural energy closes on a polynomial time scale. The two clocks, therefore, separate fitting from representation simplification. We then explain how the same mechanism can appear in ReLU MLPs. In regions where the activation patterns on the training set remain fixed, the network reduces to a linear model in the active coordinates. In a two-layer ReLU embedding model, chain-rule estimates further show that the classifier head can receive larger effective gradients than the embedding block under controlled downstream norms. This supports a two-stage mechanism in which the classifier fits first, while the representation continues to simplify later. We use modular addition as the main experimental setting. The deep linear theory provides the rigorous core of the analysis. But the ReLU results are formulated as conditional reductions that account for empirical behavior without claiming a global proof for nonlinear training dynamics.

</details>


### [279] [TS-ICL: A Flexible Time-Indexed Foundation Model for Time Series via In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05878)
*Etienne Le Naour,Tahar Nabil,Adrien Petralia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TS-ICL 是一种新型 Transformer 架构，通过上下文学习统一了时间序列的预测与插补，在复杂采样条件和不完整数据下表现出优于现有方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的基础模型主要集中于时间序列预测，缺乏对现实世界中常见的非规则、部分观测数据的处理能力（如插补和复杂采样条件），亟需统一的通用零样本模型来解决这些问题。

Method: 引入了名为 TS-ICL 的概率型上下文学习（In-Context Learning）编码器-回归器 Transformer。该方法将时间序列任务建模为时间戳对齐的回归问题，并通过基于因果数据先验生成的合成依赖结构来整合协变量。

Result: TS-ICL 在缺失值插补方面达到了业界领先水平，同时在单变量和协变量感知预测任务中与现有顶级基础模型表现持平，特别是在处理具有部分观测回溯窗口的预测场景时表现出显著优势。

Conclusion: TS-ICL 是一种通用的时间序列基础模型，能够统一处理预测和插补任务，并展示了在大规模、复杂时间序列环境下比现有专用模型更强的泛化能力和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Foundation models mark a profound paradigm shift in time series modeling, with task-specific models being superseded by general-purpose zero-shot models. Yet, current approaches primarily focus on forecasting, while real-world time series are often irregularly and partially observed, requiring models that can jointly forecast, impute missing values, and handle degraded sampling conditions. To address these challenges, we introduce TS-ICL, a novel probabilistic In-Context Learning encoder--regressor Transformer that unifies forecasting and imputation. TS-ICL formulates time series tasks as timestamp-aligned regression and naturally incorporates covariates by training on synthetic dependency structures generated from a novel causal data prior. Empirically, TS-ICL achieves a new state-of-the-art in imputation, while remaining competitive with leading forecasting foundation models across both univariate and covariate-aware benchmarks. It shows particularly strong performance in forecasting with partially observed look-back windows.

</details>


### [280] [When Denser Credit Is Not Enough: Evidence-Calibrated Policy Optimization for Long-Horizon LLM Agent Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05885)
*Yuanfan Li,Qi Zhou,Wenjing Duan,Lu Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对长程任务中现有RL方法因样本噪声导致的训练不稳问题，ECPO通过对步级信用进行统计校准与方差过滤，显著提升了LLM智能体的学习效率与稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程LLM智能体在稀疏、延迟奖励下的强化学习存在信用分配难题。现有方法（如GiGPO）通过锚点状态构建步级优势，但在采样不足时容易受到随机“幸运”动作的干扰，导致锚点偏差和训练振荡。

Method: 提出了ECPO（证据校准策略优化）算法，包含两项核心技术：1) 证据校准动作优势（Evidence-Calibrated Action Advantage）：通过按规范动作对轨迹进行分组并收缩低样本量估计；2) 方差门控信用加权（Variance-Gated Credit Weighting）：抑制受动作内噪声影响较大的锚点状态。该方法为无批评者（critic-free）架构。

Result: 在ALFWorld和WebShop基准测试中（使用Qwen2.5-1.5B/7B模型），ECPO表现优于多个强基线，在Qwen2.5-1.5B上分别比GiGPO提升了5.2和7.3个成功点，且计算额外开销仅为0.1%。

Conclusion: ECPO通过引入证据校准动作优势和方差门控信用加权，有效解决了长程LLM智能体在稀疏奖励下训练不稳定的问题，在保持极低计算开销的同时，显著优于现有的GiGPO等基线方法。

Abstract: Long-horizon LLM agents require reinforcement learning methods that can assign credit to intermediate decisions under sparse and delayed rewards. Recent group-based methods such as GiGPO improve over GRPO by constructing step-level advantages at repeated anchor states. However, we show that such dense credit can be statistically unreliable: under limited rollouts, rare but lucky actions may receive overly large advantages, producing divergent anchor bias and late-stage training oscillation. We propose Evidence-Calibrated Policy Optimization (ECPO), a critic-free policy optimization algorithm that calibrates step-level credit before policy updates. ECPO combines Evidence-Calibrated Action Advantage, which groups rollouts by canonical actions and shrinks low-count estimates, with Variance-Gated Credit Weighting, which suppresses anchor states dominated by within-action noise. Experiments on ALFWorld and WebShop with Qwen2.5-1.5B/7B show that ECPO consistently outperforms strong baselines, improving GiGPO by +5.2/+7.3 success points on ALFWorld/WebShop with Qwen2.5-1.5B while adding only 0.1% additional advantage-computation overhead.

</details>


### [281] [High-Dimensional Theory of LoRA Fine-Tuning in a Solvable Attention Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05899)
*O. Duranthon,F. Boncoraglio,L. Zdeborová*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文建立了LoRA在高维统计下的数学框架，解析了预训练与微调的交互作用，为优化预训练过程和主动微调策略提供了理论支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了理解大模型微调中低秩适应（LoRA）的机制，特别是探讨预训练阶段与微调阶段之间的相互作用及其对模型性能的影响。

Method: 构建了一个可解析的高维统计框架，模拟单头注意力层在数据充足任务上的预训练和在有限数据上的秩一（rank-one）LoRA更新。利用高维极限下的有限序参数（order parameters）对这两个阶段进行精确的渐近表征。

Result: 获得了测试误差和表征对齐的精确预测公式。导出了最优预训练过程的指导原则，并证明了模型性能（测试误差）与表征质量（representation quality）在某些区域内可能不匹配。

Conclusion: 预训练对LoRA的影响可被概括为一个有效的噪声项。研究揭示了测试误差与表征质量之间可能存在偏差的机制，并基于该理论提出了主动微调（active fine-tuning）的策略。

Abstract: We develop a high-dimensional statistical theory of low-rank adaptation (LoRA) in attention models, capturing the interplay between pre-training and fine-tuning. We introduce a solvable framework in which a single-head attention layer is first pre-trained on a data-abundant task and subsequently adapted via a rank-one LoRA update on limited data. In the high-dimensional limit, both stages admit a sharp asymptotic characterization in terms of a finite set of order parameters, yielding explicit predictions for test errors and representation alignment. Our analysis shows that the impact of pre-training on LoRA is summarized by an effective noise term, from which we derive prescriptions for the optimal pre-training procedure. We also demonstrate a regime with a mismatch between the value of the test error and representation quality, and propose an application of our theory to active fine-tuning.

</details>


### [282] [Addressing Imbalance in Multi-Label Data via Label-Specific Distance-based Oversampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05927)
*Bin Liu,Jun Wu,Haoyu Peng,Ao Zhou,Jin Wang,QiaoSong Chen,Grigorios Tsoumakas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对多标签分类中传统过采样方法导致标签混淆的问题，提出 LSDMLO 方法，利用标签特异性距离实现高质量的数据平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在处理多标签不平衡时主要依赖欧氏距离，忽略了不同特征对不同标签的语义相关性，导致邻近样本出现标签不一致，进而产生标签混淆和模型过拟合。

Method: 提出了一种新的采样方法 LSDMLO（Label-Specific Distance-based Multi-Label Oversampling）。该方法通过计算加权的相关特征空间来导出标签特异性距离，从而识别标签一致的邻居；同时筛选能够表达更多边缘区域标签关联的种子实例，以生成更符合原始标签分布的合成实例。

Result: 实验表明，在多种基分类器下，LSDMLO 在处理多标签不平衡问题时，相比对比的最先进方法具有更好的性能。

Conclusion: LSDMLO 通过引入标签特异性距离（label-specific distance）和加权相关特征空间，有效解决了多标签分类中因邻域信息不一致导致的标签混淆和过拟合问题，在处理多标签数据不平衡方面表现优于现有主流方法。

Abstract: The complex imbalanced label distribution poses a crucial challenge to multi-label classification, as most classifiers are biased towards the majority class and high-frequent labels. Oversampling is an efficient and flexible solution that augments instances to provide a more balanced training dataset for multi-label classifiers. Most existing oversampling methods create synthetic instances in a heuristic way that essentially relies on neighborhood information retrieved using Euclidean distance within the entire feature space. However, they fail to consider the varying semantic relevance of features to different labels, leading to label inconsistency among proximate neighbors and further introducing label confusion and overfitting to synthetic instances. To overcome the above issue, we propose a novel sampling approach called Label-Specific Distance-based Multi-Label Oversampling (LSDMLO) that creates more useful and well-labeled synthetic instances to address the imbalance in multi-label datasets. LSDMLO derives the label-specific distance to identify label-consistent neighbors based on the weighted pertinent feature space, which facilitates selecting seed instances that express more label correlations in boundary areas and generating synthetic instances aligned with the label distribution of original data. The comprehensive experiments verify that the proposed LSDMLO outperforms the state-of-the-art multi-label sampling approaches under various base classifiers.

</details>


### [283] [Short paper: Models in the dark -- Rectification and erasure under GDPR in ML supply chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05946)
*Henrik Graßhoff,Malte Hansen,Meiko Jensen,Sara Ramezanian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过跨学科视角调研了GDPR下ML模型的纠正与删除权实施难题，指出复杂供应链中缺乏透明度的下游模型（“黑暗中的模型”）是合规的关键障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决GDPR规定的纠正权和删除权在ML领域实施困难的问题，现有研究多将法律与技术割裂，并忽视了ML供应链中多主体协作带来的复杂性与不透明性。

Method: 通过跨学科分析，综合学术文献与数据保护机构的指导意见，对ML系统中数据主体权利的实施挑战进行了调研，并提出了“黑暗中的模型”（models in the dark）这一概念来分析复杂供应链中的合规困境。

Result: 研究发现许多GDPR要求在当前技术实践中暂无法落地，且现有的ML供应链研究不足。引入“黑暗中的模型”概念，揭示了下游模型在缺乏透明度和可追溯性情况下所面临的紧迫监管与技术风险。

Conclusion: GDPR中的纠正权和删除权在机器学习（ML）系统中的实施面临巨大挑战。为实现可信人工智能，必须通过跨学科方法解决ML供应链中的监管与技术衔接问题，特别是要关注下游“黑暗中的模型”带来的合规性风险。

Abstract: The rights to rectification and erasure, as established under the General Data Protection Regulation (GDPR), are central to protecting individuals' privacy. However, their effective enforcement in machine learning (ML) systems remains challenging. Existing work has largely addressed these rights from either a legal or a technical perspective in isolation and disregards the fact that models are produced in complex supply chains involving multiple actors across development, distribution, and deployment. This paper presents a holistic survey of challenges in implementing the rights to rectification and erasure in ML models. Drawing on academic literature and guidance from data protection authorities, we find that many GDPR requirements cannot yet be technically met in practice. Our findings further suggest that issues arising in ML supply chains are insufficiently addressed in research. To tackle this gap, we introduce the notion of models in the dark -- derived models created further downstream in an ML chain without sufficient transparency or traceability -- and analyse the urgent challenges posed by this phenomenon. By adopting an interdisciplinary perspective, this work contributes to bridging the gap between legal requirements and the technical implementation of data subject rights in ML, ultimately supporting the development of trustworthy artificial intelligence.

</details>


### [284] [Steering Vectors are an Adversarial Attack Surface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05958)
*Abzal Aidakhmetov,Donato Crisostomi,Tommaso Mencattini,Adrian Robert Minut,Iacopo Masi,Emanuele Rodolà*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究揭示了激活控制（Activation steering）技术中的隐蔽投毒漏洞，攻击者可通过替换小比例数据操纵控制向量，实现模型越狱，并验证了一种有效的正交化防御手段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激活控制（Activation steering）作为一种无需微调即可操纵LLM行为的流行技术，其依赖于社区共享数据集和预计算向量。研究旨在揭示这种共享机制中存在的“隐蔽数据投毒攻击”风险，即恶意攻击者可以通过篡改数据导致下游模型越狱，并验证相应的防御对策。

Method: 1. 攻击方法：通过将操纵数据集中的4-6%的令牌替换为恶意构造的数据，使生成的操纵向量（steering vector）偏向“反拒绝”方向，实现模型越狱。
2. 验证方案：在两个开源大模型家族和八种模型属性组合上测试攻击效果。
3. 防御方案：提出“拒绝方向正交化（refusal-direction orthogonalization）”防御策略，旨在消除操纵向量中导致拒绝行为的组件。

Result: 1. 攻击效果：被投毒的控制向量在测试中实现了20%-55%的绝对攻击成功率（ASR），对比正常控制向量提升了19%至51%。
2. 防御效果：采用拒绝方向正交化防御后，可恢复约82%的抗攻击表现，且不会明显损害模型的正常功能性能。

Conclusion: 激活控制（Activation steering）管道存在严重的潜在安全风险，即隐蔽数据投毒攻击。通过在操纵数据集中注入少量恶意令牌，攻击者可诱导模型产生“反拒绝”行为。该研究验证了攻击有效性，并提出了基于拒绝方向正交化的防御机制，在缓解攻击的同时可保持模型原有功能。

Abstract: Activation steering has become a popular way to control Large Language Model (LLM) behavior without fine-tuning. Since the technique is plug-and-play, users share datasets and precomputed vectors to steer model activations. However, we show that a \emph{stealth data poisoning attack} silently compromises this pipeline. By substituting $4{-}6\%$ of tokens in the steering dataset, an attacker can silently align the resulting vector with an anti-refusal direction. This jailbreaks the target model while preserving the intended steering effect on benign prompts. Under this threat model, a malicious actor can distribute an apparently safe bundle containing texts, vectors, and weights, alongside an equivalence certificate that the end-user can verify. We test the attack on two open-weight model families and eight model-attribute combinations, observing that poisoned vectors reach an absolute attack success rate (ASR) of $20{-}55\%$, $+19\%$ to $+51\%$ over a clean reference. Finally, we find that a refusal-direction orthogonalization defense can recover ${\approx}82\%$ of the ASR gap without harming benign behavior.

</details>


### [285] [LLM Explainability with Counterfactual Chains and Causal Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05972)
*Nirit Nussbaum-Hoffer,Nitay Calderon,Liat Ein-Dor,Roi Reichart*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过四阶段构建因果图的方法，旨在透明化LLM的推理机制，利用反事实增强技术从LLM的预测中提取出人类可理解的概念依赖关系，从而实现对LLM的深度可解释性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多利用LLM去恢复外部过程的因果图，而本文旨在通过因果图去建模LLM本身的推理机制，以解决模型决策过程的“黑盒”问题，提升可解释性。

Method: 提出了一套四阶段方法：1. 识别类判别性与人类可解释的概念；2. 将输入映射为LLM感知的概念状态；3. 采用受MCMC启发的反事实增强程序来扩展稀疏观测数据；4. 使用σ-CG进行稳健的因果发现。

Result: 在疾病诊断、情感分析及LLM辅助判断等三个任务中验证了该方法。实验表明，所发现的因果图能够准确反映LLM的推理依赖关系，并具备良好的预测保真度与结构稳定性。

Conclusion: 该研究为LLM提供了概念级的可解释性基础，证明了通过因果图能够透明地展现模型在推理过程中的概念组织与依赖关系。

Abstract: Causal graphs provide a high-level language for making mechanisms transparent. Recent work uses Large Language Models (LLMs) to recover causal graphs of external-world processes. Instead, in this paper, we use causal graphs to model LLM inference itself, providing stakeholders with a transparent view of how the model perceives and organizes high-level concepts to produce a prediction. We propose a four-phase method for constructing such graphs. Given a target LLM and a set of textual examples, our method discovers class-discriminative, human-interpretable concepts and maps each input to LLM-perceived concept states. We then introduce an MCMC-inspired counterfactual augmentation procedure that expands the sparse observational data through chains of counterfactuals. This enables stable causal discovery with $σ$-CG, yielding informative, interpretable graphs. We apply our method to three LLMs across disease diagnosis, sentiment analysis, and LLM-as-a-judge classification tasks. We evaluate the learned graphs for predictive fidelity and structural stability, and the MCMC-inspired augmentation for convergence and downstream utility. Our results show that the discovered causal graphs capture meaningful dependencies consistent with LLMs' reasoning. Together, this paper provides a foundation for concept-level explainability of LLMs.

</details>


### [286] [Compress-Distill: Reasoning Trace Compression for Efficient Knowledge Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05988)
*Maxime Griot,Paul Steven Scotti,Tanishq Mathew Abraham*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究探讨了对大模型推理思维链（CoT）进行压缩以优化知识蒸馏的方法。结果显示，虽然压缩能够显著提升模型训练与推理效率，但往往伴随下游准确率的损失。该方法提供了一个平衡性能与效率的手段，而非完全取代原始长追踪的优化方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理模型生成的长思维链（Chain-of-Thought）导致蒸馏成本高昂，且倾向于使学生模型产生冗长输出。研究的主要目的是探讨在知识蒸馏前，对这些推理追踪进行后验压缩，以期在保持模型推理能力的同时，提升训练和推理效率。

Method: 1. 数据采集：利用两个教师模型（Qwen3.5-397B-A17B 和 gpt-oss-120B）生成约283k条正确的思维链。
2. 压缩处理：使用两个指令微调模型对长思维链进行后验压缩，将其长度缩短至原始字符长度的8.6%-21.0%。
3. 对比实验：通过48组主实验网格和7组Qwen模型截断消融实验，对比原始追踪、压缩追踪与简单截断追踪在训练资源消耗、训练/推理速度以及下游准确率上的表现。

Result: 1. 效率提升：压缩追踪将训练Token量减少至原先的12%-30%，训练速度提升2.0-7.6倍，推理输出长度缩短3-19倍。
2. 性能表现：与原始追踪训练的模型相比，使用压缩追踪的学生模型在不同规模下均表现出准确率下降，最高保留了约96%的原始准确率。
3. 压缩有效性：对比实验表明，模型自主压缩的效果优于单纯的截断处理，尤其对较小规模的学生模型更有利。

Conclusion: 推理追踪压缩是一种“准确性-效率”的权衡方案，而非无损优化。虽然压缩后的追踪能够显著提升模型训练和推理效率（训练速度提升至7.6倍，推理效率提升至18倍），并且模型压缩优于简单的截断，但原始冗长的思维链在下游准确率上仍占据优势。对于小参数模型，压缩能缩小差距，但目前无法超越原始追踪的表现。

Abstract: Reasoning models produce long chain-of-thought traces that are costly to distill and encourage verbose student outputs. We study post-hoc compression of such traces before knowledge distillation. Two teachers, Qwen3.5-397B-A17B and gpt-oss-120B, generate about 283k correct traces each; two instruction-tuned models then compress them to 8.6-21.0% of their original character length. Across a 48-run main grid plus seven Qwen-teacher truncation ablations, compressed traces reduce training tokens to 12-30% of raw, speed up training by 2.0-7.6x, and shorten inference outputs by 3-19x with smaller reductions under the shorter gpt-oss teacher. However, raw traces retain the highest downstream accuracy at every scale and for both teachers. A length-matched raw-trace truncation ablation shows that compression is not merely benefiting from a smaller token budget: model-compressed traces usually beat or match naive truncation, especially for smaller students, while maintaining shorter inference outputs. Overall, reasoning-trace compression offers an accuracy-efficiency trade-off rather than a free improvement: students retain up to 96% of raw-trace accuracy while gaining up to 18x higher per-token efficiency, and at the 0.8B scale under LoRA compressed traces narrow the raw-vs-compressed gap but do not exceed raw.

</details>


### [287] [HoT-SSM:Higher-order Temporal Knowledge Graph Reasoning with State Space Models for Health Care](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05994)
*Thummaluru Siddartha Reddy,Vempalli Naga Sai Saketh,Yash Punjabi,Mahesh Chandran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对临床任务中高阶交互建模不足及长期时间依赖缺失的问题，提出了 HoT-SSM 模型，通过动态超图与状态空间模型的结合，有效提升了 EHR 数据的预测表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 MKG 的方法在建模临床概念间复杂的成对关系上存在局限，无法充分描述高阶交互；此外，现有方法难以有效处理 EHR 中的时间跨度，往往丢失关键的长期临床依赖信息。

Method: 提出 HoT-SSM 模型：1. 利用域知识构建超图（Hypergraph）建模访视内的临床概念高阶关联；2. 引入基于动态超图的状态空间模型（State Space Model），建模患者潜在状态随时间的演变，从而捕捉长距离时间依赖。

Result: 在 MIMIC-III 和 MIMIC-IV 数据集上的实验结果表明，HoT-SSM 在执行临床预测任务时，性能超过了现有的最先进模型（SOTA）。

Conclusion: HoT-SSM 通过联合建模高阶临床交互和长距离时间依赖，在大规模临床数据集（MIMIC-III/IV）上显著优于当前主流方法，验证了该建模范式在医疗预测任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Medical knowledge graphs (MKGs) infused with clinical knowledge have been increasingly used to model electronic health records (EHRs) to support interpretable predictions in healthcare domain. However, existing MKG-based approaches are limited in capturing pairwise relations between clinical concepts (e.g., conditions, procedures, and medications), and restricts their ability to model higher-order interactions among co-occurring or semantically related concepts. In addition, most representation learning methods that leverage MKGs either collapse temporal information across visits or lack an explicit mechanism for modeling long-range temporal dependencies, which is critical for clinical tasks such as mortality prediction. To mitigate these limitations, we propose HoT-SSM, a parameter efficient and higher-order temporal graph reasoning with state space models. For each visit, HoT-SSM constructs hypergraphs by grouping semantically related clinical concepts into hyperedges using domain knowledge, thereby preserving visit-level clinical context. Further, to model the temporal dynamics while learning the representations, we introduce a novel dynamic hypergraph-based state space model that explicitly captures patients latent state evolution over time while preserving long-range information. The learned representations are used for downstream clinical prediction and reasoning. Experiments on MIMIC-III and MIMIC-IV datasets shows significant performance improvement over the current state-of-the-art models, demonstrating the effectiveness of jointly modeling higher-order clinical interactions and long-range temporal dependencies.

</details>


### [288] [Diffusion Models for Adaptive Sequential Data Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06007)
*Haoyang Cao,Minshuo Chen,Yinbin Han,Renyuan Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对传统扩散模型生成序列数据缺乏自适应性的挑战，本文提出了一种基于前向-后向扩散的新框架，实现了无未来信息偏见的时间序列生成，并通过理论证明与实证应用验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型在处理静态数据时表现出色，但在处理序列数据时难以有效捕捉时间依赖性和信息结构，尤其是难以实现“自适应”（即生成过程中不提前获知未来信息）。

Method: 提出了一种序列化前向-后向扩散框架（Sequential Forward-Backward Diffusion），通过条件化历史数据逐步进行噪声注入与移除以实现“适应性”（无未来偏见）；创新性地引入了一种用于高效并行训练的得分匹配（Score-matching）目标函数。

Result: 理论上提供了严谨的统计保证（包括得分逼近、估计及分布估计结果）；实验上在合成数据（ARMA、高斯过程）及大规模金融投资组合优化任务中均优于基线模型。

Conclusion: 提出了一种适用于适应性时间序列生成的序列化前向-后向扩散框架，该框架能够有效捕捉时间依赖性，在理论上具备统计保证，并在金融投资组合优化等实际应用中展现了优越性能。

Abstract: Generating realistic synthetic sequential data is critical in real-world applications across operations research, finance, healthcare, energy systems, and scientific computing, where time-indexed observations are used for prediction, simulation, risk assessment, and data-driven decision-making. While diffusion models have achieved remarkable success in generating static data, their direct extensions to sequential settings often fail to capture temporal dependence and information structure. Designing diffusion models that can simulate sequential data in an adapted manner, and hence without anticipation of future information, therefore remains an open challenge.
  In this work, we propose a sequential forward-backward diffusion framework for adapted time series generation. Our approach progressively injects and removes noise along the sequence, conditioning on the previously generated history to ensure adaptiveness. A novel score-matching objective is introduced for efficient parallel training. We derive rigorous statistical guarantees under a generic framework, then establish score approximation, score estimation, and distribution estimation results with ReLU networks serving as a concrete instance. Empirically, we validate our method on synthetic data, including ARMA models and Gaussian processes, and demonstrate its effectiveness in constructing mean-variance optimal portfolios.

</details>


### [289] [OPRD: On-Policy Representation Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06021)
*Shenzhi Yang,Guangcheng Zhu,Bowen Song,Haobo Wang,Mingxuan Xia,Xing Zheng,Yingfan Ma,Zhongqi Chen,Weiqiang Wang,Gang Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了OPRD（策略内表征蒸馏），通过在中间隐层而非输出概率空间进行蒸馏，有效解决了采样方差与信息浪费问题，实现了更优的模型性能与计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统策略内蒸馏（OPD）仅在输出空间监督，面临两个局限：1. 大词表导致蒙特卡洛KL散度估计存在采样方差；2. 将教师视为“黑盒”，忽略了极其重要的中间层隐状态信息。

Method: 提出“策略内表征蒸馏（OPRD）”，将蒸馏任务从输出空间（概率分布）提升至隐空间（Hidden States），在相同的采样路径（rollouts）上进行层级对齐，完全跳过语言模型头（LM head）。

Result: 在AIME 2024/2025和AIMO基准测试中，OPRD成功缩小了学生模型与教师模型之间的性能差距（而传统OPD效果停滞）；在效率方面，训练速度提升至1.44倍，显存需求降低了54%。

Conclusion: OPRD通过在隐空间进行对齐，克服了传统策略内蒸馏（OPD）在输出空间存在的采样方差和信息丢失问题，在复杂推理任务中显著提升了学生模型性能，并实现了更高的训练效率和更低的显存占用。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) supervises the student only in output space by matching next-token probabilities. This output-only paradigm has two limits: (1) sampling variance from Monte Carlo KL estimates over large vocabularies (e.g., Qwen's ~150k tokens) persists throughout training, and (2) it treats the teacher as a black-box, discarding all intermediate hidden states after the LM head. We propose On-Policy Representation Distillation (OPRD), which lifts distillation into hidden-state space by aligning student and teacher representations across selected layers on the same rollouts, bypassing the LM head entirely. Theoretically, OPRD eliminates sampling variance and provides richer per-layer structural information. Empirically, OPRD closes the student-teacher gap on AIME 2024/2025 and AIMO, while output-space OPD baselines plateau below the teacher. OPRD also trains 1.44x faster and uses 54% less memory than top-k OPD. Code: https://github.com/ShenzhiYang2000/OPRD.

</details>


### [290] [Catastrophic Forgetting as Accessibility Collapse: A Three-Level Framework for Knowledge Persistence in Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06032)
*Ayushman Trivedi,Bhavika Melwani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 灾难性遗忘并非模型“忘掉了”知识，而是“找不到”知识。通过实验证实即便模型表现出明显的遗忘，大部分任务信息仍客观存在于神经网络的中间层表征中，仅通过简单重置分类器头即可恢复大部分性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往将灾难性遗忘视为旧知识的“不可逆擦除”。本文提出不同假设：遗忘可能是由于失去了对已保存信息访问路径（可访问性）导致的，而非表征的物理销毁。

Method: 构建了“知识存储、表征、可访问性”三层框架；在CIFAR-100序列学习任务上使用ResNet-18进行实验；结合检查点持久性、线性探测（Linear Probing）、表征几何分析、分类器重置（Classifier-reset）和层级恢复性分析等方法。

Result: 实验显示，尽管行为指标上旧任务准确率降至0%，但线性探测仍能保留约76%的信息；重置分类器可恢复75.7%的任务性能；层级分析表明早、中层保留了高度可恢复的信息；几何分析显示知识是以分布式高维方式存在，而非局部子空间。

Conclusion: 灾难性遗忘的本质并非知识的擦除，而是提取权重的丢失。神经网络深层中仍保留了大量可恢复的知识，只需通过重置分类器或线性探测即可找回大部分性能。

Abstract: Catastrophic forgetting is commonly interpreted as the irreversible erasure of previously acquired knowledge during sequential learning. In this work, we investigate an alternative perspective: that forgetting may arise not from complete destruction of task representations but from a loss of accessibility to preserved information. We introduce a three-level framework separating knowledge storage, representation, and accessibility, and evaluate each component through a series of continual-learning experiments on sequential CIFAR-100 classification using ResNet-18. Our analysis combines checkpoint persistence, linear probing, representation geometry, classifier-reset recovery, and layer-wise recoverability experiments. We observe complete behavioral forgetting of earlier tasks, with task accuracy collapsing from 54.8% to 0%, while linear probe performance retains approximately 76% of the original representational information. Furthermore, retraining only the final classifier restores 75.7% of the original task performance without modifying the backbone network. Layer-wise analysis reveals that early and intermediate layers preserve highly recoverable task information despite severe degradation at later stages. Projection-energy and principal-angle analyses indicate that retained knowledge persists as distributed high-dimensional representations rather than through preservation of a small dominant subspace. These findings suggest that catastrophic forgetting is better characterized as an accessibility failure than complete representational erasure, and that substantial task-relevant information remains embedded within neural representations even after functional forgetting has occurred.

</details>


### [291] [When Good Enough Is Optimal: Multiplication-Only Matrix Inversion Approximation for Quantized Gated DeltaNet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06034)
*Luoming Zhang,Yuwei Ren,Kui Zhang,Tian Liu,Lingjuan Ge,Denghao Li,Matthew Harper Langston,Yin Huang,Weiliang Will Zeng,Liang Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对线性注意力机制中的矩阵求逆瓶颈，本文提出了一种基于 MatMul 的并行化算法，利用 Neumann 级数展开消除算力依赖，显著提升了 NPU 上的推理速度并降低了解码开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在块状（chunk-wise）并行线性注意力机制中，矩阵求逆是长文本建模的瓶颈，尤其是基于前向替换的方法在 NPU 上并行度低、硬件利用率差。

Method: 提出了一种基于矩阵乘法（MatMul）的算法，通过截断 Neumann 级数（Truncated Neumann expansion）、结构化掩码（Structural masking）及并行残差校正（Parallel residual correction）消除序列依赖；并针对低比特 INT 量化场景优化了动态范围扩展问题，同时根据块大小动态调整近似阶数和残差步长。

Result: 在 Qwen3.5 系列模型上实现了最高 5 倍的内核级加速，解码层开销降低 20%，同时在浮点和低精度推理下均保持了原始模型精度。

Conclusion: 该方法通过截断 Neumann 级数和并行残差校正，成功将线性注意力机制中序列依赖的矩阵求逆转化为高效的 MatMul 操作，实现了硬件友好的长文本推理加速，且在低精度下依然保持了良好的模型精度。

Abstract: Matrix inversion in chunk-wise parallel linear attention is a major bottleneck for long-context modeling, particularly on NPUs, where forward-substitution-based methods exhibit limited parallelism and poor hardware utilization. We propose a fast, Matrix Multiplication (MatMul)-based algorithm tailored for strictly lower-triangular matrices arising in chunk-wise linear attention. Motivated by the rapid growth of Neumann-series terms and the diagonal concentration of the inverse matrix, we employ a truncated Neumann expansion with structural masking and parallel residual correction to eliminate sequential dependencies. We further extend our method to low-bits INT by mitigating the dynamic range expansion arising from repeated matrix power operations, and adapt the approximation order and residual step to the chunk size to minimize computational cost while preserving the model's accuracy. Experiments on Qwen3.5-family models demonstrate up to 5$\times$ kernel-level speedup and a 20% reduction in decode-layer overhead, while preserving accuracy under both floating-point and low-precision inference. Our method offers an efficient and hardware-friendly solution for scalable linear attention.

</details>


### [292] [Online KL-Regularized Reinforcement Learning with Function Approximation under Misspecification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06053)
*Haoyang Hong,Zichen Wang,Quanquan Gu,Huazheng Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了存在模型失配情形下的KL正则化强化学习与上下文赌博机问题，提出了有效的算法框架，并在一般函数逼近下给出了包含模型偏差项的理论悔值界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的强化学习理论保证通常依赖于“可实现性假设”（realizability），但在模型存在偏差（misspecified models）的现实场景下，这些经典悔值界（regret bounds）可能失效。因此需要开发在模型失配下依然有效的理论框架。

Method: 提出了基于KL散度的函数逼近失配（misspecification）建模方法，并分析了采用Gibbs策略更新的回归算法（regression-based algorithms）。

Result: 建立了适用于KL正则化上下文赌博机和情节强化学习的高概率后悔（regret）界限，并显式包含了由模型失配引起的误差项。

Conclusion: 该研究通过引入KL散度失配刻画，成功为一般函数逼近环境下的KL正则化上下文赌博机和情节强化学习建立了理论保证。当失配项趋于零时，这些界限可自动退化为标准可实现情形下的结果。

Abstract: We study KL-regularized contextual bandits and episodic reinforcement learning (RL) under general function approximation with model misspecification. Existing guarantees rely on realizability and therefore do not extend to misspecified models, where classical regret bounds may fail. This work introduces KL misspecification formulations for contextual bandits and episodic RL and analyzes regression-based algorithms with Gibbs policy updates. High-probability KL-regret guarantees with explicit misspecification terms are established, recovering the standard realizable KL-regularized setting as a special case.

</details>


### [293] [MDP-GRPO: Stabilized Group Relative Policy Optimization for Multi-Constraint Instruction Following](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06058)
*Mohammad Mahdi Salmani-Zarchi,Zahra Rahimi,Heshaam Faili,Mohammad Javad Dousti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对 GRPO 在处理离散低方差奖励时的训练不稳定性，本文提出 MDP-GRPO 算法，通过多温度采样、双锚定优势函数、前景理论塑形及非对称 KL 正则化，显著提升了多约束指令遵循任务的性能与训练稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准 GRPO 在处理具有离散、低离散度（low-dispersion）且约束严苛的奖励时极不稳定，这归因于 z-score 分组归一化带来的低方差放大、均值中心化盲点及零方差崩溃等临床病理问题。

Method: 提出了 MDP-GRPO 算法，包含四个核心优化组件：(1) 多温度采样以增加奖励离散度；(2) 双锚定优势函数以解决梯度问题及均值中心化盲点；(3) 基于前景理论的奖励塑形以限制更新尺度；(4) 非对称 KL 正则化。

Result: 在 FollowBench、IFEval 及多约束数据集上测评，MDP-GRPO 显著优于标准 GRPO，Llama-3.2-3B 的严格约束满足度提升最高达 5.0%，且在 MMLU 和 ARC 基准测试中维持了原有性能。

Conclusion: MDP-GRPO 通过多种机制有效解决了 GRPO 在低方差、离散奖励环境下的不稳定性，在提升指令遵循和约束满足能力的同时，保持了模型的通用能力。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards is ideal for multi-constraint instruction following, yet standard group-relative policy optimization (GRPO) becomes unstable under discrete, low-dispersion rewards, where within-group reward distributions are frequently homogeneous. We identify and formalize three pathologies of z-score group normalization in this regime: low-variance amplification, mean-centering blindness, and zero-variance collapse. To address them, we propose MDP-GRPO, which stabilizes learning through (1) multi-temperature sampling to increase reward dispersion, (2) dual-anchor advantages to restore gradients in homogeneous groups and stop mean-centering blindness, (3) prospect-theoretic shaping to bound updates and penalize violations based on Kahneman and Tversky's theory, and (4) asymmetric KL regularization. Evaluated on FollowBench, IFEval, and a curated multi-constraint dataset, MDP-GRPO outperforms standard GRPO, improving strict constraint satisfaction by up to 5.0% on Llama-3.2-3B. Our method also enables stable convergence with small group sizes while preserving general capabilities on MMLU and ARC.

</details>


### [294] [On Advantage Estimates for Max@K Policy Gradients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06080)
*Shota Takashiro,Soichiro Nishimori,Paavo Parmas,Yongmin Kim,Kohsei Matsutani,Gouki Minegishi,Yusuke Iwasawa,Takeshi Kojima,Yutaka Matsuo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对大模型推理时优化（max@K 等），提出 MaxPO 方法，通过 Leave-Two-Out 基准实现优势精确中心化，解决了现有策略梯度估计器方差过大及理论不一致的问题，提升了后训练效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大语言模型后训练中，传统的带有可验证奖励的强化学习面临稀疏奖励导致的探索困难。直接优化推理时目标（如 pass@K 和 max@K）是补充手段，但在现有的策略梯度估计器中，信号、基准及归一化方式存在不一致性，缺乏统一的理论框架及高效的实现方法。

Method: 1. 分析现有推理时目标（pass@K, max@K）优化中策略梯度估计器的共性与差异；2. 揭示当前方法中优势估计器虽无偏但未能中心化的问题；3. 提出 Leave-Two-Out (L2O) 基准，以实现精确的批次优势中心化；4. 形式化推导出 max@K 的规范有限批次优势，并提出 MaxPO 方法。

Result: 实证结果表明，引入 L2O 基准的 MaxPO 方法能够有效降低梯度方差，并在多个推理时目标优化指标上超过现有的非中心化方法。

Conclusion: 本文提出的 MaxPO 方法通过引入 Leave-Two-Out（L2O）基准，有效解决了推理时目标（如 max@K）优化中的非中心化优势问题。L2O 在保持策略梯度无偏性的同时实现了批次内的精确中心化，显著降低了梯度方差，并在大语言模型后训练的群组强化学习中表现优异。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards is widely used for post-training reasoning models, but sparse outcome rewards make exploration difficult. A complementary approach is to optimize inference-time objectives such as pass@K and max@K directly, yet existing policy-gradient estimators for these objectives use different signals, baselines, and normalizations, making their relationships unclear. We study this issue through baseline design and advantage centering. Starting from the advantage estimator of a leading method in the field, we show that it is policy-gradient unbiased but yields a non-centered advantage.
